Week 1 · 论文 · 全文精译（章节级）

深入 Claude Code：生产级 Agent 的设计空间

原文：Dive into Claude Code: The Design Space of Today's and Future AI Agent Systems

Jiacheng Liu, Xiaohan Zhao, Xinyi Shang, Zhiqiang Shen 2026-10 约 45 分钟

总结

  • 核心 agent loop 极其简单：调用模型 → 执行工具 → 把结果放回上下文 → 重复。
  • 真正复杂的部分是 loop 周围的系统：权限、上下文压缩、工具路由、扩展机制、subagent、会话持久化。
  • 论文提炼出五个价值：人的决策权、安全与隐私、可靠执行、能力放大、上下文适应性。
  • Claude Code 的权限系统是 deny-first，有 7 种模式，并用 ML 分类器判断 auto 模式下的风险。
  • 上下文管理是五层压缩管线；扩展机制有四类：MCP、plugins、skills、hooks。
  • Subagent 的关键不是“多一个模型”，而是隔离上下文，只把最终结果返回主会话。
  • 论文最后提出六个开放方向：可观测性、跨会话记忆、harness 边界、长时程扩展、治理、长期开发者能力。

原文逐段对照翻译

说明：论文共 54 页，本页采用章节级精译，覆盖全部 14 个章节；逐段机翻保留在下方“机器翻译参考”中，方便核对细节。

研究问题与方法

Claude Code is an agentic coding tool that can run shell commands, edit files, and call external services on behalf of the user. This study describes its comprehensive architecture by analyzing the publicly available TypeScript source code and further comparing it with two independent open-source AI agent systems, OpenClaw and Hermes Agent.

Claude Code 是一个 agentic coding 工具：它能代替用户执行 shell 命令、编辑文件、调用外部服务。这篇论文通过分析公开的 TypeScript 源码，并与 OpenClaw、Hermes Agent 两个独立开源 agent 系统对比，来描述它的完整架构。

Our analysis identifies five human values, philosophies, and needs that motivate the architecture: human decision authority, safety, security, and privacy, reliable execution, capability amplification, and contextual adaptability. We then trace them through thirteen design principles to specific implementation choices.

论文提炼出五个驱动架构的人类价值：人的决策权；安全、安保与隐私；可靠执行；能力放大；上下文适应性。然后把这些价值拆成十三条设计原则，再追踪到具体实现选择。

The core of the system is a simple while-loop that calls the model, runs tools, and repeats. Most of the code, however, lives in the systems around this loop: a permission system with seven modes and an ML-based classifier, a five-layer compaction pipeline for context management, four extensibility mechanisms (MCP, plugins, skills, and hooks), a subagent delegation and orchestration mechanism, and append-oriented session storage.

系统核心是一个简单的 while-loop：调用模型、执行工具、重复。但大部分代码都在 loop 周围：7 种模式加 ML 分类器的权限系统；五层上下文压缩管线；四类扩展机制（MCP、plugins、skills、hooks）；subagent 委派与编排；以及追加式会话存储。

五个价值

Human Decision Authority: the human retains ultimate decision authority over what the system does, organized through a principal hierarchy (Anthropic, then operators, then users). The system is designed so humans can observe actions in real time, approve or reject proposed operations, interrupt compatible in-progress operations, and audit after the fact.

人的决策权：人保留对系统行为的最终决定权，按 principal hierarchy 组织（Anthropic → operator → user）。系统让人能实时观察、批准/拒绝、打断进行中的操作，并在事后审计。

When Anthropic found that users approve 93% of permission prompts, the response was not to add more warnings but to restructure the problem: defined boundaries (sandboxing, auto-mode classifiers) within which the agent can work freely, rather than per-action approvals that users stop reviewing once habituated.

Anthropic 发现用户会批准 93% 的权限提示后，没有继续堆警告，而是重构问题：用沙箱、auto-mode 分类器划出可信边界，让 agent 在边界内自由工作，而不是依赖用户会逐渐麻木的逐动作审批。

Safety, Security, and Privacy: the system protects humans, their code, their data, and their infrastructure from harm, even when the human is inattentive or makes mistakes. The auto-mode threat model explicitly targets four risk categories: overeager behavior, honest mistakes, prompt injection, and model misalignment.

安全、安保与隐私：即使人不在场或犯错，系统也要保护人、代码、数据和基础设施。auto-mode 的威胁模型明确覆盖四类风险：过度积极、诚实错误、prompt injection、模型错位。

Reliable Execution: the agent does what the human actually meant, stays coherent over time, and supports verifying its work before declaring success. Capability Amplification: the agent enables qualitatively new workflows, not just faster completion. Contextual Adaptability: the agent adjusts context to the codebase, task, and tool returns instead of relying on one fixed prompt.

可靠执行：agent 要做人真正想做的事，跨时间保持连贯，并在宣布完成前验证结果。能力放大：agent 应支持以前做不到的新工作流，而不只是更快地补全。上下文适应性：agent 要根据代码库、任务和工具返回动态调整上下文，而不是依赖一个固定 prompt。

核心循环与 Harness

The core loop is: assemble context (system prompt, history, tool definitions, CLAUDE.md); call the model; the model returns tool calls; the permission system checks; tools execute; results are written back into context; repeat until the model gives a final answer.

核心循环是：组装上下文（system prompt、历史、工具定义、CLAUDE.md）→ 调用模型 → 模型返回工具调用 → 权限系统检查 → 执行工具 → 结果写回上下文 → 重复，直到模型给出最终回答。

The architectural key is that the loop itself is thin, and the complexity is around it. That is why building a coding agent is easy, but building a reliable coding agent is hard.

架构上的关键是：loop 本身很薄，复杂度都在它周围。这也是为什么“做一个 coding agent”很容易，“做一个可靠的 coding agent”很难。

权限系统

Claude Code's permission system is not a simple allow/deny dialog. It uses deny-first rule evaluation, seven permission modes, an ML classifier for auto mode, hook interception, and permission dialogs for high-risk actions. The goal is not to ask for consent on every step, but to concentrate human attention on genuinely high-risk actions.

Claude Code 的权限系统不是简单的允许/拒绝弹窗，而是 deny-first 规则评估、7 种权限模式、auto 模式的 ML 分类器、hook 拦截，以及高风险动作的权限对话框。目标不是每一步都让人点同意，而是把人的注意力集中到真正高风险的动作上。

上下文与压缩

Context is a scarce resource. Claude Code's compaction is not a single "summarize" step but five layers: budget control, snipping clearly unneeded fragments, micro-compaction, collapsing large history blocks, and automatic summarization. The cost of compaction is not only tokens but information loss: the model may forget files it read, decisions it made, or exact strings.

上下文是稀缺资源。Claude Code 的压缩不是单一“总结”，而是五层：预算控制、裁掉明显不需要的片段、微压缩、折叠大块历史、自动摘要。压缩的代价不只是 token，还有信息损失：模型可能忘记读过的文件、做过的决定、精确字符串。

扩展机制

Claude Code partitions extensibility into four mechanisms: MCP for connecting external services and data; plugins for packaging a set of capabilities; skills for teaching the agent a procedure, with only name and description resident and the body loaded on demand; and hooks for deterministic execution at lifecycle events.

Claude Code 把扩展分成四类：MCP 连接外部服务和数据；plugins 打包一组能力；skills 教 agent 一套流程，只常驻名称和描述，正文按需加载；hooks 在生命周期事件上确定性执行。

The key observation is that extension mechanisms are not "the more the better"; each mechanism needs a clear responsibility boundary. If everything goes into the system prompt, context explodes; if everything becomes a tool, the model's selection burden explodes.

关键观察是：扩展机制不是越多越好，每种机制都要有清晰职责边界。所有东西都塞进 system prompt，上下文会爆炸；所有东西都做成工具，模型的选择负担会爆炸。

Subagent 与会话持久化

The architectural value of subagents is not just "doing more in parallel" but isolating context: each subtask runs in its own context window, intermediate results do not pollute the main session, and only the final message returns. This is an extension of the principle that context is a scarce resource.

subagent 的架构价值不只是“并行做更多”，而是隔离上下文：子任务在自己的窗口运行，中间结果不污染主会话，只返回最终消息。这是“上下文是稀缺资源”原则的延伸。

Session storage is primarily append-only JSONL: each record is an independent JSON object, simple to write, recoverable after crashes, and easy to audit. Session-scoped permissions are not persisted into the transcript; this is a deliberate safety choice, because resuming a session should not automatically restore sensitive permissions.

会话存储以追加式 JSONL 为主：每条记录是独立 JSON 对象，写入简单、崩溃后可恢复、便于审计。会话级权限不会持久化到 transcript，这是有意的安全选择：恢复会话不应自动恢复敏感权限。

与 OpenClaw、Hermes 的对比

The three systems answer the same design questions but under different deployment contexts. Claude Code is a single CLI loop emphasizing per-action safety around a local developer's codebase. OpenClaw is a multi-channel personal assistant gateway emphasizing perimeter-level access control, with the runtime embedded in the gateway control plane. Hermes Agent is a single-process, multi-surface assistant rendering per-action approvals across many interfaces.

三个系统回答同一组设计问题，但部署场景不同：Claude Code 是单 CLI loop，强调本地开发者代码库的 per-action 安全；OpenClaw 是多通道个人助理网关，强调 perimeter 级访问控制，把 runtime 嵌在网关控制面；Hermes Agent 是单进程、多 surface 助理，把 per-action 审批渲染到多个界面。

The conclusion is not "who is better" but that deployment context shapes architectural answers. The same permission question gets different implementations in a CLI, an enterprise gateway, and a multi-surface assistant.

结论不是“谁更好”，而是：部署场景会塑造架构答案。同一个“权限”问题，在 CLI、企业网关、多端助理里会得到不同实现。

六个开放方向

The paper identifies six open directions: the observability-evaluation gap (how to surface silent agent mistakes); cross-session persistence (the missing layer between static instructions and a single transcript); harness boundary evolution (where, when, what, and with whom the agent acts); horizon scaling (from a session to a multi-week program); governance and oversight; and long-term developer capability.

论文提出六个开放方向：可观测性与评估缺口（如何发现 agent 的静默错误）；跨会话持久化（静态指令与单次 transcript 之间缺失的一层）；harness 边界演化（在哪里、何时、对什么、和谁行动）；长时程扩展（从单会话到数周项目）；治理与监督；长期开发者能力。

The last question is especially important: while the Claude Code agent system amplifies the short-term capabilities of programmers, it offers limited mechanisms that explicitly support long-term human improvement, deeper understanding, and sustained codebase coherence.

最后一个问题尤其重要：Claude Code 放大了程序员的短期能力，但对长期的人类成长、更深的理解、代码库持续一致性，提供的显式机制很有限。

十三条设计原则

The five values are operationalized through thirteen design principles, each answering a recurring question. Key principles include: deny-first with human escalation; a graduated trust spectrum; defense in depth with layered mechanisms; externalized programmable policy; context as a scarce resource with progressive management; append-only durable state; minimal scaffolding with maximal operational harness; values over rules; composable multi-mechanism extensibility; reversibility-weighted risk assessment; transparent file-based configuration and memory; isolated subagent boundaries; and graceful recovery and resilience.

五个价值被拆成十三条设计原则，每条回答一个反复出现的问题。核心原则包括：deny-first + 人工升级；渐进式信任光谱；分层纵深防御；可外部化的可编程策略；把上下文当稀缺资源并渐进管理；追加式持久状态；最小决策脚手架 + 最大操作 harness；价值高于规则；可组合的多机制扩展；按可逆性加权风险评估；透明的文件式配置与记忆；隔离的 subagent 边界；优雅恢复与韧性。

These principles can be read against three alternative design families: rule-based orchestration frameworks that encode decision logic as explicit state graphs; container-isolated agents that treat sandboxing as the primary safety mechanism; and minimal-harness agents that rely almost entirely on model capability. Claude Code's principle set is distinctive in combining minimal decision scaffolding with layered policy enforcement.

这些原则可以和三类替代设计对照：把决策逻辑编码成显式状态图的规则编排框架；把容器隔离当作主要安全机制的 agent；几乎完全依赖模型能力的最小 harness。Claude Code 的独特之处，是把“最小决策脚手架”和“分层策略执行”组合在一起。

架构总览：七个组件

The high-level architecture has seven components: the user; the agent loop; interfaces; the permission system; tools; state and persistence; and the execution environment. Runtime turns flow through context assembly, model call, tool request, permission check, tool execution, result synchronization, and either another iteration or a final response.

高层架构有七个组件：用户；agent loop；接口；权限系统；工具；状态与持久化；执行环境。一次运行时 turn 会经过：上下文组装 → 模型调用 → 工具请求 → 权限检查 → 工具执行 → 结果同步 → 继续迭代或给出最终回答。

The package is organized around a src/ directory. query.ts contains the core agent loop and five context shapers; QueryEngine.ts wraps the SDK/headless conversation; Tool.ts defines the tool interface; compact.ts is the compaction engine; AgentTool.tsx dispatches subagents; runAgent.ts coordinates agent lifecycle. The tools/ directory contains roughly 42 tool implementations and commands/ roughly 86 slash commands.

代码包围绕 `src/` 组织：`query.ts` 是核心 agent loop 和五个上下文 shaper；`QueryEngine.ts` 封装 SDK/headless 会话；`Tool.ts` 定义工具接口；`compact.ts` 是压缩引擎；`AgentTool.tsx` 负责 subagent 派发；`runAgent.ts` 协调 agent 生命周期。`tools/` 约 42 个工具实现，`commands/` 约 86 个 slash command。

工具与扩展面

Tool availability is conditional. getAllBaseTools() constructs different tool sets depending on mode, build, environment, and feature flags. The model may see as few as three tools in simple mode (Bash, Read, Edit) or up to 54 tools (19 unconditional plus 35 conditional) in a full internal build with all features enabled.

工具可用性是条件化的。`getAllBaseTools()` 会根据模式、构建、环境和 feature flag 构造不同工具集。简单模式可能只有 3 个工具（Bash、Read、Edit）；完整内部构建最多 54 个（19 个无条件 + 35 个条件工具）。

Always included tools include AgentTool, BashTool, FileReadTool, FileEditTool, FileWriteTool, SkillTool, WebFetchTool, and WebSearchTool. Environment-specific tools include Glob/Grep, ConfigTool, and PowerShellTool on Windows. Feature-flagged tools include task tools, worktree, team/swarm tools, and tool search.

始终包含的工具包括 AgentTool、BashTool、FileReadTool、FileEditTool、FileWriteTool、SkillTool、WebFetchTool、WebSearchTool。环境相关工具包括 Glob/Grep、ConfigTool、Windows 上的 PowerShellTool。feature flag 工具包括 task、worktree、team/swarm、tool search。

上下文管理：五层压缩与 CLAUDE.md

Context management is a five-layer compaction pipeline (budget, snip, micro, collapse, auto), plus a four-level CLAUDE.md hierarchy, auto memory in memdir/, and context assembly through getSystemContext() and getUserContext(). The design treats context as a scarce resource and tries to preserve the most decision-relevant material.

上下文管理是五层压缩管线（budget、snip、micro、collapse、auto），加上四级 CLAUDE.md 层级、`memdir/` 自动记忆，以及通过 `getSystemContext()` / `getUserContext()` 做上下文组装。设计把上下文当稀缺资源，尽量保留与决策最相关的材料。

Compaction is lossy. The system has to trade token cost against information loss: a longer raw window preserves exact strings and decisions but costs more per step; a shorter summary saves tokens but can make the agent forget files, constraints, and earlier reasoning.

压缩是有损的。系统必须在 token 成本与信息损失之间取舍：更长的原始窗口保留精确字符串和决定，但每一步更贵；更短的摘要省 token，但可能让 agent 忘记文件、约束和之前的推理。

会话持久化

Session persistence is append-oriented. Each session is stored as JSONL transcripts, with sidechains and file-history. Append-only storage is simple to write, recoverable after crashes, and auditable. Session-scoped permissions are deliberately not persisted, so resuming a session does not silently restore sensitive authority.

会话持久化以追加为主：每个会话存成 JSONL transcript，带 sidechain 和 file-history。追加式存储写入简单、崩溃后可恢复、便于审计。会话级权限有意不持久化，恢复会话时不会悄悄恢复敏感权限。

相关工作与讨论

The paper positions Claude Code against prior agent architectures, software-engineering agents, and work on context, memory, and tool use. It reads the architecture as a coherent design point and surfaces tensions: model capability versus harness investment, local autonomy versus global coherence, context preservation versus token cost, and short-term productivity versus long-term developer capability.

论文把 Claude Code 放在既有 agent 架构、软件工程 agent、上下文/记忆/工具使用研究的背景下，把它的架构读成一个自洽的设计点，并揭示几组张力：模型能力 vs harness 投资；局部自主 vs 全局一致；保留上下文 vs token 成本；短期生产力 vs 长期开发者能力。

The discussion also notes that Claude Code gives the model broad local autonomy while surrounding it with a dense deterministic harness. Good local decisions can still produce poor global outcomes when bounded context prevents global awareness.

讨论还指出：Claude Code 给模型广泛的局部自主权，同时用一层密集、确定性的 harness 包围它。即使局部决策很好，只要上下文边界阻止了全局视野，也可能产生糟糕的全局结果。

结论

Production coding agents can be understood as answers to a recurring set of design questions: where reasoning sits relative to the harness, how execution, safety, extensibility, context, delegation, and persistence are organized, and which trade-offs those choices encode. Claude Code occupies a clear design point: it gives the model broad local autonomy while surrounding it with a dense deterministic harness.

生产级 coding agent 可以理解为对一组反复出现的设计问题的回答：推理相对 harness 放在哪里；执行、安全、扩展、上下文、委派、持久化如何组织；这些选择编码了哪些取舍。Claude Code 占据了一个清晰的设计点：给模型广泛的局部自主权，同时用一层密集、确定性的 harness 包围它。

机器翻译参考（未精修）

展开全文逐段机翻（857 段） > **翻译说明：**以下为原文逐段直译，保留原文段落顺序与信息；术语做了统一，机器辅助翻译后经过常见误译校正。总结、学习者视角与拓展已单独列出。

Dive into Claude Code: The Design Space of Today’s

深入了解 Claude Code：当今的设计空间

and Future AI Agent Systems Jiacheng Liu1,∗, Xiaohan Zhao1,∗, Xinyi Shang1,2, Zhiqiang Shen1,† 1VILA Lab, Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence, 2University College London ∗These authors contributed equally to this work, †Corresponding author Claude Code is an agentic coding tool that can run shell commands, edit files, and call external services on behalf of the user. This study describes its comprehensive architecture by analyzing the publicly available TypeScript source codea and further comparing it with two independent open-source AI agent systems, OpenClaw and Hermes Agent, that answer many similar or even the same design questions from different deployment contexts. Our analysis identifies five human values, philosophies, and needs that motivate the architecture: human decision authority, safety, security, and privacy, reliable execution, capability amplification, and contextual adaptability. We then trace them through thirteen design principles to specific implementation choices. The core of the system is a simple while-loop that calls the model, runs tools, and repeats. Most of the code, however, lives in the systems around this loop: a permission system with seven modes and an ML-based classifier, a five-layer compaction pipeline for context management, four extensibility mechanisms (MCP, plugins, skills, and hooks), a subagent delegation and orchestration mechanism, and append-oriented session storage.

和未来人工智能代理系统 Jia Cheng Liu1,*、Xiaohan Zhao1,*、Xinyi Shang1,2、Zhiqiang Shen1,† 1VILA 实验室、穆罕默德·本·扎耶德人工智能大学、2 伦敦大学学院 *这些作者对这项工作做出了同等贡献，†通讯作者 Claude Code 是一种代理编码工具，可以代表用户运行 shell 命令、编辑文件和调用外部服务。本研究通过分析公开的 TypeScript 源代码来描述其综合架构，并进一步将其与两个独立的开源 AI agent系统 OpenClaw 和 Hermes Agent 进行比较，这两个系统回答了来自不同部署环境的许多相似甚至相同的设计问题。我们的分析确定了推动架构的五种人类价值观、哲学和需求：人类决策权、安全性和隐私、可靠执行、能力放大和上下文适应性。然后，我们通过十三个设计原则追踪它们，直至具体的实现选择。该系统的核心是一个简单的 while 循环，用于调用模型、运行工具并重复。然而，大部分代码都存在于围绕此循环的系统中：具有七种模式和基于 ML 的分类器的权限系统、用于上下文管理的五层压缩管道、四种可扩展性机制（MCP、插件、技能和挂钩）、subagent委托和编排机制以及面向附加的会话存储。

Comparisons with OpenClaw (a multi-channel personal assistant gateway) and Hermes Agent (a single-process, multi-surface assistant) show that the same design questions produce different answers across three deployment contexts. Claude Code emphasizes per-action safety, OpenClaw emphasizes perimeter-level access, and Hermes renders per-action approvals across many surfaces. At the runtime layer, Claude Code uses a single CLI loop, OpenClaw embeds the runtime within a gateway control plane, and Hermes uses one process whose role is set by its entry point. At the context and extension layer, Claude Code extends the context window, OpenClaw registers gateway-wide capabilities, and Hermes provides pluggable memory and model backends. We finally identify six open design directions for future agent systems, grounded in recent empirical, architectural, and policy literature. Our GitHub is available at: https://github.com/VILA-Lab/Dive-into-Claude-Code.

与 OpenClaw（多通道个人助理网关）和 Hermes Agent（单进程、多表面助理）的比较表明，相同的设计问题在三种部署环境中会产生不同的答案。 Claude Code 强调每次操作的安全性，OpenClaw 强调外围级别的访问，而 Hermes 则在许多表面上提供每次操作的批准。在运行时层，Claude Code 使用单个 CLI 循环，OpenClaw 将运行时嵌入网关控制平面内，而 Hermes 使用一个进程，其角色由其入口点设置。在上下文和扩展层，Claude Code扩展了上下文窗口，OpenClaw注册了网关范围的功能，Hermes提供了可插拔的内存和模型后端。我们最终根据最近的经验、架构和政策文献，确定了未来代理系统的六个开放设计方向。我们的 GitHub 位于：https://github.com/VILA-Lab/Dive-into-Claude-Code。

Correspondence: Zhiqiang Shen (Zhiqiang.Shen@mbzuai.ac.ae)

通讯作者：沉志强（Zhiqiang.Shen@mbzuai.ac.ae）

1

Introduction

简介

AI-assisted software development has evolved from autocomplete-style tools such as GitHub Copilot (Chen et al., 2021), through IDE-integrated assistants like Cursor (Cursor, 2026), to fully agentic systems that autonomously plan multi-step modifications, execute shell commands, read and write files, and iterate on their own outputs. Claude Code (Anthropic, 2026a) is an agentic coding tool released by Anthropic (Anthropic, 2026g).

人工智能辅助软件开发已经从 GitHub Copilot（Chen 等人，2021）等自动完成式工具，通过 Cursor（Cursor，2026）等 IDE 集成助手，发展到自主规划多步骤修改、执行 shell 命令、读写文件以及迭代自己的输出的完全代理系统。 Claude Code (Anthropic, 2026a) 是 Anthropic (Anthropic, 2026g) 发布的代理编码工具。

Its official documentation describes an “agentic loop” that plans and executes actions toward accomplishing a goal and can call tools, evaluate results, and continue until the task is done 1. This shift

其官方文档描述了一个“代理循环”，该循环计划和执行行动以实现目标，并且可以调用工具、评估结果并继续直到任务完成 1。这种转变

from suggestion to autonomous action introduces architectural requirements that have no counterpart in

completion-based tools. These requirements define a design space, a set of recurring questions spanning topics such as safety, context management, extensibility, and delegation that every coding agent must navigate. This study uses source-level analysis of Claude Code to show how one production system answers these questions.

基于完成的工具。这些需求定义了一个设计空间，一组重复出现的问题，涵盖每个coding agent必须导航的安全性、上下文管理、可扩展性和委派等主题。这项研究使用Claude代码的源代码级分析来展示一个生产系统如何回答这些问题。

Despite growing adoption, Anthropic publishes user-facing documentation for Claude Code but not detailed 1https://code.claude.com/docs/en/how-claude-code-works.

尽管采用率不断提高，Anthropic 发布了面向用户的 Claude Code 文档，但没有详细说明 1https://code.claude.com/docs/en/how-claude-code-works。

1

1

arXiv:2604.14228v3 [cs.SE] 10 Oct 2026 architectural descriptions. This study uses source code analysis to describe architectural design decisions.

arXiv:2604.14228v3 [cs.SE] 2026 年 10 月 10 日架构描述。本研究使用源代码分析来描述架构设计决策。

Anthropic’s internal survey of 132 engineers and researchers (Huang et al., 2025) reports that about 27% of Claude Code-assisted tasks were work that would not have been attempted without the tool, suggesting that the architecture enables qualitatively new workflows rather than merely accelerating existing ones.

Anthropic 对 132 名工程师和研究人员进行的内部调查（Huang 等人，2025）报告称，大约 27% 的 Claude Code 辅助任务是如果没有该工具就不会尝试的工作，这表明该架构能够实现高质量的新工作流程，而不仅仅是加速现有的工作流程。

In this work, we first identify five human values/philosophies and thirteen design principles that motivate the architecture (Section 2), then organize the analysis in three parts:

在这项工作中，我们首先确定了推动架构的五种人类价值观/哲学和十三种设计原则（第 2 节），然后将分析分为三个部分：

1. Design-space analysis. We identify recurring design questions (where reasoning lives, how the iteration loop is structured, what safety posture to adopt, how the extension surface is partitioned, how context is managed, how work is delegated across subagents, and how sessions persist) and analyze Claude Code’s answers through a 7-component high-level structure and a 5-layer subsystem architecture, tracing each choice to specific source files (Section 3). The analysis aims to build a deep understanding of the system mechanism, with the goal of informing the design of better and more powerful agent systems.

1.设计空间分析。我们识别重复出现的设计问题（推理在哪里、迭代循环如何构建、采用什么安全态势、扩展表面如何分区、上下文如何管理、如何跨subagent委派工作以及会话如何持续），并通过 7 个组件的高级结构和 5 层子系统架构分析 Claude Code 的答案，将每个选择跟踪到特定的源文件（第 3 节）。该分析旨在加深对系统机制的理解，为更好、更强大的代理系统的设计提供信息。

2. Architectural contrast with OpenClaw and Hermes Agent. Beyond analyzing Claude Code itself, we also compare its design philosophy with that of two open-source agent systems, OpenClaw (Steinberger and OpenClaw Contributors, 2026) (a multi-channel personal assistant gateway) and Hermes Agent (Nous Research, 2026) (a single-process, multi-surface assistant), across six design dimensions to show how the same recurring questions produce different answers under different deployment contexts (Section 10), in order to highlight both the common principles and the key differences between commercial and open-source software. This comparison helps reveal how deployment setting, product goals, safety requirements, and user assumptions shape architectural choices in different ways. By examining where these systems converge and where they diverge, our study aims to provide useful guidance and practical insights for the design of future, more capable agent systems.

2.与OpenClaw和Hermes Agent的架构对比。除了分析 Claude Code 本身之外，我们还将其设计理念与两个开源代理系统 OpenClaw（Steinberger 和 OpenClaw Contributors，2026）（多通道个人助理网关）和 Hermes Agent（Nous Research，2026）（单进程、多表面助理）的设计理念进行了六个设计维度的比较，以展示相同的重复问题如何在不同的部署环境下产生不同的答案（第 10 节），以突出商业软件和开源软件之间的共同原则和主要区别。这种比较有助于揭示部署设置、产品目标、安全要求和用户假设如何以不同的方式影响架构选择。通过研究这些系统的汇聚点和分歧点，我们的研究旨在为未来更强大的代理系统的设计提供有用的指导和实用的见解。

3. Open directions for future agent systems. Building on the design-space analysis and the OpenClaw and Hermes contrasts, Section 13 identifies six open directions spanning the observability-evaluation gap, cross-session persistence, harness boundary evolution, horizon scaling, governance, and long-term developer capability, each drawing on empirical, architectural, and policy literature. The long-term capability question also reveals an open concern: while the Claude Code agent system amplifies the short-term capabilities of programmers and end users, it offers limited mechanisms that explicitly support long-term human improvement, deeper understanding, and sustained codebase coherence.

3.为未来代理系统开放方向。基于设计空间分析以及 OpenClaw 和 Hermes 对比，第 13 节确定了六个开放方向，涵盖可观察性评估差距、跨会话持久性、harness边界演变、水平扩展、治理和长期开发人员能力，每个方向都借鉴了经验、架构和政策文献。长期能力问题还揭示了一个公开的担忧：虽然Claude代码代理系统增强了程序员和最终用户的短期能力，但它提供了有限的机制，明确支持长期的人类改进、更深入的理解和持续的代码库一致性。

The core agent loop is a while-true cycle with state management. The surrounding subsystems for safety, extensibility, context management, delegation, and persistence make up the bulk of the implementation.

核心代理循环是一个带有状态管理的 while-true 循环。安全性、可扩展性、上下文管理、委派和持久性的周围子系统构成了实现的大部分。

Source-level analysis2 allows us to identify design choices, subsystem boundaries, and implementation trade-offs directly from the system itself rather than inferring them solely from product descriptions.

源代码级分析2使我们能够直接从系统本身识别设计选择、子系统边界和实现权衡，而不是仅从产品描述中推断它们。

Running example.

运行示例。

To keep the architecture concrete, we trace the task “Fix the failing test in auth.test.ts” through Sections 3 to 9. This example illustrates how a seemingly simple user request activates multiple architectural layers, including tool invocation, permission checks, context selection, iterative repair, delegation, and session persistence.

为了保持架构具体，我们通过第 3 节到第 9 节跟踪任务“修复 auth.test.ts 中的失败测试”。这个示例说明了看似简单的用户请求如何激活多个架构层，包括工具调用、权限检查、上下文选择、迭代修复、委托和会话持久性。

Paper organization.

论文组织。

Section 2 identifies the human values and design principles that motivate the architecture.

第 2 部分确定了推动架构的人类价值观和设计原则。

Section 3 introduces the high-level architecture and the design questions it answers. Sections 4 to 9 each analyze a major subsystem’s design choices. Section 10 contrasts the analysis with OpenClaw and Hermes Agent, Section 11 positions the work against prior agent and software-engineering literature, Section 12 provides discussion, and Section 13 surveys open questions for future agent systems. Section 14 concludes. The appendix describes the evidence base and methodology, package-structure notes, community reimplementations, and a companion map from new agent-system signals to the paper’s design-space questions.

第 3 节介绍了高层架构及其回答的设计问题。第 4 节到第 9 节分别分析了主要子系统的设计选择。第 10 节将分析与 OpenClaw 和 Hermes Agent 进行对比，第 11 节根据先前的代理和软件工程文献定位工作，第 12 节提供讨论，第 13 节调查未来代理系统的开放问题。第 14 节总结。附录描述了证据基础和方法、包结构注释、社区重新实现以及从新代理系统信号到论文设计空间问题的配套图。

2Our analysis is grounded primarily in the source code, supplemented by official Anthropic documentation and selected community analysis. The appendix details the evidence base and methodology.

2我们的分析主要基于源代码，辅以官方 Anthropic 文档和选定的社区分析。附录详细介绍了证据基础和方法。

2

2

Design Philosophies, Design Principles and Architectural Motivations

设计理念、设计原则和建筑动机

Production coding agents are built by humans, for humans, and the architectural decisions they embed reflect what their creators believe matters. This section identifies the human values that motivate Claude Code’s design, traces them through recurring design principles, and frames the design-space questions that organize the analysis in Sections 3 to 9.

生产coding agent是由人类构建的，为人类而构建，它们嵌入的架构决策反映了其创建者认为重要的内容。本节确定了激发 Claude Code 设计的人类价值观，通过重复出现的设计原则追踪它们，并构建了组织第 3 节至第 9 节中分析的设计空间问题。

Anthropic’s framework for safe agents states a central tension: “Agents must be able to work autonomously; their independent operation is exactly what makes them valuable. But humans should retain control over how their goals are pursued” (Anthropic, 2025a). Claude’s Constitution resolves this not through rigid decision procedures but by cultivating “good judgment and sound values that can be applied contextually” (Anthropic, 2026b).

Anthropic 的安全代理框架提出了一个核心张力：“代理必须能够自主工作；他们的独立运作正是使他们有价值的原因。但人类应该保留对其目标实现方式的控制”（Anthropic，2025a）。Claude宪法不是通过严格的决策程序来解决这个问题，而是通过培养“良好的判断力和可以根据具体情况应用的合理价值观”（Anthropic，2026b）。

These commitments, together with empirical findings about how developers actually use the tool (Huang et al., 2025; McCain et al., 2026), point to five human values that shape the architecture.

这些承诺以及有关开发人员实际如何使用该工具的实证结果（Huang 等人，2025 年；McCain 等人，2026 年）指出了塑造架构的五种人类价值观。

2.1

2.1

Five Values and Philosophies

五种价值观和哲学

Human Decision Authority.

人类决策权。

The human retains ultimate decision authority over what the system does, organized through a principal hierarchy (Anthropic, then operators, then users) that formalizes who holds authority over what (Anthropic, 2026b). The system is designed so that humans can exercise informed control: they can observe actions in real time, approve or reject proposed operations, interrupt compatible in-progress operations, and audit after the fact. When Anthropic found that users approve 93% of permission prompts (Hughes, 2026), the response was not to add more warnings but to restructure the problem: defined boundaries (sandboxing, auto-mode classifiers) within which the agent can work freely, rather than per-action approvals that users stop reviewing once habituated (Dworken and Weller-Davies, 2025).

人类保留对系统功能的最终决策权，通过主要层次结构（人择，然后操作员，然后用户）组织，正式确定谁对什么拥有权力（人择，2026b）。该系统的设计使人们能够进行知情控制：他们可以实时观察操作，批准或拒绝拟议的操作，中断兼容的正在进行的操作，并在事后进行审核。当 Anthropic 发现用户批准了 93% 的权限提示时（Hughes，2026），回应不是添加更多警告，而是重构问题：定义代理可以在其中自由工作的边界（沙箱、自动模式分类器），而不是用户一旦习惯就停止审查的每个操作批准（Dworken 和 Weller-Davies，2025）。

Safety, Security, and Privacy.

安全、安保和隐私。

The system protects humans, their code, their data, and their infrastructure

该系统保护人类、他们的代码、他们的数据和他们的基础设施

from harm, even when the human is inattentive or makes mistakes. This is distinct from Human Decision

Authority: where authority is about the human’s power to choose, safety is about the system’s obligation to protect even when that power lapses. Anthropic’s safe-agents framework separately identifies securing agent interactions and protecting privacy across extended interactions as core commitments (Anthropic, 2025a).

权威：权威是指人类的选择权，而安全则是指系统即使在这种权力失效时也有义务提供保护。 Anthropic 的安全代理框架将安全代理交互和跨扩展交互的隐私保护分别确定为核心承诺（Anthropic，2025a）。

The auto-mode threat model (Hughes, 2026) explicitly targets four risk categories: overeager behavior, honest mistakes, prompt injection, and model misalignment.

自动模式威胁模型（Hughes，2026）明确针对四种风险类别：过度热切行为、诚实错误、提示注入和模型错位。

Reliable Execution.

可靠的执行。

The agent does what the human actually meant, stays coherent over time, and supports

代理按照人类实际的意思行事，随着时间的推移保持一致，并支持

verifying its work before declaring success. This value spans both single-turn correctness (did it interpret the request faithfully?) and long-horizon dependability (does it remain coherent across context window boundaries, session resumption, and multi-agent delegation?). Anthropic’s product documentation (Anthropic, 2026h) describes a three-phase loop that the agent repeats until the task is complete: gather context, take action, and verify results. The agent design guidance (Schluntz and Zhang, 2024) further emphasizes that “ground truth from the environment” at each step assesses progress. The harness-design guidance (Rajasekaran, 2026) likewise notes that “agents tend to respond by confidently praising the work,” even when quality is mediocre, motivating separation of generation from evaluation.

在宣布成功之前验证其工作。该值涵盖单轮正确性（它是否忠实地解释了请求？）和长期可靠性（它是否在上下文窗口边界、会话恢复和多代理委托之间保持一致？）。 Anthropic 的产品文档（Anthropic，2026h）描述了代理重复直到任务完成的三阶段循环：收集上下文、采取行动并验证结果。代理设计指南（Schluntz 和Zhang，2024）进一步强调每一步的“来自环境的基本事实”都会评估进度。harness设计指南（Rajasekaran，2026）同样指出，即使质量平庸，“代理人往往会通过自信地赞扬工作来做出回应”，从而促使生成与评估分离。

Capability Amplification.

能力放大。

The system materially increases what the human can accomplish per unit of effort

该系统大大增加了人类每单位努力所能完成的任务

and cost. Anthropic’s internal survey (Huang et al., 2025), discussed in Section 1, suggests that the architecture enables qualitatively new workflows, not merely faster existing ones: approximately 27% of tasks represented work that would not otherwise have been attempted. The system is described by its creators as “a Unix utility rather than a traditional product,” built from the smallest building blocks that are “useful, understandable, and extensible” (Cherny and Wu, 2025). The architecture invests in deterministic infrastructure (context management, tool routing, recovery) rather than decision scaffolding (explicit planners or state graphs), on the premise that increasingly capable models benefit more from a rich operational environment than from frameworks that constrain their choices.

和成本。 Anthropic 的内部调查（Huang 等人，2025）（在第 1 节中讨论）表明，该架构能够实现高质量的新工作流程，而不仅仅是更快的现有工作流程：大约 27% 的任务代表了原本不会尝试的工作。该系统被其创建者描述为“一个 Unix 实用程序，而不是传统产品”，由“有用、可理解和可扩展”的最小构建块构建而成（Cherny 和 Wu，2025）。该架构投资于确定性基础设施（上下文管理、工具路由、恢复），而不是决策脚手架（显式规划器或状态图），前提是功能日益强大的模型从丰富的操作环境中获益更多，而不是从限制其选择的框架中受益。

Contextual Adaptability.

情境适应性。

The system fits the user’s specific context (their project, tools, conventions, and

该系统适合用户的特定环境（他们的项目、工具、约定和

skill level) and the relationship improves over time. The extension architecture (CLAUDE.md, skills, MCP, 3 Table 1 Design principles, the values they serve, and the design-space question each answers. Principles map to multiple values; implementations appear in the sections indicated.

技能水平），并且关系会随着时间的推移而改善。扩展架构（CLAUDE.md、技能、MCP、3 表 1 设计原则、它们所服务的价值以及每个答案的设计空间问题。原则映射到多个价值；实现出现在指示的部分中。

Context as scarce resource

上下文作为稀缺资源

hooks, plugins) provides configurability at multiple levels of context cost (Sections 6 and 7). Longitudinal data (McCain et al., 2026) shows that the human-agent relationship evolves: auto-approve rates increase

钩子、插件）提供了多个上下文成本级别的可配置性（第 6 节和第 7 节）。纵向数据（McCain 等人，2026）显示人与代理关系的演变：自动批准率增加

from approximately 20% at fewer than 50 sessions to over 40% by 750 sessions. This pattern, described as

autonomy that is “co-constructed by the model, the user, and the product,” means the system is designed for trust trajectories rather than fixed trust states. MCP’s donation to the Linux Foundation’s Agentic AI Foundation (The Linux Foundation, 2025) reflects the ecosystem dimension of this value.

“由模型、用户和产品共同构建”的自治意味着系统是针对信任轨迹而不是固定的信任状态而设计的。 MCP 对 Linux 基金会 Agentic AI 基金会（The Linux Foundation，2025）的捐赠反映了这一价值的生态系统维度。

2.2

2.2

Design Principles

设计原则

These values are operationalized through thirteen design principles, each answering a recurring question that production coding agents must resolve. Table 1 summarizes the principles; subsequent sections (Section 3 to Section 9) trace each through specific implementation choices.

这些价值观通过十三个设计原则进行操作，每个原则都回答了生产coding agent必须解决的重复出现的问题。表 1 总结了原则；后续部分（第 3 节到第 9 节）通过具体的实现选择来跟踪每个部分。

These principles can be read against three major alternative design families. First, rule-based orchestration:

这些原则可以针对三个主要的替代设计系列来解读。首先，基于规则的编排：

frameworks such as LangGraph (LangChain, Inc., 2024) encode decision logic as explicit state graphs with typed edges, choosing scaffolding over minimal harness. Second, container-isolated execution: SWE-Agent and OpenHands (Yang et al., 2024; Wang et al., 2024b) rely on Docker isolation rather than layered policy enforcement. Third, version-control-as-safety: tools like Aider (Gauthier, 2024) use Git rollback as the primary 4 User Agent Loop Interfaces Permission System Tools State & Persistence Execution Environment Prompt Command/ Task Request Progress Allow/Ask/Deny Tool Result Files/Shell/Web/MCP Load Persist Propose Action Figure 1 High-level system structure of Claude Code. The system decomposes into seven functional components: user, interfaces, the agent loop, a permission system, tools, state & persistence, and an execution environment. All entry surfaces converge on the same agent loop.

LangGraph（LangChain，Inc.，2024）等框架将决策逻辑编码为具有类型化边缘的显式状态图，选择脚手架而不是最小harness。其次，容器隔离执行：SWE-Agent 和 OpenHands（Yang 等人，2024；Wang 等人，2024b）依赖于 Docker 隔离而不是分层策略执行。第三，版本控制即安全：Aider（Gauthier，2024）等工具使用 Git 回滚作为主要 4 个用户代理循环接口 权限系统工具 状态和持久性 执行环境 提示命令/任务请求进度 允许/询问/拒绝工具 结果文件/Shell/Web/MCP 加载持久建议操作 图 1 Claude 代码的高级系统结构。该系统分为七个功能组件：用户、接口、代理循环、权限系统、工具、状态和持久性以及执行环境。所有入口表面汇聚在同一个代理环路上。

safety mechanism rather than deny-first evaluation. Claude Code’s principle set is distinctive in combining minimal decision scaffolding with layered policy enforcement, values-based judgment with deny-first defaults, and progressive context management with composable extensibility.

安全机制而不是拒绝优先评估。 Claude Code 的原则集的独特之处在于将最小的决策框架与分层策略执行、基于价值的判断与拒绝优先默认值以及渐进式上下文管理与可组合的可扩展性相结合。

2.3

2.3

From Values to Architecture

从价值观到架构

Each value traces through its principles to specific architectural decisions:

每个值都通过其原则追溯到特定的架构决策：

• Human Decision Authority motivates deny-first evaluation, the graduated trust spectrum, append-only state (auditable history), externalized programmable policy, and values-over-rules (Sections 5 to 7 and 9).

• 人类决策权威促进拒绝优先评估、分级信任谱、仅附加状态（可审计历史记录）、外部化可编程策略以及价值高于规则（第 5 至 7 和 9 节）。

• Safety, Security, and Privacy motivates defense in depth, deny-first defaults, reversibility-weighted assessment, externalized policy, and isolated subagent boundaries (Sections 5 and 8).

• 安全、安保和隐私激发深度防御、拒绝优先默认值、可逆性加权评估、外部化策略和隔离的subagent边界（第 5 节和第 8 节）。

• Reliable Execution motivates context-as-scarce-resource, append-only durable state, graceful recovery, isolated subagent boundaries, and defense in depth (Sections 4 and 7 to 9).

• 可靠执行促进上下文作为稀缺资源、仅附加持久状态、优雅恢复、隔离subagent边界以及深度防御（第 4 节和第 7 至 9 节）。

• Capability Amplification motivates minimal scaffolding, composable extensibility, reversibility-weighted risk, context management, and graceful recovery (Sections 4 to 6).

• 能力放大促进最小的脚手架、可组合的可扩展性、可逆性加权风险、上下文管理和优雅的恢复（第 4 节到第 6 节）。

• Contextual Adaptability motivates transparent file-based memory, composable extensibility, the graduated trust spectrum, and externalized programmable policy (Sections 5 to 7).

• 上下文适应性激发透明的基于文件的内存、可组合的可扩展性、分级信任范围和外部化的可编程策略（第 5 节到第 7 节）。

These mappings also reveal what the architecture does not do: it does not impose explicit planning graphs on the model’s reasoning, does not provide a single unified extension mechanism, and does not restore all session-scoped trust-related state across resume. These absences are consistent with the principle set above.

这些映射还揭示了该架构不做什么：它不会对模型的推理强加显式的规划图，不提供单一的统一扩展机制，并且不会跨恢复恢复所有会话范围的信任相关状态。这些缺席与上述原则是一致的。

2.4

2.4

A Cross-Cutting Question: Long-term Developer Capability

一个交叉问题：长期开发人员能力

The five values above describe what the architecture is designed to serve. This paper also asks a sixth question: whether the architecture helps developers preserve long-term understanding and capability. This concern is real: Anthropic’s own study of 132 engineers and researchers (Huang et al., 2025) documents a “paradox of supervision” in which overreliance on AI risks atrophying the skills needed to supervise it, and independent research (Shen and Tamkin, 2026) finds that developers in AI-assisted conditions score 17% lower on comprehension tests. However, this concern is not prominently reflected as a design driver in the architecture or in Anthropic’s stated design values. We therefore treat it not as a co-equal value but as a cross-cutting concern: a question applied across all five values in Section 12, asking whether short-term amplification costs long-term human understanding, codebase coherence, and the developer pipeline.

上述五个值描述了架构的设计目的。本文还提出了第六个问题：该架构是否有助于开发人员保持长期的理解和能力。这种担忧是真实的：Anthropic 对 132 名工程师和研究人员的研究（Huang 等人，2025）记录了一个“监督悖论”，其中过度依赖人工智能可能会削弱监督所需的技能，而独立研究（Shen 和 Tamkin，2026）发现，在人工智能辅助条件下的开发人员在理解测试中的得分降低了 17%。然而，这种担忧并未在架构或 Anthropic 规定的设计价值观中作为设计驱动因素得到突出体现。因此，我们不将其视为同等价值，而是将其视为一个跨领域的关注点：一个适用于第 12 节中所有五个价值的问题，询问短期放大是否会损害长期的人类理解、代码库一致性和开发人员管道。

5

5

3

3

Architecture Overview

架构概述

Building a production coding agent requires answering several recurring design questions: where should reasoning live, how many execution engines are needed, what safety posture to adopt, and what resource to treat as the binding constraint. Claude Code’s architecture can be read as one set of answers to these questions. At the implementation level, the system has seven components connected by a main data flow: a user submits a prompt through one of several interfaces, which feeds into a shared agent loop. The agent loop assembles context, calls the Claude model, receives responses that may include tool-use requests, routes those requests through a permission system, and dispatches approved actions to concrete tools that interact with the execution environment. Throughout this process, state and persistence mechanisms record the conversation transcript, manage session identity, and support resume, fork, and rewind operations.

构建生产coding agent需要回答几个反复出现的设计问题：推理应该在哪里进行、需要多少个执行引擎、采用什么安全态势以及将什么资源视为绑定约束。 Claude Code 的架构可以被解读为这些问题的一组答案。在实现层面，系统有七个组件通过主数据流连接：用户通过几个接口之一提交提示，该提示馈入共享代理循环。代理循环组装上下文，调用 Claude 模型，接收可能包括工具使用请求的响应，通过权限系统路由这些请求，并将批准的操作分派给与执行环境交互的具体工具。在整个过程中，状态和持久性机制会记录对话记录、管理会话身份并支持恢复、分叉和倒回操作。

3.1

3.1

Design Questions and Running Example

设计问题和运行示例

The description is organized around four design questions that recur across production coding agents, each grounding one or more of the design principles identified in Table 1. Each question is introduced here with Claude Code’s answer, a note on plausible alternatives, and then demonstrated progressively through Sections 4 to 9.

该描述围绕四个设计问题进行组织，这些问题在生产coding agent中重复出现，每个问题都基于表 1 中确定的一个或多个设计原则。这里介绍每个问题以及 Claude Code 的答案，对可行的替代方案进行注释，然后通过第 4 节到第 9 节逐步演示。

Where does reasoning live?

推理住在哪里？

In Claude Code, the model reasons about what to do; the harness is responsible

在Claude代码中，模型推理要做什么；harness负责

for executing actions. The model emits tool_use blocks as part of its response, and the harness parses them, checks permissions, dispatches them to tool implementations, and collects results (query.ts). The model never directly accesses the filesystem, runs shell commands, or makes network requests. This separation has a security consequence: because reasoning and enforcement occupy separate code paths, a compromised or adversarially manipulated model cannot override the sandboxing, permission checks, or deny-first rules implemented in the harness. The model’s only interface to the outside world is the structured tool_use protocol, which the harness validates before execution. Community analysis of the extracted source estimates that only about 1.6% of Claude Code’s codebase constitutes AI decision logic, with the remaining 98.4% being operational infrastructure, a ratio that illustrates how thin the core agent reasoning layer is. Alternative designs invest more in scaffolding-side reasoning: Devin maintains explicit planning and task-tracking structures, while LangGraph (LangChain, Inc., 2024) routes control flow through developer-defined state graphs.

用于执行操作。该模型发出 tool_use 块作为其响应的一部分，harness解析它们、检查权限、将它们分派给工具实现并收集结果 (query.ts)。该模型从不直接访问文件系统、运行 shell 命令或发出网络请求。这种分离会产生安全后果：由于推理和执行占用不同的代码路径，因此受损或敌对操纵的模型无法覆盖harness中实现的沙箱、权限检查或拒绝优先规则。该模型与外界的唯一接口是结构化的 tool_use 协议，harness在执行之前验证该协议。社区对提取的源代码进行分析估计，Claude Code 的代码库中只有约 1.6% 构成了 AI 决策逻辑，其余 98.4% 是运营基础设施，这一比例足以说明核心代理推理层的薄弱程度。替代设计在脚手架端推理上投入更多：Devin 维护明确的规划和任务跟踪结构，而 LangGraph（LangChain，Inc.，2024）通过开发人员定义的状态图路由控制流。

How many execution engines?

有多少个执行引擎？

Claude Code uses a single queryLoop() function that executes regardless of

Claude Code 使用单个 queryLoop() 函数，该函数无论在什么情况下都会执行

whether the user is interacting through an interactive terminal, a headless CLI invocation, the Agent SDK, or an IDE integration (query.ts). Only the rendering and user-interaction layer varies. Other systems use mode-specific engines: for example, an IDE integration may follow a different code path than a CLI tool, trading uniformity for surface-specific optimization.

用户是否通过交互式终端、无头 CLI 调用、Agent SDK 或 IDE 集成 (query.ts) 进行交互。只有渲染和用户交互层有所不同。其他系统使用特定于模式的引擎：例如，IDE 集成可能遵循与 CLI 工具不同的代码路径，以牺牲统一性来换取特定于表面的优化。

What is the default safety posture?

默认的安全姿势是什么？

Claude Code’s default safety posture is deny-first with human escalation:

Claude Code 的默认安全态势是“拒绝优先”并进行人为升级：

deny rules override ask rules override allow rules, and unrecognized actions are escalated to the user rather than allowed silently (permissions.ts). Multiple independent safety layers (permission rules, PreToolUse hooks, the auto-mode classifier when enabled, and optional shell sandboxing) are stacked, so any one can block an action (Section 5). This combines the deny-first with human escalation and defense in depth with layered mechanisms principles from Table 1. Alternative approaches shift the trust boundary elsewhere: SWE-Agent and OpenHands (Yang et al., 2024; Wang et al., 2024b) rely on container-based isolation to contain arbitrary execution, while Aider (Gauthier, 2024) uses git-based rollback as its primary safety net.

拒绝规则覆盖询问规则覆盖允许规则，并且无法识别的操作将升级给用户而不是静默允许（permissions.ts）。多个独立的安全层（权限规则、PreToolUse 挂钩、启用时的自动模式分类器以及可选的 shell 沙箱）堆叠在一起，因此任何一层都可以阻止某个操作（第 5 节）。这将拒绝优先与人为升级和深度防御与表 1 中的分层机制原则相结合。替代方法将信任边界转移到其他地方：SWE-Agent 和 OpenHands（Yang 等人，2024；Wang 等人，2024b）依赖基于容器的隔离来包含任意执行，而 Aider（Gauthier，2024）使用基于 git 的回滚作为其主要安全网。

What is the binding resource constraint?

什么是绑定资源约束？

In Claude Code, the context window (200K for older models, 1M

在 Claude Code 中，上下文窗口（旧型号为 200K，1M

for the Claude 4.6 series) is the binding resource constraint. Five distinct context-reduction strategies execute before every model call (query.ts), and several other subsystem decisions (lazy loading of instructions, deferred tool schemas, summary-only subagent returns) exist to limit context consumption (Section 7). The five-layer pipeline exists because no single compaction strategy addresses all types of context pressure. Budget reduction targets individual tool outputs that overflow size limits. Snip handles temporal depth. Microcompact reacts to cache overhead. Context collapse manages very long histories. Auto-compact performs semantic compression 6 User Prompt Context Assembly (settings, history) Tool Request Permission Allow Execute -> Result Sync | subagent | background Compact (context pressure) Tool Request Permission Deny Deny Feedback No Tool Use Assistant Response More Iterations Iter 1 Iter 2 Iter N ··· User Reads & Replies Figure 2 Runtime turn flow showing the end-to-end execution of a single agentic turn: user prompt enters through context assembly, the model is called, tool requests pass through the permission gate, tool results feed back into the loop, and compaction manages context pressure.

对于 Claude 4.6 系列）是绑定资源约束。在每次模型调用 (query.ts) 之前执行五种不同的上下文缩减策略，并且存在几个其他子系统决策（延迟加载指令、延迟工具模式、仅摘要subagent返回）来限制上下文消耗（第 7 节）。五层管道的存在是因为没有单一的压缩策略可以解决所有类型的上下文压力。预算削减的目标是超出规模限制的单个工具产出。 Snip 处理时间深度。 Microcompact 对缓存开销做出反应。上下文崩溃管理着很长的历史。自动压缩执行语义压缩 6 用户提示上下文组件（设置、历史） 工具请求权限 允许执行 -> 结果同步 |分代理|背景 Compact（上下文压力） 工具请求 权限拒绝 拒绝反馈 无工具使用助手响应 更多迭代 Iter 1 Iter 2 Iter N … 用户读取和回复 图 2 运行时转向流程显示单个代理转向的端到端执行：用户提示通过上下文组装进入，模型被调用，工具请求通过权限门，工具结果反馈到循环中，压缩管理上下文压力。

as a last resort. Each layer operates at a different cost-benefit tradeoff, and earlier, cheaper layers run before costlier ones. Alternative architectures treat other resources as the primary bottleneck, for instance compute budget (limiting the number of model calls or tool invocations) or working memory (maintaining an explicit scratchpad rather than relying on the conversation history).

作为最后的手段。每个层都以不同的成本效益权衡运行，并且较早、较便宜的层先于较昂贵的层运行。替代架构将其他资源视为主要瓶颈，例如计算预算（限制模型调用或工具调用的数量）或工作内存（维护显式暂存器而不是依赖于对话历史记录）。

Running example.

运行示例。

To ground these principles, we thread a single task through Sections 3 to 9: “Fix the failing test in auth.test.ts.” In this section the user submits the prompt through one of Claude Code’s interfaces.

为了落实这些原则，我们在第 3 节到第 9 节中串联了一个任务：“修复 auth.test.ts 中失败的测试。”在本节中，用户通过 Claude Code 的界面之一提交提示。

Subsequent sections trace the request through the query loop, permission gate, tool pool, context window, subagent delegation, and session persistence.

后续部分通过查询循环、权限门、工具池、上下文窗口、subagent委派和会话持久性来跟踪请求。

3.2

3.2

High-Level System Structure

高层系统结构

The seven-component model (Figure 1) maps directly to source files:

七组件模型（图 1）直接映射到源文件：

1. User: Submits prompts, approves permissions, reviews output.

1. 用户：提交提示、批准权限、审查输出。

2. Interfaces: Interactive CLI, headless CLI (claude -p), Agent SDK, and IDE/Desktop/Browser. All surfaces feed the same loop.

2. 接口：交互式 CLI、无头 CLI (claude -p)、Agent SDK 和 IDE/桌面/浏览器。所有表面都馈送相同的循环。

3. Agent loop: The iterative cycle of model call, tool dispatch, and result collection, implemented as the queryLoop() async generator in query.ts.

3. 代理循环：模型调用、工具调度和结果收集的迭代循环，在 query.ts 中实现为 queryLoop() 异步生成器。

4. Permission system: Deny-first rule evaluation (permissions.ts), the auto-mode ML classifier, and hook-based interception (types/hooks.ts).

4. 权限系统：拒绝优先规则评估 (permissions.ts)、自动模式 ML 分类器和基于钩子的拦截 (types/hooks.ts)。

5. Tools: Up to 54 built-in tools (19 unconditional, 35 conditional on feature flags and user type) assembled via assembleToolPool() (tools.ts), merged with MCP-provided tools. Plugins contribute indirectly through MCP servers and the skill/command registry.

5. 工具：通过 assembleToolPool() (tools.ts) 组装多达 54 个内置工具（19 个无条件工具，35 个基于功能标志和用户类型的条件工具），并与 MCP 提供的工具合并。插件通过 MCP 服务器和技能/命令注册表间接贡献。

6. State & persistence: Mostly append-only JSONL session transcripts (sessionStorage.ts), global prompt history (history.ts), and subagent sidechain files.

6. 状态和持久性：主要是仅附加 JSONL 会话记录 (sessionStorage.ts)、全局提示历史记录 (history.ts) 和subagent侧链文件。

7. Execution environment: Shell execution with optional sandboxing (shouldUseSandbox.ts), filesystem operations, web fetching, MCP server connections, and remote execution.

7. 执行环境：带有可选沙箱（shouldUseSandbox.ts）的 Shell 执行、文件系统操作、Web 获取、MCP 服务器连接和远程执行。

The data flow follows a left-to-right spine: the user submits a request through an interface, which enters the agent loop. The loop proposes actions to the permission system; approved actions reach tools, which interact with the execution environment and return tool_result messages back to the loop. State and persistence sit alongside the loop, recording transcripts and loading prior session data.

数据流遵循从左到右的主干：用户通过接口提交请求，该请求进入代理循环。该循环向权限系统提出操作建议；批准的操作到达工具，工具与执行环境交互并将 tool_result 消息返回到循环。状态和持久性位于循环旁边，记录转录并加载先前的会话数据。

The application entry point main() in main.tsx initializes security settings (including NoDefaultCurrentDi- rectoryInExePath to prevent Windows PATH hijacking), registers signal handlers for graceful shutdown, and dispatches to the appropriate execution mode.

main.tsx 中的应用程序入口点 main() 初始化安全设置（包括 NoDefaultCurrentDirectoryInExePath 以防止 Windows PATH 劫持）、注册信号处理程序以正常关闭，并分派到适当的执行模式。

3.3

3.3

Layered Subsystem Decomposition

分层子系统分解

The five-layer decomposition (Figure 3) expands the seven-component model into a finer-grained view, mapping each layer to specific source directories.

五层分解（图 3）将七组件模型扩展为更细粒度的视图，将每一层映射到特定的源目录。

Surface layer (entry points and rendering).

表面层（入口点和渲染）。

The src/entrypoints/ directory contains startup paths, including

src/entrypoints/目录包含启动路径，包括

the SDK entry with coreTypes.ts, controlSchemas.ts, and coreSchemas.ts. The src/screens/ directory composes full-screen layouts, and src/components/ provides terminal UI building blocks via the ink framework.

SDK 条目包含 coreTypes.ts、controlSchemas.ts 和 coreSchemas.ts。 src/screens/ 目录组成全屏布局，src/components/ 通过 ink 框架提供终端 UI 构建块。

The interactive CLI launches a terminal UI with real-time streaming, permission dialogs, and progress indicators.

交互式 CLI 启动带有实时流、权限对话框和进度指示器的终端 UI。

The headless CLI (claude -p) creates a QueryEngine instance, a thin conversation wrapper around the shared query path (Section 3.4), for single-shot processing. The Agent SDK emits typed events via async generators.

无头 CLI (claude -p) 创建一个 QueryEngine 实例，这是一个围绕共享查询路径（第 3.4 节）的瘦对话包装器，用于单次处理。 Agent SDK 通过异步生成器发出类型化事件。

Core layer (agent loop, compaction pipeline).

核心层（代理循环、压缩管道）。

The queryLoop() async generator (query.ts) implements the

queryLoop() 异步生成器 (query.ts) 实现

iterative agent loop, consuming assembled context from the state layer and dispatching tool requests to the safety/action layer. Before every model call, a compaction pipeline of five sequential shapers (query.ts:365- 453) manages context pressure: budget reduction, snip, microcompact, context collapse, and auto-compact (Sections 4.3 and 7.3).

迭代代理循环，使用来自状态层的组装上下文并将工具请求分派到安全/操作层。在每次模型调用之前，由五个顺序整形器 (query.ts:365-453) 组成的压缩管道会管理上下文压力：预算缩减、剪切、微压缩、上下文折叠和自动压缩（第 4.3 节和第 7.3 节）。

Safety/action layer (permission system, hooks, extensibility, tools, sandbox, subagents).

安全/操作层（权限系统、挂钩、可扩展性、工具、沙箱、subagent）。

The permission

许可

system (permissions.ts) implements deny-first rule evaluation with up to seven permission modes (if also counting internal-only bubble and feature-gated auto) (types/permissions.ts) and an integrated auto-mode ML classifier (yoloClassifier.ts) that provides a two-stage fast-filter and chain-of-thought evaluation of tool 8 safety (Section 5). A hook pipeline spanning 27 event types (coreTypes.ts; output schemas in types/hooks.ts) can block, rewrite, or annotate tool requests; of these, 5 are safety-related while the remaining 22 serve lifecycle and orchestration purposes (Section 6). An extensibility subsystem allows plugins and skills to register tools and hooks into the runtime. Tool pool assembly via assembleToolPool() (tools.ts) merges built-in and MCP-provided tools. Approved shell commands pass through a shell sandbox (shouldUseSandbox.ts) that restricts filesystem and network access independently of the permission system. Subagent spawning via AgentTool (AgentTool.tsx, runAgent.ts) is dispatched through the same buildTool() factory as all other tools, re-entering the queryLoop() with an isolated context window and returning only a summary to the parent (Section 8).

系统 (permissions.ts) 实现拒绝优先规则评估，具有最多七种权限模式（如果还计算内部专用气泡和功能门控自动）(types/permissions.ts) 和集成自动模式 ML 分类器 (yoloClassifier.ts)，该分类器提供工具 8 安全性的两阶段快速过滤和思想链评估（第 5 节）。跨越 27 种事件类型（coreTypes.ts；types/hooks.ts 中的输出模式）的钩子管道可以阻止、重写或注释工具请求；其中，5 个与安全相关，其余 22 个用于生命周期和编排目的（第 6 节）。可扩展性子系统允许插件和技能将工具和挂钩注册到运行时中。通过 assembleToolPool() (tools.ts) 进行的工具池组装合并了内置工具和 MCP 提供的工具。批准的 shell 命令通过 shell 沙箱 (shouldUseSandbox.ts)，该沙箱独立于权限系统限制文件系统和网络访问。通过 AgentTool（AgentTool.tsx、runAgent.ts）生成的subagent与所有其他工具一样通过相同的 buildTool() 工厂进行调度，使用隔离的上下文窗口重新进入 queryLoop() 并仅将摘要返回给父级（第 8 节）。

State layer (context assembly, runtime state, persistence, memory, sidechains).

状态层（上下文组装、运行时状态、持久性、内存、侧链）。

Context assembly is a

上下文组件是一个

memoized state loader, not a routing hub: getSystemContext() (context.ts) computes session-level system context including git status, and getUserContext() (context.ts) loads the CLAUDE.md hierarchy and current date. Both are cached for reuse: system context is appended to the system prompt, while user context is added as a user-context message. The src/state/ directory manages runtime application state.

记忆状态加载器，而不是路由集线器： getSystemContext() (context.ts) 计算会话级系统上下文，包括 git status，而 getUserContext() (context.ts) 加载 CLAUDE.md 层次结构和当前日期。两者都被缓存以供重用：系统上下文附加到系统提示符中，而用户上下文则作为用户上下文消息添加。 src/state/ 目录管理运行时应用程序状态。

Session transcripts are stored as mostly append-only JSONL files at project-specific paths (sessionStorage.ts).

会话记录主要以仅附加 JSONL 文件的形式存储在项目特定路径 (sessionStorage.ts) 中。

The CLAUDE.md + memory subsystem provides a four-level instruction hierarchy (claudemd.ts) from managed settings to directory-specific files, plus auto-memory entries that Claude writes during conversations (Section 7.2). Sidechain transcripts (sessionStorage.ts:247) store each subagent’s conversation in a separate file, preventing subagent content from inflating the parent context (Section 8.3). Global prompt history is maintained in history.jsonl (history.ts). Resume and fork operations reconstruct session state from transcripts (conversationRecovery.ts).

CLAUDE.md + 内存子系统提供了从托管设置到特定于目录的文件的四级指令层次结构 (claudemd.ts)，以及 Claude 在对话期间写入的自动内存条目（第 7.2 节）。侧链转录本 (sessionStorage.ts:247) 将每个subagent的对话存储在单独的文件中，防止subagent内容膨胀父上下文（第 8.3 节）。全局提示历史记录保存在history.jsonl (history.ts) 中。恢复和分叉操作从记录中重建会话状态 (conversationRecovery.ts)。

Backend layer (execution backends, external resources).

后端层（执行后端、外部资源）。

Shell command execution with optional sandboxing

使用可选沙箱执行 Shell 命令

(BashTool.tsx, PowerShellTool.tsx), remote execution support (src/remote/), MCP server connections across multiple transport variants including stdio, SSE, HTTP, WebSocket, SDK, and IDE-specific adapters (services/mcp/client.ts), and 42 tool subdirectories in src/tools/ implement concrete tool logic.

（BashTool.tsx、PowerShellTool.tsx）、远程执行支持（src/remote/）、跨多种传输变体（包括 stdio、SSE、HTTP、WebSocket、SDK 和 IDE 特定适配器（services/mcp/client.ts））的 MCP 服务器连接，以及 src/tools/ 中的 42 个工具子目录实现具体工具逻辑。

3.4

3.4

QueryEngine: A Clarification

QueryEngine：澄清

The class documentation at QueryEngine.ts states: “QueryEngine owns the query lifecycle and session state for a conversation. It extracts the core logic from ask() into a standalone class that can be used by both the headless/SDK path and (in a future phase) the REPL.” The class is a conversation wrapper for non-interactive surfaces, not the engine itself. Its constructor accepts a QueryEngineConfig with initial messages, an abort controller, a file-state cache, and other per-conversation state. Its submitMessage() method is an async generator that orchestrates a single turn. The shared query path lives in query() (query.ts), which wraps an internal queryLoop(); QueryEngine delegates to query().

QueryEngine.ts 的类文档指出：“QueryEngine 拥有对话的查询生命周期和会话状态。它将核心逻辑从 Ask() 提取到一个独立的类中，该类可以由 headless/SDK 路径和（在未来阶段）REPL 使用。”该类是非交互式表面的对话包装器，而不是引擎本身。它的构造函数接受带有初始消息的 QueryEngineConfig、中止控制器、文件状态缓存和其他每个会话状态。它的submitMessage()方法是一个异步生成器，可以编排单个回合。共享查询路径位于 query() (query.ts) 中，它包装了一个内部 queryLoop()； QueryEngine 委托给 query()。

This distinction matters architecturally: the interactive CLI also calls query(), bypassing QueryEngine entirely. The shared code path is the loop function, not the engine class.

这种区别在架构上很重要：交互式 CLI 还调用 query()，完全绕过 QueryEngine。共享代码路径是循环函数，而不是引擎类。

3.5

3.5

Permission and Safety Layers

权限和安全层

The safety-by-default principle is implemented through seven independent layers. A request must pass through all applicable layers, and any single layer can block it:

默认安全原则是通过七个独立层实现的。请求必须通过所有适用的层，任何单个层都可以阻止它：

1. Tool pre-filtering (tools.ts): Blanket-denied tools are removed from the model’s view before any call, preventing the model from attempting to invoke them.

1. 工具预过滤（tools.ts）：在任何调用之前，被拒绝的工具都会从模型的视图中删除，从而防止模型尝试调用它们。

2. Deny-first rule evaluation (permissions.ts): Deny rules always take precedence over allow rules, even when the allow rule is more specific.

2. 拒绝优先规则评估 (permissions.ts)：即使允许规则更具体，拒绝规则始终优先于允许规则。

3. Permission mode constraints (types/permissions.ts): The active mode determines baseline handling for requests matching no explicit rule.

3. 权限模式约束（types/permissions.ts）：主动模式确定对未匹配显式规则的请求的基线处理。

4. Auto-mode classifier: In auto mode, an ML-based classifier can evaluate requests still requiring approval after rule evaluation and fast-path checks; selected allow rules that would bypass it (e.g., Bash(*)) are set aside while auto mode is active.

4. 自动模式分类器：在自动模式下，基于机器学习的分类器可以评估规则评估和快速路径检查后仍需要批准的请求；当自动模式处于活动状态时，选定的允许绕过它的规则（例如 Bash(*)）将被搁置。

9

9

5. Shell sandboxing (shouldUseSandbox.ts): Approved shell commands may still execute inside a sandbox restricting filesystem and network access.

5. Shell 沙箱 (shouldUseSandbox.ts)：批准的 shell 命令仍可以在限制文件系统和网络访问的沙箱内执行。

6. Not restoring permissions on resume (conversationRecovery.ts): Session-scoped permissions are not stored in the transcript, so a session resumed in a new process starts without them.

6. 不恢复恢复权限 (conversationRecovery.ts)：会话范围的权限不会存储在记录中，因此在新进程中恢复的会话在没有这些权限的情况下启动。

7. Hook-based interception (types/hooks.ts): PreToolUse hooks can modify permission decisions; Per- missionRequest hooks can resolve decisions asynchronously alongside the user dialog (or before it, in coordinator mode).

7.基于Hook的拦截（types/hooks.ts）：PreToolUse钩子可以修改权限决策； PermissionRequest 挂钩可以与用户对话框一起异步解决决策（或在协调器模式下在其之前）。

These layers are described in detail in Section 5.

这些层在第 5 节中详细描述。

3.6

3.6

Context as Bottleneck: Beyond Compaction

上下文作为瓶颈：超越压缩

Beyond the five-layer compaction pipeline (detailed in Section 7), several other subsystem decisions reflect the context-as-bottleneck constraint:

除了五层压缩管道（第 7 节中详细介绍）之外，其他几个子系统决策也反映了上下文瓶颈约束：

• CLAUDE.md lazy loading: The base CLAUDE.md hierarchy is loaded at session start, but additional nested-directory instruction files and conditional rules are loaded only when the agent reads files in those directories, preventing unused instructions from consuming context.

• CLAUDE.md 延迟加载：基本 CLAUDE.md 层次结构在会话启动时加载，但仅当代理读取这些目录中的文件时才加载其他嵌套目录指令文件和条件规则，从而防止未使用的指令消耗上下文。

• Deferred tool schemas: When ToolSearch is enabled, some tools include only their names in the initial context; full schemas are loaded on demand.

• 延迟工具架构：当启用ToolSearch 时，某些工具仅在初始上下文中包含其名称。完整模式按需加载。

• Subagent summary-only return: Subagents return only summary text to the parent, not their full conversation history (Section 8).

• 仅返回subagent摘要：subagent仅向父代理返回摘要文本，而不返回完整的对话历史记录（第 8 节）。

• Per-tool-result budget: Individual tool results are capped at a configurable size, preventing a single verbose output from consuming disproportionate context.

• 每个工具结果的预算：单个工具结果的上限为可配置的大小，以防止单个详细输出消耗不成比例的上下文。

4

4

Turn Execution: The Agentic Query Loop

轮次执行：代理查询循环

When the user submits “Fix the failing test in auth.test.ts,” the input enters a reactive loop, one of several possible orchestration patterns for coding agents. This section examines Claude Code’s choice of a simple while-loop architecture and traces one turn of that loop end-to-end, illustrating three design principles from Table 1: minimal scaffolding with maximal operational harness, context as scarce resource with progressive management, and graceful recovery and resilience.

当用户提交“修复 auth.test.ts 中的失败测试”时，输入进入反应循环，这是coding agent的几种可能的编排模式之一。本节研究 Claude Code 对简单 while 循环架构的选择，并端到端地跟踪该循环的一圈，说明表 1 中的三个设计原则：具有最大操作harness的最小脚手架、具有渐进式管理的稀缺资源上下文以及优雅的恢复和弹性。

4.1

4.1

The Query Pipeline

查询管道

Each turn follows a fixed sequence (Figure 2, query.ts):

每个回合都遵循固定的顺序（图 2，query.ts）：

1. Settings resolution. The queryLoop() function destructures immutable parameters including the system prompt, user context, permission callback, and model configuration.

1. 设置分辨率。 queryLoop() 函数解构不可变参数，包括系统提示、用户上下文、权限回调和模型配置。

2. Mutable state initialization. A single State object stores all mutable state across iterations, including messages, tool context, compaction tracking, and recovery counters. The loop’s seven continue points (the “continue sites”) each overwrite this object in one whole-object assignment rather than mutating fields individually.

2. 可变状态初始化。单个 State 对象存储迭代中的所有可变状态，包括消息、工具上下文、压缩跟踪和恢复计数器。循环的七个继续点（“继续站点”）每个都在一个整体对象分配中覆盖该对象，而不是单独改变字段。

3. Context assembly. The function getMessagesAfterCompactBoundary() retrieves messages from the last compact boundary forward, ensuring that compacted content is represented by its summary rather than the original messages.

3. 上下文组装。函数 getMessagesAfterCompactBoundary() 从最后一个压缩边界向前检索消息，确保压缩内容由其摘要而不是原始消息表示。

4. Pre-model context shapers. Five shapers execute sequentially (Section 4.3).

4. 预建模环境塑造者。五个整形器按顺序执行（第 4.3 节）。

5. Model call. A for await loop over deps.callModel() streams the model’s response, passing assembled messages (with user context prepended), the full system prompt, thinking configuration, the available tool set, an abort signal, the current model specification, and additional options including fast-mode settings, effort value, and fallback model.

5.模型调用。 deps.callModel() 上的 for wait 循环流式传输模型的响应，传递组装的消息（前置用户上下文）、完整的系统提示、思考配置、可用的工具集、中止信号、当前模型规范以及其他选项，包括快速模式设置、工作量值和回退模型。

6. Tool-use dispatch. If the response contains tool_use blocks, they flow to the tool orchestration layer (Section 4.2).

6、工具使用调度。如果响应包含 tool_use 块，它们将流向工具编排层（第 4.2 节）。

7. Permission gate. Each tool request passes through the permission system (Section 5).

7. 许可门。每个工具请求都会经过权限系统（第 5 节）。

8. Tool execution and result collection. Tool results are added to the conversation as tool_result messages, and the loop continues.

8. 工具执行和结果收集。工具结果将作为 tool_result 消息添加到对话中，然后循环继续。

10

10

9. Stop condition. If the response contains no tool_use blocks (text only), the turn is complete.

9. 停止条件。如果响应不包含 tool_use 块（仅限文本），则回合完成。

The queryLoop() function is defined as an AsyncGenerator, yielding StreamEvent, RequestStartEvent, Message, TombstoneMessage, and ToolUseSummaryMessage events as it progresses. This generator-based design enables streaming output to the UI layer while maintaining a single synchronous control flow within the loop.

queryLoop() 函数被定义为 AsyncGenerator，在运行时产生 StreamEvent、RequestStartEvent、Message、TombstoneMessage 和 ToolUseSummaryMessage 事件。这种基于生成器的设计可以将流式输出输出到 UI 层，同时在循环内保持单个同步控制流。

Claude Code’s reactive loop follows the ReAct pattern (Yao et al., 2022): the model generates reasoning and tool invocations, the harness executes actions, and results feed the next iteration. Alternative orchestration patterns include explicit graph-based routing (LangChain, Inc., 2024), where control flow is defined as a state machine with typed edges, and tree-search methods (Zhou et al., 2023) that explore multiple action trajectories before committing. Anthropic’s own documentation (Schluntz and Zhang, 2024) identifies five composable workflow patterns (prompt chaining, routing, parallelization, orchestrator-workers, and evaluator-optimizer) of which Claude Code primarily uses the orchestrator-workers pattern for subagent delegation (Section 8) while keeping the core loop reactive. The reactive design trades search completeness for simplicity and latency:

Claude Code 的反应式循环遵循 ReAct 模式（Yao 等人，2022）：模型生成推理和工具调用，harness执行操作，并将结果提供给下一次迭代。替代编排模式包括显式的基于图的路由（LangChain, Inc., 2024），其中控制流被定义为具有类型化边缘的状态机，以及在提交之前探索多个操作轨迹的树搜索方法（Zhou et al., 2023）。 Anthropic 自己的文档（Schluntz 和Zhang，2024）确定了五种可组合的工作流模式（提示链、路由、并行化、编排器工作人员和评估器优化器），其中 Claude Code 主要使用编排工作人员模式进行subagent委托（第 8 节），同时保持核心循环反应性。响应式设计以搜索完整性为代价换取简单性和延迟：

each turn commits to one action sequence without backtracking.

每一回合都会执行一个动作序列而不会回溯。

4.2

4.2

Tool Dispatch and Streaming Execution

工具调度和流式执行

When the model response contains tool_use blocks, the system chooses between two execution paths. The primary path uses StreamingToolExecutor, which begins executing tools as they stream in from the model response, reducing latency for multi-tool responses. The fallback path uses runTools() in toolOrchestration.ts, which iterates over partitions produced by partitionToolCalls(). Both paths classify tools as concurrent-safe or exclusive. Read-only operations can execute in parallel, while state-modifying operations like shell commands are serialized.

当模型响应包含 tool_use 块时，系统在两个执行路径之间进行选择。主要路径使用 StreamingToolExecutor，它在工具从模型响应流入时开始执行工具，从而减少多工具响应的延迟。后备路径使用 toolOrchestration.ts 中的 runTools()，它迭代由 partitionToolCalls() 生成的分区。这两种路径都将工具分类为并发安全或独占。只读操作可以并行执行，而状态修改操作（例如 shell 命令）是串行执行的。

The StreamingToolExecutor (StreamingToolExecutor.ts) manages concurrent execution with two coordina- tion mechanisms:

StreamingToolExecutor (StreamingToolExecutor.ts) 通过两种协调机制管理并发执行：

• Sibling abort controller. Fires when any Bash tool errors, immediately terminating other in-flight subprocesses rather than letting them run to completion.

• 同级中止控制器。当任何 Bash 工具出错时触发，立即终止其他正在进行的子进程而不是让它们运行完成。

• Progress-available signal. Wakes up the getRemainingResults() consumer when new output is ready.

• 进度可用信号。当新输出准备就绪时唤醒 getRemainingResults() 使用者。

Results are buffered so that none is emitted ahead of an earlier non-concurrency-safe tool that is still running; results from concurrency-safe tools running in parallel are emitted as they complete. This concurrent-read, serial-write execution model occupies a middle ground between fully serial dispatch and more aggressive speculative approaches such as PASTE (Sui et al., 2026), which speculatively pre-executes predicted future tool calls while the model is still generating, hiding tool latency through speculation.

结果被缓冲，以便在仍在运行的早期非并发安全工具之前不会发出任何结果；并行运行的并发安全工具的结果在完成时发出。这种并发读取、串行写入执行模型占据了完全串行调度和更激进的推测方法（例如 PASTE）（Sui 等人，2026）之间的中间地带，后者在模型仍在生成时推测性地预执行预测的未来工具调用，通过推测隐藏工具延迟。

The tool result collection phase iterates over updates from either the streaming executor or the batched runTools() async generator. Both are async iterables consumed by the same for await loop; the contrast is in whether tool execution starts before the model finishes streaming (streaming) or after partitioning into concurrency-safe groups (batched). Each update may carry a tool result, an attachment, or a progress event.

工具结果收集阶段迭代来自流式执行器或批处理 runTools() 异步生成器的更新。两者都是由同一个 for wait 循环消耗的异步迭代；对比在于工具执行是在模型完成流式传输（流式传输）之前开始，还是在划分为并发安全组（批处理）之后开始。每个更新可能携带工具结果、附件或进度事件。

A special check detects hook_stopped_continuation attachments: if a PostToolUse hook signals that the turn should not continue, a shouldPreventContinuation flag is set. Results are normalized for the Anthropic API via normalizeMessagesForAPI(), filtering to keep only user-type messages.

特殊检查会检测 hook_stopped_continuation 附件：如果 PostToolUse 挂钩发出信号表明转弯不应继续，则设置 shouldPreventContinuation 标志。通过 normalizeMessagesForAPI() 对 Anthropic API 的结果进行标准化，过滤以仅保留用户类型的消息。

4.3

4.3

Pre-Model Context Shapers

预模型环境塑造者

Five context shapers execute sequentially in query.ts before every model call, each operating on the mes- sagesForQuery array. The five shapers run in sequence, with earlier steps applying lighter reductions before later steps apply broader compaction.

在每次模型调用之前，五个上下文整形器在 query.ts 中按顺序执行，每个都对 messagesForQuery 数组进行操作。五个整形器按顺序运行，前面的步骤应用较轻的缩减，然后后面的步骤应用更广泛的压缩。

Budget reduction.

预算减少。

(applyToolResultBudget()). Enforces per-message size limits on tool results, replacing oversized outputs with content references. Exempt tools (those where maxResultSizeChars is not finite) retain their full output. Content replacements are persisted for agent and session query sources to enable reconstruction on resume. Budget reduction runs before microcompact because microcompact operates purely by tool_use_id and never inspects content; the two compose cleanly.

（应用工具结果预算（））。对工具结果强制执行每条消息的大小限制，用内容引用替换过大的输出。豁免工具（maxResultSizeChars 不是有限的工具）保留其完整输出。代理和会话查询源的内容替换会保留下来，以便在恢复时进行重建。预算缩减在 microcompact 之前运行，因为 microcompact 纯粹通过 tool_use_id 运行并且从不检查内容；两人的组合干净利落。

11

11

Snip.

剪断。

(snipCompactIfNeeded(), gated by HISTORY_SNIP). A lightweight trim that removes older history segments, returning {messages, tokensFreed, boundaryMessage}. The snipTokensFreed value is plumbed to auto-compact because the main token counter derives context size from the usage field on the most recent assistant message, and that message survives snip with its pre-snip input_tokens still attached; snip’s savings are therefore invisible to the counter unless passed through explicitly.

（snipCompactIfNeeded()，由 HISTORY_SNIP 门控）。一个轻量级的修剪，删除旧的历史片段，返回{messages，tokensFreed，boundaryMessage}。 snipTokensFreed 值被检测为自动压缩，因为主token计数器从最近的辅助消息上的使用字段派生上下文大小，并且该消息在截断后仍然存在，其预截断 input_tokens 仍然附加；因此，除非明确通过，否则 snip 的节省对柜台来说是不可见的。

Microcompact.

微型。

Fine-grained compression that always runs a time-based path and optionally a cache-aware

细粒度压缩始终运行基于时间的路径和可选的缓存感知

path (gated by CACHED_MICROCOMPACT). When the cached path is enabled, boundary messages are deferred until after the API response so they can use actual cache_deleted_input_tokens rather than estimates. Returns {messages, compactionInfo} where compactionInfo may include pendingCacheEdits.

路径（由 CACHED_MICROCOMPACT 控制）。启用缓存路径后，边界消息将推迟到 API 响应之后，以便它们可以使用实际的 cache_deleted_input_tokens 而不是估计值。返回{messages，compactionInfo}，其中compactionInfo可能包括pendingCacheEdits。

Context collapse.

语境崩溃。

Gated by CONTEXT_COLLAPSE. A read-time projection over the conversation history.

由 CONTEXT_COLLAPSE 控制。对话历史记录的阅读时间投影。

The source comments explain: “Nothing is yielded; the collapsed view is a read-time projection over the REPL’s full history. Summary messages live in the collapse store, not the REPL array. This is what makes collapses persist across turns.” Unlike the other shapers, context collapse does not mutate the REPL’s stored history; it replaces the messagesForQuery array with a projected view via applyCollapsesIfNeeded(), so the model sees the collapsed version while the full history remains available for reconstruction.

源注释解释道：“没有产生任何结果；折叠视图是对 REPL 完整历史记录的读取时间投影。摘要消息存在于折叠存储中，而不是 REPL 数组中。这就是折叠在各个回合中持续存在的原因。”与其他塑造器不同，上下文崩溃不会改变 REPL 存储的历史记录；它通过 applyCollapsesIfNeeded() 将 messagesForQuery 数组替换为投影视图，因此模型可以看到折叠版本，同时完整的历史记录仍可用于重建。

Auto-compact.

自动紧凑。

The fifth shaper, triggering a full model-generated summary via compactConversation()

第五个整形器，通过compactConversation()触发完整的模型生成的摘要

in compact.ts. This function executes PreCompact hooks, creates a summary request using getCompact- Prompt(), and calls the model to produce a compressed summary. The result feeds into buildPostCom- pactMessages() (compact.ts). Auto-compact fires only when the context still exceeds the pressure threshold after all four previous shapers have run.

在compact.ts中。该函数执行 PreCompact 挂钩，使用 getCompactPrompt() 创建摘要请求，并调用模型来生成压缩摘要。结果将输入到 buildPostCompactMessages() (compact.ts) 中。仅当所有四个之前的整形器运行后上下文仍然超过压力阈值时，自动压缩才会触发。

4.4

4.4

Recovery Mechanisms

恢复机制

The query loop implements several recovery mechanisms for edge cases:

查询循环针对边缘情况实现了多种恢复机制：

• Max output tokens escalation: When the response hits the output token cap, the system can retry with an escalated limit, subject to a GrowthBook flag and the absence of an existing override or environment-variable cap. Up to three recovery attempts are allowed per turn (MAX_OUTPUT_- TOKENS_RECOVERY_LIMIT = 3).

• 最大输出token升级：当响应达到输出token上限时，系统可以使用升级的限制重试，但须遵守GrowthBook 标志以及不存在现有覆盖或环境变量上限的情况。每回合最多允许 3 次恢复尝试 (MAX_OUTPUT_- TOKENS_RECOVERY_LIMIT = 3)。

• Reactive compaction (gated by REACTIVE_COMPACT): After a prompt-too-long or oversized-media error, the loop can attempt reactive compaction and retry the request if recovery succeeds.

• 反应式压缩（由REACTIVE_COMPACT 控制）：在提示太长或过大媒体错误后，循环可以尝试反应式压缩，如果恢复成功则重试请求。

The

的

hasAttemptedReactiveCompact flag ensures this fires at most once per turn.

hasAttemptedReactiveCompact 标志确保每回合最多触发一次。

• Prompt-too-long handling: If the API returns a prompt_too_long error, the loop first attempts context- collapse overflow recovery and reactive compaction. Only after these fail does it terminate with reason:

• Prompt-too-long 处理：如果API 返回prompt_too_long 错误，则循环首先尝试上下文崩溃溢出恢复和反应式压缩。只有在这些失败之后，它才会有理由终止：

’prompt_too_long’. • Streaming fallback: The onStreamingFallback callback handles streaming API issues, allowing the loop to retry with a different strategy.

'prompt_too_long'。 • 流回退：onStreamingFallback 回调处理流API 问题，允许循环使用不同的策略重试。

• Fallback model: The fallbackModel parameter enables switching to an alternative model if the primary model fails.

• Fallback 模型：fallbackModel 参数允许在主模型失败时切换到备用模型。

4.5

4.5

Stop Conditions

停止条件

Multiple conditions can terminate the loop:

多个条件可以终止循环：

1. No tool use: The model produces only text content (the primary stop condition).

1. 不使用工具：模型仅生成文本内容（主要停止条件）。

2. Max turns: The configurable maxTurns limit is reached.

2. 最大匝数：达到可配置的最大匝数限制。

3. Context overflow: The API returns prompt_too_long.

3. 上下文溢出：API 返回prompt_too_long。

4. Hook intervention: A PostToolUse hook sets hook_stopped_continuation.

4. 钩子干预：PostToolUse 钩子设置 hook_stopped_continuation。

5. Explicit abort: The abortController signal fires.

5. 显式中止：abortController 信号触发。

The turn pipeline determines how tool requests are orchestrated and recovered. The next section examines the gate that determines whether each request is permitted to execute at all.

转向管道决定如何编排和恢复工具请求。下一节将检查决定每个请求是否被允许执行的门。
