5

5

Tool Authorization and Control Boundaries

工具授权和控制边界

Production coding agents adopt different safety architectures: layered policy enforcement, OS-level sandboxing, or version-control-based rollback. Claude Code combines the first two, implementing four design principles

生产coding agent采用不同的安全架构：分层策略实施、操作系统级沙箱或基于版本控制的回滚。 Claude Code 结合了前两者，实现了四个设计原则

from Table 1: deny-first with human escalation, graduated trust spectrum, defense in depth with layered

mechanisms, and reversibility-weighted risk assessment.

机制和可逆性加权风险评估。

When Claude decides to execute a tool (for example, running npm test via BashTool to reproduce the auth test failure), the request enters the permission pipeline shown in Figure 4. Except in bypass mode, tool invocations pass through the permission system. In the default interactive posture, actions beyond read-only access ask for approval rather than run silently, while more permissive modes replace some prompts with scoped auto-approval, denial, or classifier-mediated review. This default is motivated by a documented behavioral pattern: Anthropic’s auto-mode analysis (Hughes, 2026) found that users approve approximately 93% of permission prompts, indicating that approval fatigue renders interactive confirmation behaviorally unreliable as a sole safety mechanism. Because users habitually approve without careful review, the system must maintain safety independently of human vigilance. This motivates the architectural commitment to deny-first evaluation, blanket-deny pre-filtering, and sandboxing as independent layers that operate regardless of user attentiveness.

当 Claude 决定执行某个工具时（例如，通过 BashTool 运行 npm test 来重现身份验证测试失败），请求会进入如图 4 所示的权限管道。除了旁路模式外，工具调用都会通过权限系统。在默认的交互状态下，只读访问之外的操作请求批准而不是静默运行，而更宽松的模式则用范围内的自动批准、拒绝或分类器介导的审查替换一些提示。这种默认设置是由记录的行为模式驱动的：Anthropic 的自动模式分析（Hughes，2026）发现用户批准了大约 93% 的权限提示，这表明批准疲劳导致交互式确认作为唯一的安全机制在行为上不可靠。由于用户习惯性地在不仔细审查的情况下进行批准，因此系统必须独立于人类的警惕性来维护安全。这促使架构承诺将拒绝优先评估、全面拒绝预过滤和沙盒作为独立层，无论用户的注意力如何，都可以运行。

5.1

5.1

Permission Modes and Rule Evaluation

权限模式和规则评估

Seven permission modes exist across the type definitions (5 external modes at types/permissions.ts; auto added conditionally; bubble in the type union):

类型定义中存在七种权限模式（types/permissions.ts 中的 5 种外部模式；有条件地自动添加；类型联合中的气泡）：

1. plan: The model must create a plan; execution proceeds only after user approval.

1.计划：模型必须创建计划；仅在用户批准后才继续执行。

2. default: Standard interactive use. Most operations require user approval.

2.默认：标准交互使用。大多数操作需要用户批准。

3. acceptEdits: Edits within the working directory and certain filesystem shell commands (mkdir, rmdir, touch, rm, mv, cp, sed) are auto-approved; other shell commands require approval.

3. AcceptEdits：自动批准工作目录和某些文件系统 shell 命令（mkdir、rmdir、touch、rm、mv、cp、sed）内的编辑；其他 shell 命令需要批准。

4. auto: An ML-based classifier evaluates requests that do not pass fast-path checks (gated by TRAN- SCRIPT_CLASSIFIER).

4. auto：基于 ML 的分类器评估未通过快速路径检查的请求（由 TRANSCRIPT_CLASSIFIER 控制）。

5. dontAsk: Prompts are suppressed, and actions that would otherwise prompt are auto-denied; explicit allow and deny rules still apply.

5. dontAsk：禁止提示，自动拒绝本来会提示的操作；明确的允许和拒绝规则仍然适用。

6. bypassPermissions: Skips most permission prompts, but safety-critical checks and bypass-immune rules still apply.

6.bypassPermissions：跳过大多数权限提示，但安全关键检查和旁路免疫规则仍然适用。

7. bubble: Internal-only mode for subagent permission escalation to the parent terminal.

7. bubble：仅限内部模式，用于subagent权限升级到父终端。

The five externally visible modes (acceptEdits, bypassPermissions, default, dontAsk, plan) are defined in the EXTERNAL_PERMISSION_MODES array. The auto mode is conditionally included only when the TRANSCRIPT_CLASSIFIER feature flag is active. The bubble mode exists in the type union but not in 13 either mode array; it is used internally for subagent permission escalation (Section 8).

五种外部可见模式（acceptEdits、bypassPermissions、default、dontAsk、plan）在 EXTERNAL_PERMISSION_MODES 数组中定义。仅当 TRANSCRIPT_CLASSIFIER 功能标志处于活动状态时，才会有条件地包含自动模式。气泡模式存在于类型联合中，但不存在于 13 任一模式数组中；它在内部用于subagent权限升级（第 8 节）。

Permission rules are evaluated in deny-first order (permissions.ts). The toolMatchesRule() function checks deny rules first: a deny rule always takes precedence over an allow rule, even when the allow rule is more specific. A broad deny (“deny all shell commands”) cannot be overridden by a narrow allow (“allow npm test”). The rule system supports tool-level matching (by tool name) and content-level matching (matching specific tool input patterns, such as Bash(prefix:npm)).

权限规则按拒绝优先的顺序进行评估 (permissions.ts)。 toolMatchesRule() 函数首先检查拒绝规则：拒绝规则始终优先于允许规则，即使允许规则更具体也是如此。宽泛的拒绝（“拒绝所有 shell 命令”）不能被狭义的允许（“允许 npm 测试”）覆盖。规则系统支持工具级匹配（按工具名称）和内容级匹配（匹配特定工具输入模式，例如 Bash(prefix:npm)）。

The seven modes span a graduated autonomy spectrum, from plan (user approves all plans before execution) through default and acceptEdits to bypassPermissions (minimal prompting).

这七种模式涵盖了分级自治范围，从计划（用户在执行前批准所有计划）到默认和接受编辑到绕过权限（最少提示）。

This gradient reflects a

这个梯度反映了

recurring design tension: as autonomy increases, the system must shift from interactive approval to automated safety checks. Other agent systems resolve this tension differently. SWE-Agent and OpenHands (Yang et al., 2024; Wang et al., 2024b) use Docker container isolation, sandboxing the agent’s entire execution environment rather than evaluating individual tool invocations. Aider (Gauthier, 2024) relies on Git as a safety net, making all changes reversible through version control. Claude Code’s approach layers multiple policy-enforcement mechanisms on top of an optional shell sandbox (Section 5.4), trading simplicity for fine-grained control over individual actions.

反复出现的设计紧张：随着自主权的增加，系统必须从交互式审批转向自动化安全检查。其他代理系统以不同的方式解决这种紧张关系。 SWE-Agent 和 OpenHands（Yang 等人，2024；Wang 等人，2024b）使用 Docker 容器隔离，对代理的整个执行环境进行沙箱处理，而不是评估单个工具调用。 Aider (Gauthier, 2024) 依赖 Git 作为安全网，通过版本控制使所有更改都可逆。 Claude Code 的方法将多个策略执行机制置于可选的 shell 沙箱之上（第 5.4 节），牺牲了简单性，换取了对各个操作的细粒度控制。

5.2

5.2

The Authorization Pipeline

授权管道

The full authorization pipeline proceeds through several stages:

完整的授权管道经过几个阶段：

Pre-filtering.

预过滤。

Before any tool request reaches runtime evaluation, filterToolsByDenyRules() (tools.ts) strips blanket-denied tools from the model’s view entirely at tool pool assembly time. The documentation states:

在任何工具请求到达运行时评估之前，filterToolsByDenyRules() (tools.ts) 在工具池组装时完全从模型视图中删除全面拒绝的工具。文档指出：

“Uses the same matcher as the runtime permission check, so MCP server-prefix rules like mcp__server strip all tools from that server before the model sees them.” This prevents the model from attempting to invoke forbidden tools, so the model does not waste calls on them.

“使用与运行时权限检查相同的匹配器，因此像 mcp__server 这样的 MCP 服务器前缀规则会在模型看到它们之前从该服务器中删除所有工具。”这可以防止模型尝试调用禁止的工具，因此模型不会浪费对它们的调用。

PreToolUse hook.

PreToolUse 挂钩。

Registered hooks fire as part of the permission pipeline. A PreToolUse hook can re-

注册的钩子作为权限管道的一部分触发。 PreToolUse 钩子可以重新

turn a permissionDecision to deny or ask, or an updatedInput that modifies the tool’s input parameters (types/hooks.ts). A hook allow does not bypass subsequent rule-based denies or safety checks. In the interactive path, the user dialog is queued first and hooks run asynchronously; coordinator and similar background-agent paths await automated checks before showing the dialog.

转动permissionDecision来拒绝或询问，或者转动updatedInput来修改工具的输入参数（types/hooks.ts）。挂钩允许不会绕过后续基于规则的拒绝或安全检查。在交互路径中，用户对话框首先排队，钩子异步运行；协调器和类似的后台代理路径在显示对话框之前等待自动检查。

Rule evaluation.

规则评估。

The deny-first rule engine evaluates the request. MCP tools are matched by their fully

拒绝优先规则引擎评估该请求。 MCP 工具完全匹配

qualified mcp__server__tool name, and server-level rules match all tools from that server.

限定的 mcp__server__tool 名称，并且服务器级规则匹配该服务器中的所有工具。

Permission handler.

权限处理程序。

The handler in useCanUseTool.tsx branches into one of four paths based on runtime

useCanUseTool.tsx 中的处理程序根据运行时分支到四个路径之一

context: 1. Coordinator: For multi-agent coordination mode. Attempts automated resolution (classifier, hooks, rules) before falling back to user interaction.

context: 1. Coordinator：用于多Agent协调模式。在回退到用户交互之前尝试自动解决（分类器、挂钩、规则）。

2. Swarm worker: Handles worker agents in a multi-agent swarm with their own resolution logic.

2. Swarm Worker：使用自己的解析逻辑处理多代理群中的工作代理。

3. Speculative classifier: When BASH_CLASSIFIER is enabled and the tool is BashTool, a speculative classifier races a pre-started classification result against a timeout. If the classifier returns with high confidence, the tool is approved instantly without user interaction.

3. 推测分类器：当启用 BASH_CLASSIFIER 并且工具是 BashTool 时，推测分类器会与预先启动的分类结果与超时进行竞争。如果分类器以高置信度返回，则该工具会立即获得批准，无需用户交互。

4. Interactive: The fallback path. Presents the standard user approval dialog through the terminal UI.

4. 互动：后备路径。通过终端 UI 呈现标准用户批准对话框。

In coordinator and some background paths, automated resolution is attempted before user interaction. In the standard interactive path, the dialog can appear first while hooks or classifier checks continue in parallel.

在协调器和某些后台路径中，会在用户交互之前尝试自动解决。在标准交互路径中，对话框可以首先出现，同时挂钩或分类器检查继续并行。

When the classifier or a deny rule blocks an action, the system treats the denial as a routing signal rather than a hard stop: the model receives the denial reason, revises its approach, and attempts a safer alternative in the next loop iteration. The PermissionDenied hook event (Section 6) enables external code to observe and respond to these denials programmatically. This recovery-oriented design means that permission enforcement shapes the agent’s behavior rather than simply halting it.

当分类器或拒绝规则阻止某个操作时，系统会将拒绝视为路由信号而不是硬停止：模型接收拒绝原因，修改其方法，并在下一个循环迭代中尝试更安全的替代方案。 PermissionDenied 挂钩事件（第 6 节）使外部代码能够以编程方式观察和响应这些拒绝。这种以恢复为导向的设计意味着权限执行会塑造代理的行为，而不是简单地停止它。

14

14

5.3

5.3

Auto-Mode Classifier and Hook Lifecycle

自动模式分类器和 Hook 生命周期

The auto-mode classifier (yoloClassifier.ts) participates in permission decisions when enabled. When TRAN- SCRIPT_CLASSIFIER is enabled, the classifier loads three prompt resources:

自动模式分类器（yoloClassifier.ts）在启用时参与权限决策。启用 TRANSCRIPT_CLASSIFIER 时，分类器会加载三个提示资源：

• A base system prompt. • An external permissions template.

• 基本系统提示符。 • 外部权限模板。

• For Anthropic-internal users, a separate internal template.

• 对于Anthropic 内部用户，单独的内部模板。

The classifier evaluates the proposed tool invocation against the conversation transcript and the permission tem- plate, producing an allow, deny, or request for manual approval. The function isUsingExternalPermissions() checks USER_TYPE and a forceExternalPermissions config flag to select the appropriate template.

分类器根据对话记录和权限模板评估建议的工具调用，生成允许、拒绝或手动批准的请求。函数isUsingExternalPermissions() 检查USER_TYPE 和forceExternalPermissions 配置标志以选择适当的模板。

Of the 27 hook events defined in the source (coreTypes.ts), five participate directly in the permission flow, each with a specific Zod-validated output schema (types/hooks.ts):

在源代码 (coreTypes.ts) 中定义的 27 个挂钩事件中，有 5 个直接参与权限流，每个事件都具有经过 Zod 验证的特定输出模式 (types/hooks.ts)：

• PreToolUse: Can return permissionDecision (deny or ask, but allow does not bypass subsequent checks), permissionDecisionReason, and updatedInput (modify parameters).

• PreToolUse：可以返回permissionDecision（拒绝或询问，但允许不绕过后续检查）、permissionDecisionReason 和updatedInput（修改参数）。

• PostToolUse: Can inject additionalContext and, for MCP tools, return updatedMCPToolOutput to modify results before they enter the context.

• PostToolUse：可以注入additionalContext，并且对于MCP 工具，返回updatedMCPToolOutput 以在结果进入上下文之前修改结果。

• PostToolUseFailure: Can inject additionalContext for error-specific guidance.

• PostToolUseFailure：可以注入额外的上下文以获取特定于错误的指导。

• PermissionDenied: Can provide retry guidance after auto-mode denials.

• PermissionDenied：可以在自动模式拒绝后提供重试指导。

• PermissionRequest: Can return a decision of allow or deny. In coordinator and similar paths, this can resolve before the user dialog. In the standard interactive path, it can also run alongside the dialog.

• PermissionRequest：可以返回允许或拒绝的决定。在协调器和类似路径中，这可以在用户对话之前解决。在标准交互路径中，它也可以与对话框一起运行。

For non-MCP tools, the tool_result is emitted before the PostToolUse hook fires. For MCP tools, the result is delayed until after post hooks have run, enabling updatedMCPToolOutput to take effect.

对于非 MCP 工具，tool_result 在 PostToolUse 挂钩触发之前发出。对于 MCP 工具，结果会延迟到运行后挂钩之后，从而使 UpdatedMCPToolOutput 生效。

5.4

5.4

Shell Sandboxing

壳牌沙箱

Shell sandboxing provides an additional layer of protection for Bash and PowerShell commands (shouldUseSandbox.ts).

Shell 沙箱为 Bash 和 PowerShell 命令提供了额外的保护层 (shouldUseSandbox.ts)。

The shouldUseSandbox() function checks whether sandboxing is globally enabled, whether the invocation has opted out, and whether the command matches any exclusion patterns.

shouldUseSandbox() 函数检查沙箱是否全局启用、调用是否已选择退出以及命令是否与任何排除模式匹配。

When active, the sandbox provides filesystem and network isolation independent of the application-level permission model.3 A command can be permission-approved but still sandboxed, or permission-denied and never reach the sandbox check. The two systems operate on different axes: authorization versus isolation.

当处于活动状态时，沙箱提供独立于应用程序级权限模型的文件系统和网络隔离。3命令可以获得权限批准但仍处于沙箱状态，也可以拒绝权限但永远不会到达沙箱检查。这两个系统在不同的轴上运行：授权与隔离。

The layered safety architecture rests on an independence assumption: if one layer fails, others catch the violation. However, several layers share common performance constraints. Security researchers (Adversa.ai, 2026) have documented that commands with more than 50 subcommands fall back to a single generic approval prompt instead of running per-subcommand deny-rule checks, because per-subcommand parsing caused UI freezes. This example demonstrates that defense-in-depth can degrade when its layers share failure modes, a structural tension between safety and performance analyzed further in Section 12.3.

分层安全架构基于独立假设：如果一层失败，其他层就会发现违规行为。然而，多个层具有共同的性能限制。安全研究人员（Adversa.ai，2026）记录了具有超过 50 个子命令的命令会回退到单个通用批准提示，而不是运行每个子命令的拒绝规则检查，因为每个子命令的解析会导致 UI 冻结。这个例子表明，当纵深防御的各层共享故障模式时，纵深防御可能会降级，这是第 12.3 节中进一步分析的安全性和性能之间的结构张力。

The permission pipeline governs whether a tool request executes. The next section examines what determines which tools exist in the first place: the extensibility architecture that assembles the model’s action surface.

权限管道控制工具请求是否执行。下一节将首先研究决定哪些工具存在的因素：组装模型操作面的可扩展性架构。

6

6

Extensibility: MCP, Plugins, Skills, and Hooks

可扩展性：MCP、插件、技能和挂钩

A recurring design question for coding agents is how to structure the extension surface: a single unified mechanism, a small number of specialized mechanisms, or a layered stack with different context costs.

coding agent的一个反复出现的设计问题是如何构建扩展表面：单个统一机制、少量专门机制或具有不同上下文成本的分层堆栈。

The analysis here illustrates two design principles from Table 1: composable multi-mechanism extensibility and externalized programmable policy. Returning to the running example, once Claude is trying to repair auth.test.ts and the earlier npm test request has been mediated by the permission system (Section 5), the 3Independent security research reports a parser-differential gap in this network confinement during the analyzed window: the host allowlist used a JavaScript suffix match while name resolution truncated at an embedded null byte, so a crafted hostname could pass the suffix check yet resolve to a different, blocked host. The reported affected range (v2.0.24–v2.1.89) includes the v2.1.88 snapshot analyzed here, and no CVE was assigned (Cyber Security News, 2026) (Tier C).

这里的分析说明了表 1 中的两个设计原则：可组合的多机制可扩展性和外部化的可编程策略。回到正在运行的示例，一旦 Claude 尝试修复 auth.test.ts 并且早期的 npm 测试请求已由权限系统（第 5 节）调解，3Independent 安全研究报告在分析窗口期间此网络限制中存在解析器差异差距：主机白名单使用 JavaScript 后缀匹配，而名称解析在嵌入的空字节处被截断，因此精心设计的主机名可以通过后缀检查，但解析为不同的、被阻止的主机。报告的受影响范围 (v2.0.24–v2.1.89) 包括此处分析的 v2.1.88 快照，并且未分配 CVE（网络安全新闻，2026 年）（C 级）。

a assemble() controls what the model sees, b model() controls what it can reach, and c execute() controls whether and how an action actually runs.

a assemble() 控制模型看到的内容，b model() 控制模型可以到达的内容，cexecute() 控制操作是否实际运行以及如何运行。

next question is what extension-enabled action surface is available for the repair. When a turn begins in Claude Code, the model sees not just built-in tools like BashTool and FileReadTool, but also database query tools from an MCP server, a custom lint skill from .claude/skills/, and tools contributed by an installed plugin. These arrive through four mechanisms that extend the agent at different points of the loop: MCP servers provide external tool integration, plugins package and distribute bundles of components, skills inject domain-specific instructions, and hooks intercept the tool execution lifecycle. Anthropic’s documentation (Anthropic, 2026h) presents a broader view that includes CLAUDE.md (Section 7) and subagents (Section 8) alongside the four mechanisms analyzed here. We treat CLAUDE.md and subagents in their own sections because they operate in different subsystems (context construction and delegation, respectively), but the context-cost ordering is architecturally significant: it reveals how each extension point trades off expressiveness against the bounded context window.

下一个问题是哪些支持扩展的操作界面可用于修复。当 Claude Code 中的回合开始时，模型不仅会看到 BashTool 和 FileReadTool 等内置工具，还会看到来自 MCP 服务器的数据库查询工具、来自 .claude/skills/ 的自定义 lint 技能以及由已安装插件提供的工具。这些通过在循环的不同点扩展代理的四种机制实现：MCP 服务器提供外部工具集成、插件打包和分发组件包、技能注入特定于域的指令以及拦截工具执行生命周期的钩子。 Anthropic 的文档（Anthropic，2026h）提出了更广泛的观点，包括 CLAUDE.md（第 7 节）和subagent（第 8 节）以及此处分析的四种机制。我们将 CLAUDE.md 和subagent放在各自的部分中，因为它们在不同的子系统中运行（分别是上下文构建和委托），但上下文成本排序在架构上具有重要意义：它揭示了每个扩展点如何权衡有界上下文窗口的表达能力。

6.1

6.1

Four Extension Mechanisms

四种扩展机制

The mechanisms are implemented in distinct source directories (Figure 5) and serve different integration patterns:

这些机制在不同的源目录中实现（图 5）并服务于不同的集成模式：

MCP servers.

MCP 服务器。

The Model Context Protocol is the primary external tool integration path. MCP servers are

模型上下文协议是主要的外部工具集成路径。 MCP 服务器是

configured from multiple scopes: project, user, local, and enterprise, with additional plugin and claude.ai servers merged at runtime (services/mcp/config.ts). The MCP client (services/mcp/client.ts) supports multiple transport types: stdio, SSE, HTTP, WebSocket, SDK, plus IDE-specific variants (sse-ide, ws-ide) and an internal claudeai-proxy. Each connected server contributes tool definitions as MCPTool objects. Dedicated built-in tools ListMcpResourcesTool and ReadMcpResourceTool provide access to MCP resources.

从多个范围进行配置：项目、用户、本地和企业，并在运行时合并其他插件和 claude.ai 服务器 (services/mcp/config.ts)。 MCP 客户端 (services/mcp/client.ts) 支持多种传输类型：stdio、SSE、HTTP、WebSocket、SDK，以及 IDE 特定变体（sse-ide、ws-ide）和内部 claudeai-proxy。每个连接的服务器都将工具定义作为 MCPTool 对象提供。专用内置工具 ListMcpResourcesTool 和 ReadMcpResourceTool 提供对 MCP 资源的访问。

16

16

Plugins.

插件。

Plugins serve a dual role: they are both a packaging format and a distribution mechanism. The PluginManifestSchema (utils/plugins/schemas.ts) accepts ten component types: commands, agents, skills, hooks, MCP servers, LSP servers, output styles, channels, settings, and user configuration. The plugin loader (utils/plugins/pluginLoader.ts) validates manifests and routes each component to its respective registry:

插件有双重作用：它们既是一种打包格式，又是一种分发机制。 PluginManifestSchema (utils/plugins/schemas.ts) 接受十种组件类型：命令、代理、技能、挂钩、MCP 服务器、LSP 服务器、输出样式、通道、设置和用户配置。插件加载器 (utils/plugins/pluginLoader.ts) 验证清单并将每个组件路由到其各自的注册表：

commands and skills surface through the SkillTool meta-tool, agents appear in definitions consumed by AgentTool, hooks merge into the hook registry, MCP and LSP servers fold into their standard configurations, and output styles modify response formatting. A single plugin package can therefore extend Claude Code across multiple component types simultaneously, making plugins the primary distribution vehicle for third-party extensions.

命令和技能通过 SkillTool 元工具显示，代理出现在 AgentTool 使用的定义中，挂钩合并到挂钩注册表中，MCP 和 LSP 服务器折叠到其标准配置中，输出样式修改响应格式。因此，单个插件包可以同时跨多个组件类型扩展 Claude Code，使插件成为第三方扩展的主要分发工具。

Skills.

技能。

Each skill is defined by a SKILL.md file with YAML frontmatter. The parseSkillFrontmatterFields()

每个技能都由带有 YAML frontmatter 的 SKILL.md 文件定义。 parseSkillFrontmatterFields()

function (loadSkillsDir.ts) parses 15+ fields including display name, description, allowed tools (granting

the skill access to additional tools), argument hints, model overrides, execution context (’fork’ for isolated execution), associated agent definitions, effort levels, and shell configuration. Skills can define their own hooks, which register dynamically on invocation. Bundled skills are registered in-memory at startup. When invoked, the SkillTool meta-tool injects the skill’s instructions into the context.

访问其他工具的技能）、参数提示、模型覆盖、执行上下文（用于隔离执行的“分叉”）、关联的代理定义、工作量级别和 shell 配置。技能可以定义自己的钩子，这些钩子在调用时动态注册。捆绑技能在启动时注册在内存中。调用时，SkillTool 元工具会将技能的指令注入上下文中。

Hooks.

钩子。

The source code defines 27 hook events spanning tool authorization (PreToolUse, PostToolUse, Post- ToolUseFailure, PermissionRequest, PermissionDenied), session lifecycle (SessionStart, SessionEnd, Setup, Stop, StopFailure), user interaction (UserPromptSubmit, Elicitation, ElicitationResult), subagent coor- dination (SubagentStart, SubagentStop, TeammateIdle, TaskCreated, TaskCompleted), context manage- ment (PreCompact, PostCompact, InstructionsLoaded, ConfigChange), workspace events (CwdChanged, FileChanged, WorktreeCreate, WorktreeRemove), and notifications (coreTypes.ts, coreSchemas.ts). Of these, 15 have event-specific output schemas with rich fields supporting permission decisions, context injec- tion, input modification, MCP result transformation, and retry control (types/hooks.ts). Persisted hook commands configured via settings and plugins use four command types: shell commands (type: command), LLM prompt hooks (type: prompt), HTTP hooks (type: http), and agentic verifier hooks (type: agent) (schemas/hooks.ts). The runtime additionally supports non-persistable callback hooks (type: callback) used by the SDK and internal instrumentation (types/hooks.ts). Hook sources include settings.json, plugins, and managed policy at startup; skill hooks register dynamically on invocation (utils/hooks.ts). The five tool-authorization events are detailed in Section 5.3.

源代码定义了 27 个挂钩事件，涵盖工具授权（PreToolUse、PostToolUse、PostToolUseFailure、PermissionRequest、PermissionDenied）、会话生命周期（SessionStart、SessionEnd、Setup、Stop、StopFailure）、用户交互（UserPromptSubmit、Elicitation、EliitationResult）、subagent协调（SubagentStart、SubagentStop、TeammateIdle、TaskCreated、TaskCompleted）、上下文管理（PreCompact、PostCompact、InstructionsLoaded、ConfigChange）、工作区事件（CwdChanged、FileChanged、WorktreeCreate、WorktreeRemove）和通知（coreTypes.ts、coreSchemas.ts）。其中，15 个具有特定于事件的输出模式，具有丰富的字段支持权限决策、上下文注入、输入修改、MCP 结果转换和重试控制（types/hooks.ts）。通过设置和插件配置的持久挂钩命令使用四种命令类型：shell 命令（类型：command）、LLM 提示挂钩（类型：prompt）、HTTP 挂钩（类型：http）和代理验证器挂钩（类型：agent）(schemas/hooks.ts)。运行时还支持 SDK 和内部检测 (types/hooks.ts) 使用的非持久回调挂钩（类型：callback）。钩子源包括settings.json、插件和启动时的托管策略；技能挂钩在调用时动态注册（utils/hooks.ts）。 5.3 节详细介绍了五个工具授权事件。

6.2

6.2

Tool Pool Assembly

工具池装配

The assembleToolPool() function at tools.ts is described in source comments as “the single source of truth for combining built-in tools with MCP tools.” The assembly follows a five-step pipeline:

tools.ts 中的 assembleToolPool() 函数在源代码注释中被描述为“将内置工具与 MCP 工具相结合的单一事实来源”。组装遵循五步流程：

1. Base tool enumeration. getAllBaseTools() (tools.ts) returns an array of up to 54 tools: 19 are always included (such as BashTool, FileReadTool, AgentTool, SkillTool), and 35 more are conditionally included based on feature flags, environment variables, and user type. Anthropic-internal users get additional internal tools. Worktree mode enables EnterWorktreeTool and ExitWorktreeTool. Agent swarms enable team tools. When embedded search tools are available in the Bun binary, dedicated GlobTool and GrepTool are omitted.

1. 基础工具枚举。 getAllBaseTools() (tools.ts) 返回最多包含 54 个工具的数组：始终包含 19 个工具（例如 BashTool、FileReadTool、AgentTool、SkillTool），根据功能标志、环境变量和用户类型有条件包含另外 35 个工具。 Anthropic-内部用户可以获得额外的内部工具。 Worktree 模式启用 EnterWorktreeTool 和 ExitWorktreeTool。代理群启用团队工具。当 Bun 二进制文件中提供嵌入式搜索工具时，将省略专用的 GlobTool 和 GrepTool。

2. Mode filtering. getTools() (tools.ts) applies mode-specific filtering. In CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE mode, only Bash, Read, and Edit are available (or REPLTool in the REPL branch; plus coordinator tools if applicable). Each tool’s isEnabled() method is called for runtime availability checks.

2.模式过滤。 getTools() (tools.ts) 应用特定于模式的过滤。在 CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE 模式下，仅 Bash、Read 和 Edit 可用（或 REPL 分支中的 REPLTool；加上协调器工具（如果适用））。每个工具的 isEnabled() 方法都会被调用以进行运行时可用性检查。

3. Deny rule pre-filtering. filterToolsByDenyRules() (tools.ts) strips blanket-denied tools from the model’s view before any call.

3.拒绝规则预过滤。 filterToolsByDenyRules() (tools.ts) 在任何调用之前从模型视图中删除全面拒绝的工具。

4. MCP tool integration. MCP tools from appState.mcp.tools are filtered by deny rules and merged with built-in tools.

4.MCP工具集成。 appState.mcp.tools 中的 MCP 工具按拒绝规则过滤并与内置工具合并。

5. Deduplication. Tools are deduplicated by name, with built-in tools taking precedence over MCP tools.

5. 重复数据删除。工具按名称进行重复数据删除，内置工具优先于 MCP 工具。

Both REPL.tsx (via the useMergedTools hook) and AgentTool.tsx (when building the worker tool set) invoke this function, ensuring consistent assembly across all execution paths. At request time, deferred tools may be hidden from the model’s context until explicitly queried via ToolSearch (tools.ts).

REPL.tsx（通过 useMergedTools 挂钩）和 AgentTool.tsx（构建辅助工具集时）都会调用此函数，以确保所有执行路径中的汇编一致。在请求时，延迟工具可能会从模型上下文中隐藏，直到通过 ToolSearch (tools.ts) 显式查询。

17

17 号

Agent-based extension (custom agent definitions via .claude/agents/*.md and plugin-contributed agents) is covered in Section 8, because agents differ fundamentally from the four mechanisms above: they create new, isolated context windows rather than extending the current one.

基于代理的扩展（通过 .claude/agents/*.md 自定义代理定义和插件贡献的代理）将在第 8 节中介绍，因为代理与上述四种机制有根本的不同：它们创建新的、独立的上下文窗口，而不是扩展当前窗口。

6.3

6.3

Why Four Mechanisms?

为什么有四种机制？

Given that each additional extension mechanism increases the surface area developers must learn, a natural question is why Claude Code uses four distinct mechanisms rather than consolidating into one or two. The answer lies in the observation that different kinds of extensibility impose different costs on the context window, and a single mechanism cannot span the full range from zero-context lifecycle hooks to schema-heavy tool servers without forcing unnecessary trade-offs on extension authors.

鉴于每种额外的扩展机制都会增加开发人员必须学习的表面积，一个自然的问题是为什么 Claude Code 使用四种不同的机制而不是合并为一两种机制。答案在于观察到不同类型的可扩展性对上下文窗口施加了不同的成本，并且单一机制无法跨越从零上下文生命周期挂钩到模式密集型工具服务器的整个范围，而不迫使扩展作者进行不必要的权衡。

Table 2 What each extension mechanism uniquely provides. Context cost refers to how much of the bounded context window the mechanism consumes when active.

表 2 每种扩展机制独特提供的内容。上下文成本是指机制在活动时消耗多少有界上下文窗口。

Mechanism

机制

Unique Capability

独特的能力

Context Cost

上下文成本

Insertion Point

插入点

MCP servers

MCP服务器

External service integration (multi-transport)

外部服务集成（多传输）

High once tool schemas load

一次工具模式加载量高

model():tool pool Plugins Multi-component packaging + distribution Medium (varies) All three points Skills Domain-specific instructions + meta-tool invocation Low until invoked assemble():context injection Hooks Lifecycle interception + event-driven automation Zero by default execute():pre/post tool As Table 2 summarizes, each mechanism trades deployment complexity for a different kind of extensibility.

model():工具池 插件 多组件打包 + 分发 中等（可变） 所有三点 技能 特定于领域的指令 + 元工具调用 低直到调用 assemble():上下文注入 挂钩 生命周期拦截 + 事件驱动的自动化 默认情况下为零execute():前/后工具 正如表 2 所总结的，每种机制都以部署复杂性换取不同类型的可扩展性。

MCP servers provide runtime tool integration (the model gains new callable tools) at the cost of server management overhead and context budget consumed by tool schemas, which by default load only when a tool is needed. Skills shape how the agent thinks (not just what tools it has) at minimal context cost, since only frontmatter descriptions stay in the prompt until the skill is invoked. Hooks provide cross-cutting lifecycle control (blocking, rewriting, or annotating tool calls) with no context footprint by default, though hooks can opt into injecting additional context. Plugins bundle any combination of the other three into distributable packages, acting as the packaging and distribution layer rather than a distinct runtime primitive.

MCP 服务器提供运行时工具集成（模型获得新的可调用工具），但代价是工具模式消耗的服务器管理开销和上下文预算，默认情况下仅在需要工具时才加载。技能以最小的上下文成本塑造代理的思维方式（而不仅仅是其拥有的工具），因为在调用技能之前，只有前面的描述会保留在提示中。钩子提供横切生命周期控制（阻止、重写或注释工具调用），默认情况下没有上下文足迹，尽管钩子可以选择注入额外的上下文。插件将其他三个的任意组合捆绑到可分发包中，充当打包和分发层而不是独特的运行时原语。

The graduated context-cost ordering (zero for hooks, low for skills before invocation, medium for plugins, high for MCP) means that cheap extensions can scale widely without exhausting the context window, while expensive ones are reserved for cases that genuinely require new tool surfaces.

分级上下文成本排序（挂钩为零，调用前技能较低，插件中等，MCP 高）意味着廉价的扩展可以在不耗尽上下文窗口的情况下广泛扩展，而昂贵的扩展则保留用于真正需要新工具表面的情况。

Some agent frameworks provide a single extension mechanism, typically a tool-only API where all customization arrives as additional callable tools. Others use two tiers, separating tools from configuration or instruction injection. Claude Code’s four-mechanism approach can accommodate a broader range of extension patterns,

一些代理框架提供单一扩展机制，通常是纯工具 API，其中所有自定义都作为附加可调用工具到达。其他人使用两层，将工具与配置或指令注入分开。 Claude Code 的四种机制方法可以适应更广泛的扩展模式，

from zero-context event handlers to full external service integrations, but it increases the learning curve

developers face when deciding which mechanism to use for a given integration task.

开发人员在决定对给定集成任务使用哪种机制时面临的问题。

7

7

Context Construction and Memory

语境构建和记忆

How an agent manages its context window and persists user instructions is a central design choice, with different systems choosing between file-based transparency, database-backed retrieval, and opaque learned representations. The design choices here implement two principles from Table 1: context as scarce resource with progressive management and transparent file-based configuration and memory.

代理如何管理其上下文窗口并保留用户指令是一个核心设计选择，不同的系统在基于文件的透明性、数据库支持的检索和不透明的学习表示之间进行选择。这里的设计选择实现了表 1 中的两个原则：上下文作为稀缺资源，具有渐进式管理和透明的基于文件的配置和内存。

By this point in the running example, the task has accumulated state: the original request, the npm test permission outcome, the tool pool assembled in Section 6, and any file reads or command outputs gathered so far. This section asks how that growing state is packed into Claude Code’s bounded context window before the next model call.

此时，在运行示例中，任务已累积状态：原始请求、npm 测试权限结果、第 6 节中组装的工具池以及迄今为止收集的任何文件读取或命令输出。本节询问如何在下一次模型调用之前将增长状态打包到 Claude Code 的有界上下文窗口中。

Before the model is called, the agent loop assembles a context window from the tool pool (Section 6), CLAUDE.md files, auto memory, and conversation history. The following subsections cover the assembly order, the CLAUDE.md hierarchy, and the multi-step compaction pipeline.

在调用模型之前，代理循环从工具池（第 6 节）、CLAUDE.md 文件、自动内存和对话历史记录中组装上下文窗口。以下小节介绍了组装顺序、CLAUDE.md 层次结构和多步骤压缩管道。

7.1

7.1

Context Window Assembly

上下文窗口组件

The context window (Figure 6) is assembled from the following sources, some at initial assembly and others injected late during the turn:

上下文窗口（图 6）由以下来源组装而成，一些是在初始组装时进行的，另一些是在回合后期注入的：

1. System prompt, incorporating output style modifications and any --append-system-prompt flag content.

1. 系统提示符，包含输出样式修改和任何 --append-system-prompt 标志内容。

2. Environment info via getSystemContext() (context.ts): git status (skipped in remote mode or when git instructions are disabled) and an optional cache-breaking injection for internal builds (gated by BREAK_CACHE_COMMAND). Memoized once per session.

2. 通过 getSystemContext() (context.ts) 获取环境信息：git 状态（在远程模式下或禁用 git 指令时跳过）和用于内部构建的可选缓存破坏注入（由 BREAK_CACHE_COMMAND 控制）。每个会话记住一次。

3. CLAUDE.md hierarchy via getUserContext() (context.ts): four-level instruction file hierarchy (Sec- tion 7.2). Also memoized.

3. 通过 getUserContext() (context.ts) 的 CLAUDE.md 层次结构：四级指令文件层次结构（第 7.2 节）。也记下来了。

4. Path-scoped rules: conditional and directory-matched rules that load lazily when the agent reads files in matching directories.

4. 路径范围规则：当代理读取匹配目录中的文件时延迟加载的条件和目录匹配规则。

5. Auto memory: contextually relevant memory entries prefetched asynchronously.

5. 自动内存：异步预取上下文相关的内存条目。

6. Tool metadata: skill descriptions, MCP tool names, and deferred tool definitions (via ToolSearch, on demand).

6. 工具元数据：技能描述、MCP 工具名称和延迟工具定义（通过 ToolSearch，按需）。

7. Conversation history: carried forward, subject to compaction.

7. 对话历史：结转，需压缩。

8. Tool results: file reads, command outputs, subagent summaries.

8. 工具结果：文件读取、命令输出、subagent摘要。

9. Compact summaries: replacing older history segments.

9. 紧凑摘要：替换旧的历史片段。

The system prompt assembly at query.ts combines system context with the base prompt via asSystem- Prompt(appendSystemContext(systemPrompt, systemContext))(). User context (CLAUDE.md and date) is prepended to the message array via prependUserContext(). This separation means CLAUDE.md content occupies a different structural position in the API request than the system prompt, potentially affecting model attention patterns.

query.ts 中的系统提示组件通过 asSystemPrompt(appendSystemContext(systemPrompt, systemContext))() 将系统上下文与基本提示结合起来。用户上下文（CLAUDE.md 和日期）通过 prependUserContext() 添加到消息数组之前。这种分离意味着 CLAUDE.md 内容在 API 请求中占据与系统提示不同的结构位置，可能会影响模型注意力模式。

Several context sources are injected late, after the main window is constructed: relevant-memory prefetch 19 (query.ts), MCP instructions deltas (only new or changed server instructions), agent listing deltas, and background agent task notifications. The context window is therefore not static at assembly time but can grow during the turn.

在构建主窗口之后，稍后注入多个上下文源：相关内存预取 19 (query.ts)、MCP 指令增量（仅新的或更改的服务器指令）、代理列表增量和后台代理任务通知。因此，上下文窗口在组装时不是静态的，而是可以在转动过程中增长。

7.2

7.2

CLAUDE.md Hierarchy and Auto Memory

CLAUDE.md 层次结构和自动内存

A design principle shapes the memory system: stored context should be inspectable and editable by the user.

记忆系统的设计原则是：存储的上下文应该可由用户检查和编辑。

CLAUDE.md files are plain-text Markdown rather than structured configuration or opaque database entries.

CLAUDE.md 文件是纯文本 Markdown，而不是结构化配置或不透明的数据库条目。

This transparency choice trades expressiveness for auditability: users can directly inspect and edit the memory files they control, and project-level memory can be version-controlled alongside the codebase (Anthropic, 2026i; MindStudio Team, 2026). Alternative memory architectures illustrate the trade-off. Retrieval-augmented approaches use embedding-based lookup to surface relevant prior context, gaining flexibility at the cost of inspectability: the user cannot easily see or edit what the retrieval system considers relevant. Database-backed memory offers structured querying but requires additional infrastructure and is opaque to version control.

这种透明度选择以表达力换取可审计性：用户可以直接检查和编辑他们控制的内存文件，项目级内存可以与代码库一起进行版本控制（Anthropic，2026i；MindStudio Team，2026）。替代内存架构说明了这种权衡。检索增强方法使用基于嵌入的查找来显示相关的先验上下文，以牺牲可检查性为代价获得灵活性：用户无法轻松查看或编辑检索系统认为相关的内容。数据库支持的内存提供结构化查询，但需要额外的基础设施，并且对版本控制不透明。

Claude Code’s file-based approach makes these memory surfaces readable and editable, even though other context sources still enter through the system prompt, tools, hooks, MCP servers, and runtime state. The system does not use embeddings or a vector similarity index for memory retrieval; instead it uses an LLM-based scan of memory-file headers to select up to five relevant files on demand, surfacing them at file granularity rather than entry granularity. Embedding-based systems can retrieve individual entries more selectively, at the cost of inspectability and the infrastructure needed to maintain an index.

Claude Code 基于文件的方法使这些内存表面可读和可编辑，即使其他上下文源仍然通过系统提示符、工具、挂钩、MCP 服务器和运行时状态进入。该系统不使用嵌入或向量相似性索引来进行内存检索；相反，它使用基于 LLM 的内存文件头扫描来按需选择最多五个相关文件，以文件粒度而不是条目粒度来显示它们。基于嵌入的系统可以更有选择性地检索单个条目，但代价是可检查性和维护索引所需的基础设施。

CLAUDE.md files follow a multi-level loading hierarchy. The source header (claudemd.ts) defines four memory types:

CLAUDE.md 文件遵循多级加载层次结构。源头（claudemd.ts）定义了四种内存类型：

1. Managed memory (e.g. /etc/claude-code/CLAUDE.md on Linux): OS-level policy for all users.

1. 托管内存（例如 Linux 上的 /etc/claude-code/CLAUDE.md）：适用于所有用户的操作系统级策略。

2. User memory (~/.claude/CLAUDE.md): private global instructions.

2. 用户内存（~/.claude/CLAUDE.md）：私有全局指令。

3. Project memory (CLAUDE.md, .claude/CLAUDE.md, and .claude/rules/*.md in project roots): in- structions checked into the codebase.

3. 项目内存（项目根目录中的 CLAUDE.md、.claude/CLAUDE.md 和 .claude/rules/*.md）：签入代码库的指令。

4. Local memory (CLAUDE.local.md in project roots): gitignored, for private project-specific instructions.

4. 本地内存（项目根目录中的 CLAUDE.local.md）：gitignored，用于私有项目特定指令。

File discovery traverses from the current directory up to root, checking for all project and local memory files in each directory. Files closer to the current directory have higher priority (loaded later).

文件发现从当前目录一直遍历到根目录，检查每个目录中的所有项目和本地内存文件。靠近当前目录的文件具有更高的优先级（稍后加载）。

Files load in “reverse order of priority”: later-loaded files receive more model attention. For root-to-CWD directories, unconditional rules from .claude/rules/*.md load eagerly at startup. For nested directories below CWD, even unconditional rules are loaded lazily when the agent reads files in matching directories. This means the model’s instruction set can evolve during a conversation as new parts of the codebase are explored.

文件以“优先级相反的顺序”加载：稍后加载的文件会受到更多的模型关注。对于根到 CWD 目录，来自 .claude/rules/*.md 的无条件规则在启动时会立即加载。对于 CWD 以下的嵌套目录，当代理读取匹配目录中的文件时，即使是无条件规则也会延迟加载。这意味着随着代码库新部分的探索，模型的指令集可以在对话过程中不断发展。

CLAUDE.md content is delivered as user context (a user message), not as system prompt content (context.ts).

CLAUDE.md 内容作为用户上下文（用户消息）传递，而不是作为系统提示内容（context.ts）传递。

This architectural choice has a significant implication: because CLAUDE.md content is delivered as conversa- tional context rather than system-level instructions, model compliance with these instructions is probabilistic rather than guaranteed. Permission rules evaluated in deny-first order (Section 5) provide the deterministic enforcement layer. This creates a deliberate separation between guidance (CLAUDE.md, probabilistic) and enforcement (permission rules, deterministic). The function calls setCachedClaudeMdContent() to cache the loaded content for the auto-mode classifier, to avoid an import cycle between the CLAUDE.md loader and the permission system.

这种架构选择具有重要意义：因为 CLAUDE.md 内容是作为会话上下文而不是系统级指令提供的，所以模型对这些指令的合规性是概率性的，而不是保证的。以拒绝优先顺序评估的权限规则（第 5 节）提供了确定性执行层。这在指导（CLAUDE.md，概率性）和执行（许可规则，确定性）之间故意分开。该函数调用 setCachedClaudeMdContent() 来缓存自动模式分类器加载的内容，以避免 CLAUDE.md 加载器和权限系统之间的导入循环。

Memory files support an @include directive for modular instruction sets (processMemoryFile() at claudemd.ts).

内存文件支持模块化指令集的 @include 指令（claudemd.ts 中的 processMemoryFile()）。

Syntax variants include @path, @./relative, @~/home, and @/absolute. The directive works in leaf text nodes only (not inside code blocks). In the implementation, the including file is pushed first and included files are appended after it, circular references are prevented by tracking processed paths, and non-existent files are silently ignored.

语法变体包括@path、@./relative、@~/home 和@/absolute。该指令仅适用于叶文本节点（不适用于代码块内）。在实现中，首先推送包含文件，然后追加包含文件，通过跟踪处理的路径来防止循环引用，并且不存在的文件将被静默忽略。

7.3

7.3

Compaction Pipeline

压实管道

The five-layer compaction pipeline (Section 4.3) implements the “context as bottleneck” principle through graduated compression (query.ts). Rather than a single strategy, Claude Code applies five layers in sequence, each with increasing aggressiveness (three are gated by build-time feature flags and budget reduction by 20 a runtime flag, while auto-compact is user-configurable). This graduated approach contrasts with simpler alternatives such as single-pass truncation (dropping the oldest messages) or a single summarization step. The graduated design reflects a lazy-degradation principle: apply the least disruptive compression first, escalating only when cheaper strategies prove insufficient. The cost of this approach is complexity. Five interacting compression layers, several gated by feature flags, create behavior that is difficult for users to fully predict.

五层压缩管道（第 4.3 节）通过分级压缩（query.ts）实现“上下文作为瓶颈”原则。 Claude Code 不是采用单一策略，而是按顺序应用五层，每层都具有越来越大的攻击性（其中三层由构建时功能标志和预算减少 20 个运行时标志来控制，而自动压缩是用户可配置的）。这种分级方法与更简单的替代方法形成对比，例如单遍截断（删除最旧的消息）或单个汇总步骤。分级设计反映了惰性降级原则：首先应用破坏性最小的压缩，只有当更便宜的策略证明不够时才升级。这种方法的成本是复杂性。五个交互的压缩层（其中几个由功能标志控制）会创建用户难以完全预测的行为。

Auto-compact produces a visible summary in the transcript, and microcompact emits a boundary marker, but context collapse operates without user-visible output. Simpler single-pass approaches sacrifice information but are easier to reason about.

自动压缩会在记录中生成可见的摘要，而微型压缩会发出边界标记，但上下文折叠会在没有用户可见的输出的情况下进行操作。更简单的单遍方法会牺牲信息，但更容易推理。

1. Budget reduction (runtime flag): per-tool-result size limits.

1. 预算减少（运行时标志）：每个工具结果的大小限制。

2. Snip (HISTORY_SNIP): lightweight older-history trimming.

2. Snip (HISTORY_SNIP)：轻量级旧历史修剪。

3. Microcompact (CACHED_MICROCOMPACT): fine-grained cache-aware compression.

3. Microcompact (CACHED_MICROCOMPACT)：细粒度缓存感知压缩。

4. Context collapse (CONTEXT_COLLAPSE): read-time virtual projection over history.

4. 上下文折叠（CONTEXT_COLLAPSE）：读取时对历史的虚拟投影。

5. Auto-compact (enabled by default, can be disabled): full model-generated summary.

5. Auto-compact（默认启用，可以禁用）：完整模型生成的摘要。

The buildPostCompactMessages() function (compact.ts) returns the following compacted output structure:

buildPostCompactMessages() 函数 (compact.ts) 返回以下压缩输出结构：

[boundaryMarker, ...summaryMessages, ...messagesToKeep, ...attachments, ...hookResults]. The bound- ary marker is annotated with preserved-segment metadata via annotateBoundaryWithPreservedSegment(), recording headUuid, anchorUuid, and tailUuid to enable read-time chain patching. This mostly-append design means compaction never modifies or deletes previously written transcript lines; it only appends new boundary and summary events.

[boundaryMarker、...summaryMessages、...messagesToKeep、...附件、...hookResults]。边界标记通过 annotateBoundaryWithPreservedSegment() 用保留段元数据进行注释，记录 headUuid、anchorUuid 和 tailUuid 以启用读取时间链修补。这种主要是附加的设计意味着压缩永远不会修改或删除以前编写的转录行；它仅附加新的边界和摘要事件。

The compaction function compactConversation() (compact.ts) includes several design choices. Pre-compact hooks fire first, allowing hook-injected custom instructions. A GrowthBook feature flag controls whether the compaction path reuses the main conversation’s prompt cache (a code comment documents a January 2026 experiment: “false path is 98% cache miss, costs ∼0.76% of fleet cache_creation”). After compaction, attachment builders re-announce runtime state (plans, skills, and async agents) from live app state, since compaction discards prior attachment messages but not the underlying state.

压缩函数compactConversation() (compact.ts) 包括多种设计选择。预压缩钩子首先触发，允许钩子注入自定义指令。 GrowthBook 功能标志控制压缩路径是否重用主对话的提示缓存（代码注释记录了 2026 年 1 月的实验：“错误路径是 98% 的缓存未命中，成本约为舰队缓存_创建的 0.76%”）。压缩后，附件构建器从实时应用程序状态重新宣布运行时状态（计划、技能和异步代理），因为压缩会丢弃先前的附件消息，但不会丢弃底层状态。

Context isolation becomes more critical when the system delegates work to subagents, each operating in its own bounded context window.

当系统将工作委托给subagent（每个subagent都在自己的有界上下文窗口中运行）时，上下文隔离变得更加重要。

8

8

Subagent Delegation and Orchestration

subagent委派和编排

Multi-agent orchestration is a key design dimension for coding agents, with choices spanning parent-child hierarchies, peer-based conversation frameworks (Wu et al., 2024), and graph-structured workflow en- gines (LangChain, Inc., 2024). Claude Code’s delegation architecture implements the isolated subagent boundaries principle from Table 1, together with aspects of deny-first with human escalation (permission override) and reversibility-weighted risk assessment (subagent tool restrictions).

多代理编排是coding agent的一个关键设计维度，其选择涵盖父子层次结构、基于对等的对话框架（Wu et al., 2024）和图结构工作流引擎（LangChain, Inc., 2024）。 Claude Code 的委托架构实现了表 1 中的隔离subagent边界原则，以及拒绝优先、人工升级（权限覆盖）和可逆性加权风险评估（subagent工具限制）等方面。

When Claude determines that the auth test fix requires first exploring the authentication module’s structure, it can delegate this exploration to a subagent. The delegation mechanism is the Agent tool (AgentTool.tsx), with Task retained as a legacy alias. The model invokes Agent with a structured input including the delegation prompt, an optional subagent type, and configuration for isolation mode, permission overrides, and working directory.

当 Claude 确定身份验证测试修复需要首先探索身份验证模块的结构时，它可以将此探索委托给subagent。委托机制是Agent 工具 (AgentTool.tsx)，任务保留为旧别名。该模型使用结构化输入调用代理，包括委托提示、可选的subagent类型以及隔离模式、权限覆盖和工作目录的配置。

8.1

8.1

The Agent Tool and Delegation Criteria

Agent 工具和委派标准

The Agent tool input schema (Figure 7) uses feature-gated fields, omitting optional parameters when their backing features are disabled. The isolation field offers [’worktree’, ’remote’] for internal users and [’worktree’] for external users, determined at build time. The cwd field is gated by a feature flag. The run_in_background field is omitted when background tasks are disabled or when fork-subagent mode is enabled.

Agent 工具输入架构（图 7）使用功能门控字段，在禁用其支持功能时省略可选参数。隔离字段为内部用户提供[‘worktree’、‘remote’]，为外部用户提供[‘worktree’]，在构建时确定。 cwd 字段由功能标志控制。当禁用后台任务或启用 fork-sub-agent 模式时，将省略 run_in_background 字段。

Claude Code provides up to six built-in subagent types, depending on feature flags and entrypoint:

Claude Code 提供多达六种内置subagent类型，具体取决于功能标志和入口点：

• Explore: primarily read/search-oriented investigation, with write and edit tools in its deny-list.

• 探索：主要以读取/搜索为导向的调查，在其拒绝列表中包含写入和编辑工具。

• Plan: creates structured plans; execution proceeds through the standard permission model.

• 计划：创建结构化计划；执行通过标准权限模型进行。

• General-purpose: broadly capable, used when explicitly requested (note: omitting the type may route to the fork-subagent path instead, which reuses the parent’s conversation context rather than isolating it).

• 通用：功能广泛，在明确请求时使用（注意：省略类型可能会路由到 fork-sub-agent 路径，该路径会重用父级的对话上下文而不是隔离它）。

• Claude Code Guide: onboarding and documentation assistance, with its own permissionMode override.

• Claude 代码指南：入门和文档帮助，具有自己的权限模式覆盖。

• Verification: runs validation checks (test suites, linting).

• 验证：运行验证检查（测试套件、linting）。

• Statusline-setup: specialized for terminal status line configuration.

• Statusline-setup：专门用于终端状态线配置。

Beyond built-ins, users define custom subagents via .claude/agents/*.md files, and plugins contribute agent definitions via loadPluginAgents.ts. The markdown body of each file serves as the agent’s system prompt, and YAML frontmatter specifies configuration fields including description, tools (allowlist), disallowedTools, model, effort, permissionMode, mcpServers, hooks, maxTurns, skills, memory scope, background flag, and isolation mode. JSON-formatted agent definitions support the same fields plus prompt as an explicit field (loadAgentsDir.ts). This means a custom agent can be a fully configured, isolated sub-system with its own tools, model, permissions, hooks, memory scope, and isolation mode. AgentTool sits alongside SkillTool in the base tool pool as a meta-tool that dispatches to these definitions, but the two differ fundamentally:

除了内置插件之外，用户还可以通过 .claude/agents/*.md 文件定义自定义subagent，而插件则通过 loadPluginAgents.ts 提供代理定义。每个文件的 markdown 正文作为代理的系统提示符，YAML frontmatter 指定配置字段，包括描述、工具（白名单）、disallowedTools、模型、工作量、permissionMode、mcpServers、hooks、maxTurns、技能、内存范围、后台标志和隔离模式。 JSON 格式的代理定义支持与显式字段 (loadAgentsDir.ts) 相同的字段和提示。这意味着自定义代理可以是一个完全配置的、隔离的子系统，具有自己的工具、模型、权限、挂钩、内存范围和隔离模式。 AgentTool 与 SkillTool 一起位于基础工具池中，作为分派这些定义的元工具，但两者有根本的不同：

SkillTool typically injects instructions into the current context window (with an opt-in context: fork mode that spawns an isolated sub-agent via the same runAgent() machinery), while AgentTool always spawns a new, isolated one. The tradeoff is that most subagent invocations require a self-contained prompt, because the default path does not inherit the parent’s conversation history (the fork-subagent path is an exception).

SkillTool 通常将指令注入当前上下文窗口（使用选择加入上下文：fork 模式，通过相同的 runAgent() 机制生成一个隔离的subagent），而 AgentTool 始终生成一个新的、隔离的subagent。代价是大多数subagent调用需要一个独立的提示，因为默认路径不会继承父代理的对话历史记录（fork-sub-agent 路径是一个例外）。

Conversation-based frameworks that share full transcript histories avoid this cost but risk context explosion as the number of agents grows.

共享完整记录历史的基于对话的框架避免了这种成本，但随着代理数量的增长，存在上下文爆炸的风险。

8.2

8.2

Isolation Architecture

隔离架构

Subagent isolation supports multiple modes (AgentTool.tsx):

subagent隔离支持多种模式（AgentTool.tsx）：

• Worktree: Creates a temporary git worktree, giving the subagent its own copy of the repository to modify without affecting the parent’s working tree.

• 工作树：创建临时 git 工作树，为subagent提供自己的存储库副本以进行修改，而不会影响父代理的工作树。

• Remote (internal-only): Launches in a remote Claude Code Remote environment, always running in the background.

• 远程（仅限内部）：在远程Claude Code Remote 环境中启动，始终在后台运行。

• In-process (default): Shares the filesystem with the parent but operates in an isolated conversation context.

• 进程内（默认）：与父级共享文件系统，但在隔离的对话上下文中运行。

The permission override logic for subagents (runAgent.ts) involves several specific rules. When a subagent 22 defines a permissionMode, the override is applied unless the parent is already in bypassPermissions, ac- ceptEdits, or auto mode, since those modes always take precedence because they represent explicit user decisions about the safety/autonomy trade-off. For async agents, the system determines whether to avoid prompts through a cascade: explicit canShowPermissionPrompts first, then bubble mode (always show, since they escalate to the parent terminal), then the default (sync agents show prompts, async agents do not). Background agents that can show prompts set awaitAutomatedChecksBeforeDialog: true, ensuring the classifier and hooks resolve before interrupting the user.

subagent (runAgent.ts) 的权限覆盖逻辑涉及几个特定规则。当subagent22定义permissionMode时，除非父代理已经处于bypassPermissions、acceptEdits或自动模式，否则应用覆盖，因为这些模式总是优先，因为它们代表关于安全/自主权衡的明确用户决定。对于异步代理，系统通过级联确定是否避免提示：首先显式 canShowPermissionPrompts，然后是冒泡模式（始终显示，因为它们升级到父终端），然后是默认模式（同步代理显示提示，异步代理不显示提示）。可以显示提示的后台代理设置了awaitAutomatedChecksBeforeDialog: true，确保分类器和挂钩在中断用户之前解析。

These isolation modes occupy different points in a design space. Container-based isolation (used by SWE-Agent and OpenHands (Yang et al., 2024; Wang et al., 2024b)) provides stronger resource boundaries but requires container infrastructure. Context-only isolation (used by conversation-based frameworks like AutoGen (Wu et al., 2024)) shares the filesystem but separates conversation histories. Claude Code’s worktree-based isolation provides workspace-level filesystem separation without introducing container orchestration, leveraging Git’s built-in mechanism.

这些隔离模式占据设计空间中的不同点。基于容器的隔离（由 SWE-Agent 和 OpenHands 使用（Yang 等人，2024；Wang 等人，2024b））提供了更强的资源边界，但需要容器基础设施。仅上下文隔离（由 AutoGen（Wu 等人，2024）等基于对话的框架使用）共享文件系统，但分离对话历史记录。 Claude Code 基于工作树的隔离利用 Git 的内置机制，提供工作区级别的文件系统分离，而无需引入容器编排。

When allowedTools is explicitly provided to runAgent() (runAgent.ts), a two-tier permission scoping model applies. SDK-level permissions from --allowedTools are preserved: “explicit permissions from the SDK consumer that should apply to all agents.” But session-level rules are replaced with the subagent’s declared allowedTools. When allowedTools is not provided (the common AgentTool path), the parent’s session-level rules are inherited without replacement.

当 allowedTools 显式提供给 runAgent() (runAgent.ts) 时，将应用两层权限范围模型。保留 --allowedTools 中的 SDK 级别权限：“来自 SDK 使用者的显式权限应适用于所有代理。”但会话级规则被subagent声明的 allowedTools 所取代。当未提供 allowedTools 时（公共 AgentTool 路径），父级的会话级规则将被继承而无需替换。

8.3

8.3

Sidechain Transcripts

侧链转录本

Each subagent writes its own transcript as a separate .jsonl file with a .meta.json metadata file (sessionStorage.ts, runAgent.ts). This sidechain design means subagent histories are preserved for debugging and auditing but do not inflate the parent’s session file. Only the subagent’s final response text and metadata return to the parent conversation context; the full subagent history never enters the parent’s context window, respecting the “context as bottleneck” principle.

每个subagent将自己的记录写入一个单独的 .jsonl 文件和 .meta.json 元数据文件（sessionStorage.ts、runAgent.ts）。这种侧链设计意味着保留subagent历史记录以供调试和审计，但不会增加父代理的会话文件。只有subagent的最终响应文本和元数据返回到父对话上下文；完整的subagent历史记录永远不会进入父代理的上下文窗口，尊重“上下文作为瓶颈”原则。

The summary-only return model is a deliberate context-conservation choice: conversation-based frameworks that share full transcript histories between agents risk context explosion as the number of agents grows. Even isolated-context parallelism carries substantial cost. Claude Code’s agent teams consume approximately 7× the tokens of a standard session in plan mode (Anthropic, 2025b), which makes summary-only return more critical when subagents are also in isolated contexts.

仅摘要返回模型是一种有意的上下文保护选择：随着代理数量的增长，在代理之间共享完整转录历史记录的基于对话的框架面临着上下文爆炸的风险。即使是孤立上下文并行也会带来巨大的成本。 Claude Code 的代理团队在计划模式下消耗大约 7 倍的标准会话代币（Anthropic，2025b），这使得当subagent也处于隔离上下文中时，仅摘要返回变得更加重要。

For multi-instance coordination in agent teams, the harness uses file locking rather than a message bro- ker or distributed coordination service (Anthropic, 2025b). Each teammate has its own inbox JSON file (utils/teammateMailbox.ts); task assignments and other inter-agent messages are pushed into the recipient’s inbox under a per-inbox lockfile (utils/lockfile.ts), with files stored at predictable filesystem paths. This trades throughput for two properties: zero-dependency deployment (no external infrastructure required) and full debuggability (any agent’s state can be inspected by reading plain-text JSON files).

对于代理团队中的多实例协调，harness使用文件锁定而不是消息代理或分布式协调服务（Anthropic，2025b）。每个队友都有自己的收件箱 JSON 文件 (utils/teammateMailbox.ts)；任务分配和其他代理间消息被推送到收件人收件箱中的每个收件箱锁定文件 (utils/lockfile.ts) 下，文件存储在可预测的文件系统路径中。这以吞吐量换取两个属性：零依赖部署（不需要外部基础设施）和完全可调试性（可以通过读取纯文本 JSON 文件来检查任何代理的状态）。

9

9

Session Persistence and Recovery

Session Persistence and Recovery

Session persistence in coding agents involves a design choice between append-only logs, structured databases, checkpoint-based snapshots, and stateless architectures, each with different trade-offs in auditability, query power, and deployment complexity. Claude Code’s persistence design implements the append-only durable state principle from Table 1. Session-scoped permissions live in memory only and are not serialized to the transcript, so a new process started with --resume rebuilds the permission context from CLI args and disk settings; any request that the rebuilt context does not recognize falls back to deny-first prompting.

coding agent中的会话持久性涉及仅附加日志、结构化数据库、基于检查点的快照和无状态架构之间的设计选择，每种架构在可审核性、查询能力和部署复杂性方面都有不同的权衡。 Claude Code 的持久性设计实现了表 1 中的仅追加持久状态原则。会话范围的权限仅存在于内存中，不会序列化到记录中，因此以 --resume 启动的新进程会根据 CLI 参数和磁盘设置重建权限上下文；重建上下文无法识别的任何请求都会回退到拒绝优先提示。

By the time the auth-test task reaches this section, the session contains the original prompt, tool invocations and results, compact boundaries, and the subagent summary from exploring the authentication module (Section 8). This section asks which of those artifacts are durably recorded and what can be recovered later without carrying forward the session’s old permission grants.

当身份验证测试任务到达此部分时，会话包含原始提示、工具调用和结果、紧凑边界以及探索身份验证模块的subagent摘要（第 8 节）。本节询问哪些工件是持久记录的，以及哪些工件可以在以后恢复而无需继承会话的旧权限授予。

Figure 8 Session persistence and context compaction. The diagram separates live session state (context window, compaction) from durable storage (session transcripts, history.jsonl, subagent sidechains, checkpoints). The transcript restores messages but not session-scoped permissions.

图 8 会话持久性和上下文压缩。该图将实时会话状态（上下文窗口、压缩）与持久存储（会话记录、history.jsonl、subagent侧链、检查点）分开。脚本恢复消息，但不恢复会话范围的权限。

Claude Code’s persistence mechanisms write the conversation (messages, tool results, and compact boundaries) to disk as events occur.

当事件发生时，Claude Code 的持久性机制将对话（消息、工具结果和紧凑边界）写入磁盘。

9.1

9.1

Transcript Model

转录模型

Session transcripts are stored as mostly append-only JSONL files at a project-specific path (with explicit cleanup rewrites as an exception) (Figure 8). The getTranscriptPath() function (sessionStorage.ts) com- putes this as join(projectDir, ${getSessionId()}.jsonl), where projectDir is determined by first checking getSessionProjectDir() (set by switchSession() during resume/branch) and falling back to getProject- Dir(getOriginalCwd())().

会话记录主要以仅附加 JSONL 文件的形式存储在项目特定的路径中（显式清理重写作为例外）（图 8）。 getTranscriptPath() 函数 (sessionStorage.ts) 将其计算为 join(projectDir, ${getSessionId()}.jsonl)，其中 projectDir 通过首先检查 getSessionProjectDir() （在恢复/分支期间由 switchSession() 设置）并回退到 getProjectDir(getOriginalCwd())() 来确定。

Three persistence channels operate independently:

三个持久通道独立运行：

1. Session transcripts: Conversation records including user, assistant, attachment, and system messages, plus compaction and other metadata events. Project-scoped, one file per session.

1. 会话记录：对话记录，包括用户、助理、附件和系统消息，以及压缩和其他元数据事件。项目范围内，每个会话一个文件。

2. Global prompt history: User prompts only, stored in history.jsonl at the Claude configuration home directory (history.ts). The makeHistoryReader() generator yields entries in reverse order via readLi- nesReverse(), supporting Up-arrow and ctrl+r navigation.

2. 全局提示历史记录：仅限用户提示，存储在 Claude 配置主目录 (history.ts) 的 History.jsonl 中。 makeHistoryReader() 生成器通过 readLinesReverse() 以相反的顺序生成条目，支持向上箭头和 ctrl+r 导航。

3. Subagent sidechains: Separate .jsonl + .meta.json files per subagent (Section 8.3).

3. subagent侧链：每个subagent单独的 .jsonl + .meta.json 文件（第 8.3 节）。

Session transcripts store several kinds of events beyond simple messages, including compaction markers, file- history snapshots, attribution snapshots, and content-replacement records. The append-only JSONL format is a deliberate choice favoring auditability and simplicity over query power. Every logged event is human-readable, version-controllable, and inspectable without specialized tooling. Database-backed alternatives would enable richer queries over session history but introduce deployment dependencies and reduce transparency.

会话记录除了简单的消息之外还存储多种事件，包括压缩标记、文件历史快照、属性快照和内容替换记录。仅附加 JSONL 格式是一个经过深思熟虑的选择，它有利于可审计性和简单性，而不是查询能力。每个记录的事件都是人类可读的、版本可控的，并且无需专门的工具即可检查。数据库支持的替代方案可以对会话历史记录进行更丰富的查询，但会引入部署依赖性并降低透明度。

The session identity system pairs sessionId with sessionProjectDir, set together during resume or branch.

会话标识系统将 sessionId 与 sessionProjectDir 配对，在恢复或分支期间设置在一起。

The transcript path must use the same project directory that was active when messages were written, to avoid hooks looking in the wrong directory.

脚本路径必须使用与写入消息时处于活动状态的相同项目目录，以避免挂钩在错误的目录中查找。

9.2

9.2

Resume, Fork, and Not Restoring Permissions

恢复、分叉和不恢复权限

The --resume flag rebuilds the conversation by replaying the transcript (conversationRecovery.ts). Fork creates a new session from an existing one (commands/branch/branch.ts). However, the transcript does not record session-scoped permissions. Resuming with --resume starts a new process without them, so users must grant them again; resuming or forking inside a running session keeps the grants already given. This is a deliberate safety-conservative design choice: trust decisions are not carried in the transcript. Restoring previously granted permissions from the transcript would create a convenience benefit but risk carrying stale trust decisions into a changed context. The architecture opts for re-granting in a new process over implicit persistence, accepting the resulting user friction.

--resume 标志通过重播记录 (conversationRecovery.ts) 来重建对话。 Fork 从现有会话 (commands/branch/branch.ts) 创建一个新会话。但是，记录不记录会话范围的权限。使用 --resume 恢复会在没有它们的情况下启动一个新进程，因此用户必须再次授予它们；在正在运行的会话中恢复或分叉会保留已授予的权限。这是一个经过深思熟虑的安全保守设计选择：信任决策不包含在记录中。从记录中恢复先前授予的权限将带来便利，但存在将过时的信任决策带入已更改环境的风险。该架构选择在新进程中重新授予而不是隐式持久性，接受由此产生的用户摩擦。

24

24

The compact_boundary marker is carefully designed to work with persistence. The annotateBoundaryWith- PreservedSegment() function (compact.ts) records headUuid, anchorUuid, and tailUuid in the boundary event. These UUIDs enable the session loader to patch the message chain at read time: preserved messages keep their original parentUuids on disk, and the loader uses boundary metadata to link them correctly. This mostly-append design means compaction does not modify or delete previously written transcript lines.

Compact_boundary 标记经过精心设计，可与持久性一起使用。 annotateBoundaryWithPreservedSegment() 函数 (compact.ts) 记录边界事件中的 headUuid、anchorUuid 和 tailUuid。这些 UUID 使会话加载器能够在读取时修补消息链：保留的消息将其原始父 Uuid 保留在磁盘上，并且加载器使用边界元数据正确链接它们。这种主要是附加的设计意味着压缩不会修改或删除先前写入的转录本行。

The “checkpoints” in Claude Code are file-history checkpoints for --rewind-files, stored at ~/.claude/file- history/<sessionId>/. These are file-level snapshots for reverting filesystem changes, not a generic checkpoint store.

Claude Code 中的“检查点”是 --rewind-files 的文件历史记录检查点，存储在 ~/.claude/file-history/<sessionId>/ 中。这些是用于恢复文件系统更改的文件级快照，而不是通用检查点存储。

The preceding sections have documented Claude Code’s answers to recurring design questions. The next section contrasts Claude Code’s design choices with those of two architecturally independent AI agent systems.

前面的部分记录了 Claude Code 对重复出现的设计问题的回答。下一节将 Claude Code 的设计选择与两个架构上独立的人工智能代理系统的设计选择进行对比。

10

10

Comparative Analysis: Claude Code, OpenClaw, and Hermes Agent

对比分析：Claude Code、OpenClaw 和 Hermes Agent

The preceding sections documented Claude Code’s answers to recurring design questions about loop architecture, safety, extensibility, context management, delegation, and persistence. To situate these findings within the broader agent design space, this section compares Claude Code with two independent open-source AI agent systems that answer many of the same design questions from fundamentally different starting points.

前面的部分记录了 Claude Code 对有关循环架构、安全性、可扩展性、上下文管理、委托和持久性的反复出现的设计问题的回答。为了将这些发现置于更广泛的代理设计空间中，本节将 Claude Code 与两个独立的开源人工智能代理系统进行比较，这两个系统从根本不同的起点回答了许多相同的设计问题。

OpenClaw is a local-first WebSocket gateway that connects messaging surfaces (WhatsApp, Telegram, Slack, Discord, Signal, and others) to an embedded agent runtime, with companion apps on macOS, iOS, and Android (Steinberger and OpenClaw Contributors, 2026). Hermes Agent is a single Python process whose role is set by the entry point under which it was invoked, with one persistence layer and many surfaces fronting one runtime (Nous Research, 2026).4 The Hermes distribution exposes three console scripts: hermes for the CLI and gateway controller, hermes-agent for batch runs, and hermes-acp for the Agent Client Protocol adapter that lets external IDEs host Hermes the way Hermes’s own gateway can host other agents. Where Claude Code is a CLI coding harness bound to a single repository session, OpenClaw is a persistent control plane for multi-channel personal assistance, and Hermes is one process whose role and surface set are determined by which entry point launched it. The three systems occupy different regions of the agent design space. The value of the comparison lies in showing how the same recurring questions produce different architectural answers when the deployment context changes.

OpenClaw 是本地优先的 WebSocket 网关，可将消息传递界面（WhatsApp、Telegram、Slack、Discord、Signal 等）连接到嵌入式代理运行时，以及 macOS、iOS 和 Android 上的配套应用程序（Steinberger 和 OpenClaw Contributors，2026）。 Hermes Agent 是一个单一的 Python 进程，其角色由调用它的入口点设置，具有一个持久层和多个面向一个运行时的表面（Nous Research，2026）。4 Hermes 发行版公开了三个控制台脚本：用于 CLI 和网关控制器的 Hermes、用于批量运行的 Hermes-agent 以及用于代理客户端协议适配器的 Hermes-acp，该适配器允许外部 IDE 以 Hermes 自己的网关托管其他代理的方式托管 Hermes。其中，Claude Code 是绑定到单个存储库会话的 CLI 编码harness，OpenClaw 是用于多渠道个人协助的持久控制平面，而 Hermes 是一个进程，其角色和表面集由启动它的入口点决定。这三个系统占据代理设计空间的不同区域。比较的价值在于展示当部署上下文发生变化时，相同的重复问题如何产生不同的架构答案。

10.1

10.1

Six Comparison Dimensions

六个比较维度

Table 3 summarizes the comparison across six dimensions. Each dimension corresponds to a design question that all three systems must answer. Hermes capabilities that fall outside these six dimensions are discussed separately in Section 10.2.

表 3 总结了六个维度的比较。每个维度都对应一个所有三个系统都必须回答的设计问题。不属于这六个维度的 Hermes 功能将在 10.2 节中单独讨论。

System scope and deployment model.

系统范围和部署模型。

Claude Code runs as an ephemeral CLI process bound to a single

Claude Code 作为绑定到单个进程的临时 CLI 进程运行

repository. Each session starts and ends with the terminal. OpenClaw runs as a persistent daemon (default port 18789, loopback-only) that owns all messaging surface connections and coordinates clients, tools, and device nodes over a typed WebSocket protocol. Hermes Agent runs as a single long-lived Python process whose role is fixed by the entry point used to launch it; its gateway sub-command drives messaging surfaces through a two-tier adapter system (in-tree adapters under gateway/platforms/ plus a plugin tier under plugins/platforms/ for Google Chat, IRC, and Teams), and it persists session and multi-agent-board state to two on-disk SQLite files (state.db and kanban.db) rather than to a typed WebSocket protocol. This places Hermes closer to OpenClaw than to Claude Code in trust topology but closer to Claude Code than to OpenClaw in process topology; the design question of where to put the broker is answered, in Hermes, with no broker at all. These differences in system scope are the most fundamental architectural divergences in the comparison: they determine how every other design question is framed. A compositional relationship also exists across all three: OpenClaw can host Claude Code, OpenAI Codex, and Gemini CLI as external 4Source-grounded reading of Hermes surfaced two cases where the project’s own documentation has drifted from the implementation: SECURITY.md documents an approval-mode taxonomy of on/auto/off while tools/approval.py:721-750 returns manual/smart/off, and both SECURITY.md and AGENTS.md describe a delegation depth default of two while tools/delegate_- tool.py:128 sets MAX_DEPTH = 1. We cite the source rather than the docs.

存储库。每个会话都以终端开始和结束。 OpenClaw 作为持久守护进程运行（默认端口 18789，仅环回），拥有所有消息传递表面连接并通过类型化 WebSocket 协议协调客户端、工具和设备节点。 Hermes Agent 作为单个长期 Python 进程运行，其角色由用于启动它的入口点固定；它的 gateway 子命令通过两层适配器系统（网关/平台/下的树内适配器以及用于 Google Chat、IRC 和 Teams 的插件/平台/下的插件层）驱动消息传递表面，并且它将会话和多代理板状态保留到两个磁盘上的 SQLite 文件（state.db 和 kanban.db），而不是类型化的 WebSocket 协议。这使得 Hermes 在信任拓扑中比 Claude Code 更接近 OpenClaw，但在进程拓扑中比 OpenClaw 更接近 Claude Code；在 Hermes 中，根本没有经纪人就解决了在哪里放置经纪人的设计问题。系统范围的这些差异是比较中最基本的架构差异：它们决定了其他所有设计问题的框架方式。这三者之间也存在组合关系：OpenClaw 可以托管 Claude Code、OpenAI Codex 和 Gemini CLI，因为 Hermes 的外部 4Source 基础阅读出现了两种情况，其中项目自己的文档偏离了实现：SECURITY.md 记录了 on/auto/off 的审批模式分类法，而 tools/approval.py:721-750 返回手动/智能/关闭，并且 SECURITY.md 和AGENTS.md 描述了默认为 2 的委派深度，而 tools/delegate_- tool.py:128 设置 MAX_DEPTH = 1。我们引用源代码而不是文档。
