with token-budget tail protection and a tool-output prune pass; summary prefixed with a “reference only” preamble; pre-injection scan against context files (AGENTS.md, .cursorrules, SOUL.md) for injection patterns and invisible Unicode; persistent MEMORY.md, USER.md remain authoritative across compaction Multi-agent and routing Task-delegating subagents (e.g., Explore, Plan, general-purpose); worktree isolation; final response text returned to parent Two separate concerns: (a) multi-agent routing with isolated agents, distinct workspaces, and binding-based channel dispatch; (b) sub-agent delegation with configurable nesting depth (max 5, default 1, recommended 2) and thread-bound sessions delegate_task spawns child AIAgent in a thread pool; concurrency cap 3 children default, depth cap 1 (clamped to [1,3]); leaf children cannot delegate by default; children run with persistent memory disabled; Kanban subsystem (SQLite-backed work queue) with stale-claim reclamation for multi-process worker coordination coding harnesses through its ACP (Agent Client Protocol) integration, and Hermes Agent sits on both sides of the ACP host/guest split, with its gateway hosting platform adapters and tool servers while its hermes-acp adapter lets external IDEs host Hermes, making the systems stackable rather than purely alternative.

具有代币预算尾部保护和工具输出修剪通道；摘要以“仅供参考”序言为前缀；针对上下文文件（AGENTS.md、.cursorrules、SOUL.md）进行预注入扫描，以获取注入模式和不可见的 Unicode；持久 MEMORY.md、USER.md 在压缩多代理和路由任务委派subagent（例如探索、计划、通用）方面保持权威；工作树隔离；返回给父级的最终响应文本 两个独立的问题：(a) 具有独立代理、不同工作区和基于绑定的通道调度的多代理路由； (b) 具有可配置嵌套深度（最大 5，默认 1，建议 2）的subagent委托和线程绑定会话 delegate_task 在线程池中生成子 AIAgent；默认子级并发上限为 3，深度上限为 1（限制为 [1,3]）；默认情况下，叶子孩子不能委托；孩子们跑步时持久记忆被禁用；看板子系统（SQLite 支持的工作队列）通过其 ACP（代理客户端协议）集成，为多进程工作协调编码harness提供过时声明回收，Hermes 代理位于 ACP 主机/来宾分割的两侧，其网关托管平台适配器和工具服务器，而其 hermes-acp 适配器允许外部 IDE 托管 Hermes，使系统可堆叠而不是纯粹的替代。

Trust model and security architecture.

信任模型和安全架构。

The three systems address different threat models. Claude Code

这三个系统针对不同的威胁模型。Claude·科德

assumes an untrusted model operating within a trusted developer’s machine: the deny-first permission 26 system (Section 5) evaluates every tool invocation, the ML classifier provides automated safety assessment, and seven permission modes create a graduated autonomy spectrum. OpenClaw assumes a single trusted operator per gateway instance. Its security architecture begins with identity and access control (DM pairing codes, sender allowlists, gateway authentication) rather than per-action safety classification. Tool policy uses configurable allow/deny lists per agent rather than a centralized classifier. Sandboxing is available as an opt-in feature with multiple backends (Docker, SSH, or OpenShell) and configurable scope (per-agent, per-session, or shared); a non-main mode can sandbox all non-main sessions when enabled, though sandboxing is not active by default. The OpenClaw security documentation explicitly states that hostile multi-tenant isolation on a shared gateway is not a supported security boundary. Hermes Agent takes a third position. Tool authorization is centralized in one file (tools/approval.py), exposing three modes (manual, smart, off) and an unconditional HARDLINE_PATTERNS floor that blocks rm -rf /, mkfs, raw dd to block devices, fork bombs, and system shutdown commands regardless of mode Tier B. The smart mode runs an auxiliary-LLM risk assessment; an in-source comment names OpenAI Codex’s smart approvals as the design inspiration.

假设在受信任的开发人员机器中运行不受信任的模型：拒绝优先权限 26 系统（第 5 节）评估每个工具调用，ML 分类器提供自动安全评估，七种权限模式创建分级自治范围。 OpenClaw 假定每个网关实例都有一个受信任的操作员。其安全架构始于身份和访问控制（DM 配对码、发件人白名单、网关身份验证），而不是按操作进行安全分类。工具策略使用每个代理的可配置允许/拒绝列表，而不是集中式分类器。沙箱作为一项可选功能提供，具有多个后端（Docker、SSH 或 OpenShell）和可配置范围（每个代理、每个会话或共享）；启用后，非主模式可以对所有非主会话进行沙箱处理，但默认情况下沙箱处理未处于活动状态。 OpenClaw 安全文档明确指出共享网关上的敌对多租户隔离不是受支持的安全边界。爱马仕代理占据第三位。工具授权集中在一个文件（tools/approval.py）中，公开三种模式（手动、智能、关闭）和无条件的 HARDLINE_PATTERNS 层，该层可阻止 rm -rf /、mkfs、原始 dd 来阻止设备、fork 炸弹和系统关闭命令，无论模式 B 层如何。智能模式运行辅助 LLM 风险评估；一条源内评论将 OpenAI Codex 的智能批准称为设计灵感。

Because Hermes runs across many surfaces, the same approval flow is rendered four times: a CLI prompt, gateway approval keyboards on Telegram, Discord, and QQBot, an ACP request_permission round-trip for IDE clients, and a cron-only mode for unattended runs. Compared with Claude Code’s seven-mode taxonomy and ML-classifier-driven auto mode, Hermes has a smaller mode taxonomy but the same multi-layer structure:

由于 Hermes 运行在多个界面上，因此相同的批准流程会呈现四次：CLI 提示、Telegram、Discord 和 QQBot 上的网关批准键盘、IDE 客户端的 ACP request_permission 往返以及用于无人值守运行的仅 cron 模式。与 Claude Code 的七模式分类法和 ML 分类器驱动的自动模式相比，Hermes 的模式分类法更小，但多层结构相同：

unconditional safety floor, default interactive prompt, opt-in auxiliary-LLM “smart” path. The architectural answer is the same; the surface count differs. The difference across the three reflects where the trust boundary sits: Claude Code places it between the model and the execution environment, OpenClaw places it at the gateway perimeter, and Hermes sits between the two with per-action approvals like Claude Code but rendered through many surfaces like OpenClaw.

无条件安全底线、默认交互提示、选择辅助LLM“智能”路径。架构上的答案是相同的；表面计数不同。三者之间的差异反映了信任边界所在的位置：Claude Code 将其放置在模型和执行环境之间，OpenClaw 将其放置在网关周边，而 Hermes 则位于两者之间，像 Claude Code 一样具有每个操作的批准，但通过 OpenClaw 等许多表面进行渲染。

Agent runtime and tool orchestration.

代理运行时和工具编排。

All three systems implement agentic loops, but the loops occupy

所有三个系统都实现代理循环，但循环占用

different positions in their respective architectures.

在各自的架构中处于不同的位置。

In Claude Code, the queryLoop() async generator

在 Claude Code 中，queryLoop() 异步生成器

(Section 4) is the system’s center: all interfaces feed into it, and it directly manages context assembly, model calls, tool dispatch, and recovery. In OpenClaw, the agent runtime is an embedded pi-coding-agent core (an external SDK from the pi-mono project that OpenClaw integrates), sitting inside a larger gateway dispatch layer. The gateway’s agent RPC validates parameters, resolves sessions, and returns immediately; the embedded runner then executes the agentic loop while emitting lifecycle and stream events back through the gateway protocol. Runs are serialized through per-session queues and an optional global lane, preventing tool and session races across the multi-channel surface. In Hermes Agent, the loop is a synchronous while inside AIAgent.run_conversation (run_agent.py), gated on an iteration count (default 90) and a separate iteration budget; when the budget is exhausted, a single tool-stripped summary call lets the agent deliver a closing message rather than terminate mid-thought Tier B; each iteration sends tool schemas collected by model_tools.get_tool_definitions() and routes any tool calls through model_tools.handle_function_- call, with tool files in tools/*.py self-registering at import time so that adding a tool requires only one registry.register() call, and a single assistant message returning multiple tool calls is executed in parallel with a sequential fallback. All three systems follow the ReAct pattern (Yao et al., 2022), with OpenClaw’s loop a component within a control plane rather than the control plane itself, and Hermes’s loop a peer to Claude Code’s at the runtime level but shape-different in implementation, a synchronous Python loop with thread-managed concurrency rather than an async generator that yields.

（第4节）是系统的中心：所有接口都输入其中，它直接管理上下文组装、模型调用、工具调度和恢复。在 OpenClaw 中，代理运行时是一个嵌入式 pi-coding-agent 核心（来自 OpenClaw 集成的 pi-mono 项目的外部 SDK），位于更大的网关调度层内。网关的代理RPC验证参数、解析会话并立即返回；然后，嵌入式运行器执行代理循环，同时通过网关协议发出生命周期和流事件。运行通过每个会话队列和可选的全局通道进行序列化，从而防止多通道表面上的工具和会话竞争。在 Hermes Agent 中，循环在 AIAgent.run_conversation (run_agent.py) 内是同步的，根据迭代计数（默认 90）和单独的迭代预算进行门控；当预算耗尽时，单个工具剥离的摘要调用可以让代理传递结束消息，而不是终止思考中的 B 层；每次迭代都会发送由 model_tools.get_tool_definitions() 收集的工具模式，并通过 model_tools.handle_function_- 调用路由任何工具调用，tools/*.py 中的工具文件会在导入时进行自注册，以便添加工具只需要一次registry.register() 调用，并且返回多个工具调用的单个辅助消息与顺序回退并行执行。所有三个系统都遵循 ReAct 模式（Yao 等人，2022），OpenClaw 的循环是控制平面内的组件，而不是控制平面本身，Hermes 的循环在运行时级别与 Claude Code 的循环相同，但在实现上有所不同，是具有线程管理并发性的同步 Python 循环，而不是产生的异步生成器。

Extension architecture.

扩展架构。

Claude Code’s four extension mechanisms (MCP, plugins, skills, hooks) are organized

Claude Code 的四种扩展机制（MCP、插件、技能、钩子）整理

by context cost (Section 6): hooks consume zero context, skills consume low context, and MCP servers consume high context once their tool schemas load.

按上下文成本（第 6 节）：钩子消耗零上下文，技能消耗低上下文，MCP 服务器在工具模式加载后消耗高上下文。

All four extend a single agent’s context window

所有四个扩展单个代理的上下文窗口

and tool surface. OpenClaw uses a manifest-first plugin system with four architectural layers (discovery, enablement, runtime loading, surface consumption) and twelve capability types including text inference, speech, media understanding, image/music/video generation, web search, and messaging channels. Plugins register capabilities into a central registry; the gateway reads the registry to expose tools, channels, provider setup, hooks, HTTP routes, CLI commands, and services. OpenClaw also has a separate skills layer with multiple sources (workspace, project-level, personal, managed, bundled, and extra directories, with workspace skills taking highest precedence) plus a public registry (ClawHub) and supports MCP through built-in openclaw mcp commands (server and outbound client registry). Hermes Agent exposes five extension surfaces against Claude 27 Code’s four Tier B: three sit at the same level as Claude Code’s (general plugins under plugins/<name>/ with lifecycle hooks, bundled skills under skills/, and MCP servers configured in config.yaml), while two extra surfaces extend a different axis (pluggable memory providers under plugins/memory/<name>/ and pluggable model providers under plugins/model-providers/<name>/) that let Hermes swap backends rather than intercept events. Hooks in Hermes can be in-process Python callbacks or config-driven external shell commands, both dispatched through the same hook manager. When Hermes spawns a stdio MCP server, the child’s environment is restricted to a small allowlist of system variables before launch, and npx/uvx package names are screened against the OSV malware database. The architectural difference across the three is where the extension acts: Claude Code’s extensions modify one agent’s action surface, OpenClaw’s plugins extend the gateway’s capability surface across all agents, and Hermes’s extensions add a second axis on top of a Claude-Code-style per-agent surface, swapping entire memory and model-provider backends in place rather than intercepting events around them.

和工具表面。 OpenClaw 使用清单优先的插件系统，具有四个架构层（发现、启用、运行时加载、表面消耗）和十二种功能类型，包括文本推理、语音、媒体理解、图像/音乐/视频生成、网络搜索和消息传递通道。插件将功能注册到中央注册表中；网关读取注册表以公开工具、通道、提供程序设置、挂钩、HTTP 路由、CLI 命令和服务。 OpenClaw 还有一个单独的技能层，具有多个来源（工作区、项目级、个人、托管、捆绑和额外目录，其中工作区技能具有最高优先级）以及公共注册表 (ClawHub)，并通过内置 openclaw mcp 命令（服务器和出站客户端注册表）支持 MCP。 Hermes Agent 针对 Claude 27 Code 的四个 B 层公开了五个扩展表面：其中三个与 Claude Code 处于同一级别（plugins/<name>/ 下的通用插件，带有生命周期挂钩，skills/ 下的捆绑技能，以及 config.yaml 中配置的 MCP 服务器），而两个额外的表面扩展了不同的轴（plugins/memory/<name>/ 下的可插拔内存提供程序和在plugins/model-providers/<name>/) 让 Hermes 交换后端而不是拦截事件。 Hermes 中的钩子可以是进程内的 Python 回调或配置驱动的外部 shell 命令，两者都通过相同的钩子管理器调度。当 Hermes 生成 std​​io MCP 服务器时，子进程的环境在启动前被限制在一小部分系统变量允许列表中，并且 npx/uvx 包名称会根据 OSV 恶意软件数据库进行筛选。三者之间的架构差异在于扩展的作用位置：Claude Code 的扩展修改一个代理的操作面，OpenClaw 的插件将网关的功能面扩展到所有代理，Hermes 的扩展在 Claude-Code 风格的每个代理表面上添加第二个轴，交换整个内存和模型提供程序后端，而不是拦截它们周围的事件。

Memory, context, and knowledge management.

记忆、情境和知识管理。

All three systems use transparent file-based memory rather

所有三个系统都使用透明的基于文件的内存，而不是

than opaque databases. Claude Code loads a four-level CLAUDE.md hierarchy and manages context pressure through a five-layer compaction pipeline (Section 7). Memory retrieval uses an LLM-based scan of file headers. OpenClaw injects workspace bootstrap files into the system prompt at session start: five core files (AGENTS.md, SOUL.md, TOOLS.md, IDENTITY.md, USER.md) plus conditionally BOOTSTRAP.md, HEARTBEAT.md, and MEMORY.md, with large files truncated. Separately, the memory system manages three file types: MEMORY.md for long-term durable facts, date-stamped daily notes (memory/YYYY-MM- DD.md), and an optional DREAMS.md for dreaming sweep summaries. When an embedding provider is configured, memory search uses hybrid retrieval combining vector similarity with keyword matching. An experimental dreaming system performs background consolidation, scoring candidates and promoting only qualified items from short-term recall into long-term memory. Before compaction, OpenClaw automatically reminds the agent to save important notes to memory files, preventing context loss. Compaction in Hermes is one auxiliary-LLM summarizer with token-budget tail protection, preceded by a tool-output prune pass; the summary is prefixed with a long “reference only” preamble that tells the agent the compaction is background context, not new instructions, and that persistent memory (MEMORY.md, USER.md) remains authoritative Tier B. Where Claude Code’s CLAUDE.md hierarchy is fixed and its compaction has five layers, Hermes has fewer compaction layers but adds a context-file pre-injection scan: files such as AGENTS.md, .cursorrules, and SOUL.md are checked against a list of injection patterns and invisible Unicode characters, and any hit replaces the file’s content with a [BLOCKED: ...] marker. All three share the design commitment to user-visible, editable memory but give different answers to the same context-management question: Claude Code invests in graduated context compression (five layers with cache awareness); OpenClaw invests in structured long-term memory promotion (dreaming, daily notes, memory search) plus pluggable compaction providers and session pruning, though its compaction pipeline is less graduated than Claude Code’s five-layer

比不透明的数据库。 Claude Code 加载四级 CLAUDE.md 层次结构，并通过五层压缩管道管理上下文压力（第 7 节）。内存检索使用基于 LLM 的文件头扫描。 OpenClaw 在会话启动时将工作区引导文件注入到系统提示符中：五个核心文件（AGENTS.md、SOUL.md、TOOLS.md、IDENTITY.md、USER.md）以及有条件的 BOOTSTRAP.md、HEARTBEAT.md 和 MEMORY.md，其中大文件被截断。内存系统单独管理三种文件类型：用于长期持久事实的 MEMORY.md、带日期标记的日常笔记 (内存/YYYY-MM-DD.md) 以及用于梦想扫描摘要的可选 DREAMS.md。配置嵌入提供程序后，内存搜索将使用向量相似性与关键字匹配相结合的混合检索。实验性的做梦系统会进行背景巩固，对候选者进行评分，并仅将合格的项目从短期回忆提升到长期记忆中。在压缩之前，OpenClaw 会自动提醒代理将重要注释保存到内存文件中，以防止上下文丢失。 Hermes 中的压缩是一种辅助 LLM 总结器，具有token预算尾部保护，之前是工具输出修剪过程；摘要以一个长的“仅供参考”前导码为前缀，告诉代理压缩是后台上下文，而不是新指令，并且持久内存（MEMORY.md、USER.md）仍然是权威的 Tier B。 Claude Code 的 CLAUDE.md 层次结构是固定的，其压缩有五层，Hermes 的压缩层较少，但添加了上下文文件预注入扫描：诸如 AGENTS.md 之类的文件， .cursorrules 和 SOUL.md 会根据注入模式和不可见 Unicode 字符列表进行检查，任何命中都会用 [BLOCKED: ...] 标记替换文件的内容。这三者都致力于用户可见、可编辑的内存，但对同一上下文管理问题给出了不同的答案：Claude Code 投资于分级上下文压缩（具有缓存感知功能的五层）； OpenClaw 投资于结构化长期记忆提升（做梦、每日笔记、记忆搜索）以及可插拔压缩提供程序和会话修剪，尽管其压缩管道的等级不如 Claude Code 的五层

system; Hermes invests in pre-injection content scanning and prompt-level isolation of persistent memory

系统； Hermes 投资于注入前内容扫描和持久内存的提示级隔离

from compacted history rather than a deeper compaction stack.

Multi-agent architecture and routing.

多代理架构和路由。

This dimension reveals the starkest architectural difference. Claude

这个维度揭示了最明显的架构差异。克洛德

Code’s multi-agent model is task delegation: the parent spawns subagents (Explore, Plan, general-purpose, and custom types) that operate in isolated context windows with restricted tool sets and return summary-only results (Section 8). Worktree isolation provides filesystem-level separation. OpenClaw separates two distinct concerns. First, multi-agent routing: a single gateway can host multiple fully isolated agents, each with its own workspace, authentication profiles, session store, and model configuration, routed to specific channels or senders via deterministic binding rules. Second, sub-agent delegation: within a single agent, background runs can be spawned with configurable nesting depth (maximum 5, default 1, recommended 2), thread-bound sessions on supported channels, and configurable tool policy by depth. OpenClaw’s project vision explicitly rejects agent-hierarchy frameworks as a default architecture. Hermes Agent splits the question along yet a different seam. Per-turn delegation goes through the delegate_task tool, which spawns child AIAgent instances in a thread pool; the parent blocks until children return summaries, concurrency is capped at three children by default and depth is capped at one (clamped to the range [1, 3]), leaf children cannot themselves delegate by default (nested delegation is opt-in through orchestrator children and a higher spawn-depth setting), and children always run with persistent memory disabled so they cannot read or write the parent’s 28 notes Tier B. Beyond per-turn delegation, v0.13’s Kanban subsystem is a SQLite-backed work queue where multiple Hermes worker profiles claim, heartbeat, and complete tasks, with stale-claim reclamation and an automatic block after two consecutive failed attempts (hermes_cli/kanban_db.py:2703). The distinction across the three matters because Claude Code’s subagents are subordinate workers within one user’s coding session, OpenClaw’s multi-agent routing creates genuinely independent agent instances serving different users or purposes through different channels, and Hermes combines a strict per-turn delegation default with a separate Kanban layer that lets multiple agent processes coordinate over a durable shared board, a question

代码的多代理模型是任务委托：父代理生成subagent（探索、计划、通用和自定义类型），这些subagent在具有受限工具集的隔离上下文窗口中运行，并返回仅摘要结果（第 8 节）。工作树隔离提供文件系统级分离。 OpenClaw 将两个不同的问题分开。首先，多代理路由：单个网关可以托管多个完全隔离的代理，每个代理都有自己的工作区、身份验证配置文件、会话存储和模型配置，通过确定性绑定规则路由到特定通道或发送者。其次，subagent委派：在单个代理内，可以使用可配置的嵌套深度（最大 5，默认 1，建议 2）、受支持通道上的线程绑定会话以及按深度配置的工具策略来生成后台运行。 OpenClaw 的项目愿景明确拒绝将代理层次结构框架作为默认架构。赫尔墨斯特工将这个问题分成了不同的方向。每轮委托通过 delegate_task 工具，该工具在线程池中生成子 AIAgent 实例；父级会阻塞，直到子级返回摘要，默认情况下，并发上限为 3 个子级，深度上限为 1（限制在范围 [1, 3]），默认情况下，叶级子级不能自行委派（嵌套委派通过协调器子级和更高的生成深度设置选择加入），并且子级始终在禁用持久内存的情况下运行，因此它们无法读取或写入父级的 28 个注释 B 层。除了每回合委派之外，v0.13 的看板子系统是一个SQLite 支持的工作队列，其中多个 Hermes 工作配置文件声明、心跳和完成任务，并在两次连续失败尝试后进行陈旧声明回收和自动阻止 (hermes_cli/kanban_db.py:2703)。这三个问题之间的区别是因为 Claude Code 的subagent是一个用户编码会话中的从属工作人员，OpenClaw 的多代理路由创建了真正独立的代理实例，通过不同的渠道为不同的用户或目的服务，而 Hermes 将严格的每轮委派默认值与单独的看板层相结合，让多个代理进程在持久的共享板上进行协调，这是一个问题

Claude Code and OpenClaw do not ask in the same form.

Claude Code 和 OpenClaw 不以相同的形式提出要求。

10.2

10.2

Hermes Agent: Capabilities Beyond the Comparison Table

爱马仕代理：超越对照表的能力

Session persistence in Hermes uses one WAL-mode SQLite database with full-text search across all session messages and a parent-session chain that records compaction-triggered splits Tier B. Where Claude Code persists sessions to JSONL files per project (Section 9), Hermes’s choice provides cross-session search and concurrent-reader support out of the box. When the gateway restarts mid-conversation, sessions auto-resume on next message arrival, driven by transcript-timestamp freshness logic with a one-hour default window. A built-in cron scheduler and webhook subscription system let the agent run unattended on schedule expressions or external HTTP events; cron sessions run with persistent memory disabled and a configurable inactivity timeout that defaults to 600 seconds. The distribution also includes a background “curator” loop that reviews agent-created skills, archives stale ones, and never deletes, gated to skills with agent provenance so bundled and hub-installed skills are off-limits. None of these capabilities map cleanly onto the six comparison dimensions used in the main table, which is why they appear here as a Hermes-specific addendum rather than as cells in Table 3. OpenClaw’s distinctive capabilities (the ClawHub plugin registry under extension architecture and the dreaming memory subsystem under memory and context) already occupy cells in those six dimensions and so do not require a parallel addendum. The Hermes additions in this subsection follow from its persistent-process scope, a deployment-context property Hermes shares with OpenClaw but that Claude Code’s per-session CLI model does not provide.

Hermes 中的会话持久性使用一个 WAL 模式 SQLite 数据库，对所有会话消息进行全文搜索，并使用一个记录压缩触发的拆分 B 层的父会话链。Claude Code 将会话持久保存到每个项目的 JSONL 文件（第 9 节），而 Hermes 的选择提供了开箱即用的跨会话搜索和并发读取器支持。当网关在对话中重新启动时，会话将在下一条消息到达时自动恢复，由转录时间戳新鲜度逻辑驱动，默认窗口为一小时。内置的 cron 调度程序和 webhook 订阅系统让代理可以在无人值守的情况下按计划表达式或外部 HTTP 事件运行； cron 会话在禁用持久内存和可配置的非活动超时（默认为 600 秒）的情况下运行。该发行版还包括一个后台“管理者”循环，用于审查代理创建的技能、存档过时的技能，并且从不删除，与代理出处的技能相关联，因此捆绑和集线器安装的技能是禁止的。这些功能都没有清晰地映射到主表中使用的六个比较维度，这就是为什么它们在这里显示为 Hermes 特定的附录，而不是表 3 中的单元格。OpenClaw 的独特功能（扩展架构下的 ClawHub 插件注册表以及内存和上下文下的梦想内存子系统）已经占据了这六个维度中的单元格，因此不需要并行附录。本小节中的 Hermes 添加内容源自其持久进程范围，这是 Hermes 与 OpenClaw 共享的部署上下文属性，但 Claude Code 的每会话 CLI 模型不提供该属性。

10.3

10.3

What the Contrast Reveals

对比揭示了什么

The comparison surfaces three observations about the design space of AI agent systems.

通过比较，我们得出了关于人工智能代理系统设计空间的三个观察结果。

First, the recurring design questions identified in Section 3.1 (where reasoning lives, what safety posture to adopt, how to manage context, how to structure extensibility) apply beyond coding agents. OpenClaw answers every one of these questions from the starting point of a multi-channel personal assistant rather than a repository-bound coding tool, and Hermes Agent answers them from a single-process, multi-surface deployment rather than a CLI harness or a multi-channel gateway. The questions are stable; the answers vary with deployment context across the three systems.

首先，第 3.1 节中确定的反复出现的设计问题（推理存在于何处、采用什么安全姿势、如何管理上下文、如何构建可扩展性）适用于coding agent之外。 OpenClaw 从多通道个人助理而不是存储库绑定的编码工具的起点回答了每一个问题，而 Hermes Agent 从单进程、多表面部署而不是 CLI harness或多通道网关回答了这些问题。问题稳定；答案因三个系统的部署环境而异。

Second, the systems make different bets along several dimensions. Claude Code invests in graduated per-action safety evaluation; OpenClaw invests in perimeter-level identity and access control; Hermes sits between the two, with per-action approvals like Claude Code rendered through many surfaces like OpenClaw. Claude Code treats the agent loop as the architectural center; OpenClaw treats the gateway control plane as the center and embeds the agent loop as one component; Hermes’s loop is a peer to Claude Code’s at the runtime level, occupying the same architectural position rather than sitting inside a gateway dispatch layer. Claude Code’s extensions modify a single context window; OpenClaw’s plugins extend a shared gateway surface; Hermes adds a second axis on top of a Claude-Code-style per-agent surface, swapping memory and model backends in place rather than intercepting events. These differences are not arbitrary: they follow from the three systems’ different trust models and deployment topologies.

其次，系统在几个维度上做出不同的赌注。 Claude Code 投资于分级的每次操作安全评估； OpenClaw 投资于外围级别的身​​份和访问控制； Hermes 位于两者之间，通过 OpenClaw 等许多表面呈现 Claude Code 等每次操作批准。 Claude Code 将代理循环视为架构中心； OpenClaw以网关控制平面为中心，将代理循环作为一个组件嵌入； Hermes 的循环在运行时级别与 Claude Code 的循环类似，占据相同的架构位置，而不是位于网关调度层内。 Claude Code 的扩展修改了单个上下文窗口； OpenClaw 的插件扩展了共享网关表面； Hermes 在 Claude-Code 风格的每个代理表面上添加了第二个轴，交换内存和模型后端，而不是拦截事件。这些差异并不是任意的：它们源于三个系统不同的信任模型和部署拓扑。

Third, the compositional relationships across the three systems are architecturally significant. OpenClaw can host Claude Code as an external coding harness via ACP, and Hermes Agent sits on both sides of the host/guest split: its gateway hosts platform adapters and tool servers, and its hermes-acp adapter lets external IDEs host Hermes. The systems are composable rather than exclusive alternatives, which suggests that the design space of AI agents is not a flat taxonomy but a layered one, where gateway-level systems and task-level harnesses can compose at multiple positions.

第三，三个系统之间的组合关系在架构上具有重要意义。 OpenClaw 可以通过 ACP 将 Claude Code 作为外部编码harness托管，而 Hermes Agent 位于主机/来宾分割的两侧：其网关托管平台适配器和工具服务器，其 hermes-acp 适配器允许外部 IDE 托管 Hermes。这些系统是可组合的，而不是排他性的替代方案，这表明人工智能代理的设计空间不是平面分类法，而是分层的，其中网关级系统和任务级harness可以在多个位置组合。

Related Work

相关工作

11.1

11.1

Coding Agent Taxonomy

coding agent分类

AI coding tools can be organized by the degree of autonomous action they support (Table 4). Inline completion tools such as GitHub Copilot (Chen et al., 2021) suggest code fragments within the editor without autonomous action. Chat-integrated products including Cursor and Windsurf add conversational interaction and multi-file edits but remain coupled to the IDE environment. Agentic CLI tools, including Claude Code, OpenAI’s Codex CLI, and Aider (Gauthier, 2024), operate from the command line and can autonomously execute shell commands, read and write files, and iterate on outputs within a single request. Fully autonomous systems like Devin, SWE-Agent (Yang et al., 2024), and OpenHands (Wang et al., 2024b) aim for minimal human supervision, often in sandboxed cloud environments.

AI 编码工具可以根据它们支持的自主操作程度进行组织（表 4）。 GitHub Copilot（Chen 等人，2021）等内联补全工具建议编辑器内的代码片段，无需自主操作。包括 Cursor 和 Windsurf 在内的聊天集成产品添加了对话交互和多文件编辑，但仍与 IDE 环境耦合。 Agentic CLI 工具，包括 Claude Code、OpenAI 的 Codex CLI 和 Aider（Gauthier，2024），从命令行运行，可以自主执行 shell 命令、读写文件以及在单个请求中迭代输出。 Devin、SWE-Agent（Yang 等人，2024）和 OpenHands（Wang 等人，2024b）等完全自主的系统旨在实现最少的人工监督，通常在沙盒云环境中。

Claude Code shares features with higher-autonomy agents (auto-mode classifier, background agent execution, remote environments) but retains interactive approval by default. Evaluation benchmarks such as SWE-Bench (Jimenez et al., 2023) and HumanEval (Chen et al., 2021) have driven much of the academic focus on coding agents. This paper examines Claude Code’s internal architecture from source code.

Claude Code 与更高自主权的代理共享功能（自动模式分类器、后台代理执行、远程环境），但默认保留交互式批准。 SWE-Bench（Jimenez 等人，2023）和 HumanEval（Chen 等人，2021）等评估基准推动了学术界对coding agent的关注。本文从源代码角度考察了 Claude Code 的内部架构。

11.2

11.2

Agent Architecture Patterns

代理架构模式

Claude Code’s core loop follows the ReAct pattern (Yao et al., 2022): the model generates reasoning and tool invocations, the harness executes actions, and results feed the next iteration. Toolformer (Schick et al., 2023) demonstrated that language models can learn to use tools. Claude Code uses up to 54 built-in tools and a layered permission system. The broader design space has been mapped by several surveys. Weng (2023) offered the now-standard decomposition into planning, memory, and tool use, and Wang et al. (2024a) catalogued early autonomous-agent work. Xu (2026) frames the field around three recurring trade-offs (autonomy vs.

Claude Code 的核心循环遵循 ReAct 模式（Yao 等人，2022）：模型生成推理和工具调用，harness执行操作，并将结果提供给下一次迭代。 Toolformer（Schick 等人，2023）证明了语言模型可以学习使用工具。 Claude Code 使用多达 54 个内置工具和分层权限系统。多项调查已经绘制了更广泛的设计空间。 Weng（2023）提供了对规划、记忆和工具使用的现在标准分解，Wang 等人。 (2024a) 对早期的自主代理工作进行了分类。 Xu（2026）围绕三个反复出现的权衡（自治与自治）构建了该领域。

controllability, latency vs. accuracy, capability vs. reliability) that recur throughout our analysis, and Hu et al.

可控性、延迟与准确性、能力与可靠性）在我们的分析中反复出现，Hu 等人。

(2024) casts agent design itself as a search problem over components, algorithms, and evaluation functions.

（2024）将代理设计本身视为组件、算法和评估函数的搜索问题。

This paper characterizes one specific point in that space.

本文描述了该领域的一个具体点。

A recent survey on code as agent harness frames code not only as model output but as the substrate for reasoning, action, environment modeling, and execution-based verification (Ning et al., 2026). That framing matches this paper’s focus on the harness around the model rather than on benchmark scores alone.

最近一项关于代码作为代理harness的调查将代码不仅作为模型输出，而且作为推理、动作、环境建模和基于执行的验证的基础（Ning 等人，2026）。该框架与本文对模型harness的关注相匹配，而不仅仅是基准分数。

Recent runtime mechanisms.

最近的运行时机制。

Recent surveys and systems make several harness choices explicit. Graph

最近的调查和系统明确了一些harness的选择。图

Engineering separates task dependencies, agent communication, and execution state (Feng et al., 2026), and the Self-Evolving Coding Agents survey organizes persistent changes by their target, timing, and feedback source (Zhou et al., 2026). LoopsBench and LoopArena evaluate how prerequisites, retained regression checks, and a controller’s decisions to check or stop shape a coding loop (Li et al., 2026; Wang et al., 2026a), and Symphony separates issue scheduling from the coding agent’s own loop (OpenAI, 2026b). For recovery, Resume Means Resume tests the checkpoint and resume behavior of workflow frameworks against their stated semantics (Khan, 2026), and Temporal’s Deep Agents integration runs each model call and I/O tool call as a Temporal activity while the control loop replays inside the workflow (Temporal Technologies, 2026).

工程将任务依赖性、代理通信和执行状态分开（Feng 等人，2026），而自进化coding agent调查则根据目标、时间和反馈源组织持续变化（Zhou 等人，2026）。 LoopsBench 和 LoopArena 评估先决条件、保留的回归检查以及控制器检查或停止形成编码循环的决定（Li 等人，2026；Wang 等人，2026a），而 Symphony 将问题调度与coding agent自己的循环分开（OpenAI，2026b）。对于恢复，Resume Means Resume 根据工作流框架声明的语义测试检查点和恢复行为（Khan，2026），Temporal 的深度agent 集成将每个模型调用和 I/O 工具调用作为 Temporal 活动运行，同时控制循环在工作流内重放（Temporal Technologies，2026）。

Living-Harness updates an episodic memory and a state graph between episodes while the model and tools stay fixed (Du et al., 2026). Graphiti and Cognee store entity relations in knowledge graphs (Zep, 2026; Cognee, 2026). APPA checks a tool’s result before admitting it to the agent’s context, separating permission to call a tool from admission of the data it returns (Kravchenko et al., 2026). At the protocol level, the 2026-07-28 30 Table 5 Design space of context management approaches in LLM-based tools.

MCP revision removes protocol-level sessions and lets one tool operation span several requests (Model Context Protocol, 2026b,a), the Agent Host Protocol lets several clients share a host-owned session (Lourens et al., 2026), and A2A connects independent agent systems (A2A Project, 2026). Cursor reports coordinating agent swarms through shared design documents (Lin, 2026).

MCP 修订版删除了协议级会话，并允许一个工具操作跨越多个请求（模型上下文协议，2026b，a），代理主机协议允许多个客户端共享主机拥有的会话（Lourens 等人，2026），A2A 连接独立代理系统（A2A 项目，2026）。 Cursor 通过共享设计文档报告协调代理群（Lin，2026）。

Multi-agent orchestration frameworks such as AutoGen (Wu et al., 2024), LangChain (LangChain, 2026b), and CrewAI (CrewAI, 2026) provide conversation-based agent coordination. Claude Code’s subagent delegation (Section 8) includes permission override precedence, two-level permission scoping, and separate transcript files for each subagent. LATS (Zhou et al., 2023) unifies reasoning, acting, and planning in a tree-search framework. Claude Code’s plan permission mode implements a simpler plan-then-execute approach.

AutoGen (Wu et al., 2024)、LangChain (LangChain, 2026b) 和 CrewAI (CrewAI, 2026) 等多代理编排框架提供基于会话的代理协调。 Claude Code 的subagent委托（第 8 节）包括权限覆盖优先级、两级权限范围以及每个subagent的单独脚本文件。 LATS（Zhou et al., 2023）将推理、行动和规划统一在树搜索框架中。 Claude Code 的计划许可模式实现了一种更简单的计划然后执行的方法。

Practitioner writing has converged on a handful of recurring patterns that Claude Code’s architecture instantiates. Anthropic’s own “Building Effective Agents” (Schluntz and Zhang, 2024) distinguishes agents

从业者的写作集中在 Claude Code 架构实例化的一些重复出现的模式上。 Anthropic 自己的“构建有效代理”（Schluntz 和Zhang，2024）区分了代理

from workflows and argues for simple composable patterns over heavy frameworks. Martin (2026) synthesizes

seven patterns observed in production systems, including giving agents filesystem and shell access as a general-purpose action layer, and discovering actions on demand rather than loading every tool schema upfront.

在生产系统中观察到的七种模式，包括为代理提供文件系统和 shell 访问作为通用操作层，以及按需发现操作而不是预先加载每个工具模式。

Chase (2025) observes that Claude Code’s planning tool is “basically a no-op” whose value lies in keeping the agent on track rather than in performing any external computation. Wang (2025) argues that authority is the element academic frameworks most often leave out, calling trust “the most overlooked element” in production agent design, a gap the permission analysis in Section 5 attempts to close. Huyen (2025) makes the compound-error concern concrete: at 95% per-step accuracy, a 100-step task succeeds only 0.6% of the time, which motivates the per-step verification patterns we trace in Section 4 and Section 5. OpenAI makes a similar point in its own writing on harness engineering, treating the design of the harness itself as a central engineering task (OpenAI, 2026a). Recently, a new concept called loop engineering has also emerged: instead of prompting the agent each turn, the developer builds a loop that finds work, assigns it, checks the result, and decides what comes next, assembled from the same primitives this paper documents, such as skills, MCP servers, and subagents (Osmani, 2026). It lets a single developer direct far more work autonomously and has drawn interest, including from Claude Code’s own team, though reducing the person’s turn-by-turn involvement also sharpens the long-term developer-capability concern that Section 14 returns to.

Chase（2025）观察到 Claude Code 的规划工具“基本上是一个无操作”，其价值在于使代理保持在正轨上，而不是执行任何外部计算。 Wang (2025) 认为，权威是学术框架最常忽略的要素，称信任是生产代理设计中“最被忽视的要素”，第 5 节中的权限分析试图弥补这一差距。 Huyen (2025) 将复合错误问题具体化：在每步精度为 95% 的情况下，100 步任务的成功率仅为 0.6%，这激发了我们在第 4 节和第 5 节中跟踪的每步验证模式。OpenAI 在其自己的harness工程著作中提出了类似的观点，将harness本身的设计视为中心工程任务（OpenAI，2026a）。最近，还出现了一种称为循环工程的新概念：开发人员不是每次都提示代理，而是构建一个循环来查找工作、分配工作、检查结果并决定下一步，该循环由本文记录的相同原语组装而成，例如技能、MCP 服务器和subagent（Osmani，2026）。它让单个开发人员可以自主指导更多的工作，并引起了人们的兴趣，包括 Claude Code 自己的团队，尽管减少个人的逐一参与也加剧了第 14 节所提到的对开发人员能力的长期担忧。

Context management.

上下文管理。

Table 5 presents a design-space taxonomy of context management approaches. Claude

表 5 展示了上下文管理方法的设计空间分类。克洛德

Code’s five-layer compaction pipeline applies multiple strategies at different granularities before escalating, with cache-aware compression and virtual-view-on-read semantics. Zhang et al. (2025a) characterizes two failure modes that this design mitigates (summarization that drops domain details, and detail loss from iterative context rewriting), and instead proposes treating context as an “evolving playbook” that accumulates strategies over time. Claude Code’s approach is consistent with that framing, since the CLAUDE.md hierarchy accumulates structured instructions rather than repeatedly summarizing them. Hu et al. (2025) distinguishes context engineering from agent memory: context engineering handles transient assembly, while memory covers persistent factual knowledge and experiential traces. Claude Code’s architecture separates the two in the same way, pairing a compaction pipeline with a file-based memory hierarchy.

代码的五层压缩管道在升级之前以不同粒度应用多种策略，并具有缓存感知压缩和读取时虚拟视图语义。张等人。 (2025a) 描述了这种设计可以缓解的两种故障模式（丢弃领域细节的摘要以及迭代上下文重写导致的细节丢失），并建议将上下文视为随着时间的推移积累策略的“不断发展的剧本”。 Claude Code 的方法与该框架一致，因为 CLAUDE.md 层次结构累积结构化指令而不是重复总结它们。胡等人。 （2025）将情境工程与代理记忆区分开来：情境工程处理瞬态组装，而记忆则涵盖持久的事实知识和经验痕迹。 Claude Code 的架构以相同的方式将两者分开，将压缩管道与基于文件的内存层次结构配对。

Safety and permissions.

安全和权限。

Production coding agents adopt safety architectures that vary along three axes:

生产coding agent采用沿三个轴变化的安全架构：

approval model (per-action prompting, classifier-mediated automation, or no prompting with post-hoc review), isolation boundary (OS-level container, filesystem sandbox, permission-scoped tool pool, or none), and recovery mechanism (version-control rollback, session-scoped permission reset, or checkpoint-based rewind). SWE- Agent and OpenHands (Yang et al., 2024; Wang et al., 2024b) rely primarily on Docker container isolation, 31 providing environment-level sandboxing that constrains all agent actions. Codex CLI supports sandbox modes and approval policies for shell commands. OpenCode and Cline also provide tool approval controls (Anomaly, 2026; Cline, 2026). Aider (Gauthier, 2024) uses Git as its primary safety mechanism, making all changes reversible through version control. Claude Code combines per-action deny-first rules, an ML-based classifier for automated approval, optional shell sandboxing, and session-scoped permission non-restoration, layering multiple mechanisms rather than relying on a single isolation boundary.

批准模型（每个操作提示、分类器介导的自动化或事后审查无提示）、隔离边界（操作系统级容器、文件系统沙箱、权限范围工具池或无）和恢复机制（版本控制回滚、会话范围权限重置或基于检查点的倒带）。 SWE-Agent 和 OpenHands（Yang 等人，2024；Wang 等人，2024b）主要依赖于 Docker 容器隔离，31 提供限制所有代理操作的环境级沙箱。 Codex CLI 支持 shell 命令的沙箱模式和批准策略。 OpenCode 和 Cline 还提供工具审批控制（Anomaly，2026；Cline，2026）。 Aider (Gauthier, 2024) 使用 Git 作为其主要安全机制，通过版本控制使所有更改都可逆。 Claude Code 结合了按操作拒绝优先规则、用于自动批准的基于 ML 的分类器、可选的 shell 沙箱和会话范围的权限非恢复，分层多种机制而不是依赖于单个隔离边界。

Sandboxing reports make this boundary concrete. E2B and OpenSandbox expose isolated execution environ- ments through APIs (E2B, 2026; OpenSandbox, 2026). OpenAI’s Windows sandbox discussion describes write access scoped to the working directory and configured writable roots, with explicit deny rules for selected read-only paths inside those roots (OpenAI, 2026c). Cursor’s cloud-agent report adds the cloud side of the same problem: network policy, secret redaction, and credential management become part of the agent environment (Ma, 2026). These details make the safety boundary an execution boundary, not only an approval prompt.

沙箱报告使这一边界变得具体。 E2B 和 OpenSandbox 通过 API 公开隔离的执行环境（E2B，2026；OpenSandbox，2026）。 OpenAI 的 Windows 沙箱讨论描述了仅限于工作目录和配置的可写根的写入访问，并为这些根内选定的只读路径提供了明确的拒绝规则（OpenAI，2026c）。 Cursor 的云代理报告添加了同一问题的云端：网络策略、秘密编辑和凭证管理成为代理环境的一部分（Ma，2026）。这些细节使安全边界成为执行边界，而不仅仅是审批提示。

Protocols and extensibility.

协议和可扩展性。

The Model Context Protocol that Claude Code uses as its primary external tool

Claude Code 用作其主要外部工具的模型上下文协议

integration has become a de facto standard with a substantial ecosystem and a corresponding attack surface.

集成已成为事实上的标准，具有庞大的生态系统和相应的攻击面。

Hou et al. (2025) catalogues thousands of community-developed MCP servers across 26 major directories and organizes MCP-specific threats into four attacker categories and sixteen scenarios, including tool poisoning, rug pulls, and cross-server shadowing. The permission and deny-rule machinery analyzed in Section 5 and the pre-filtering step in Section 6.2 can be read as the runtime side of the mitigations that survey calls for.

侯等人。 (2025) 对 26 个主要目录中的数千个社区开发的 MCP 服务器进行了编目，并将 MCP 特定威胁分为四个攻击者类别和 16 个场景，包括工具中毒、拉扯和跨服务器阴影。第 5 节中分析的许可和拒绝规则机制以及第 6.2 节中的预过滤步骤可以理解为调查要求的缓解措施的运行时方面。

The same point applies to higher-level tool design.

同样的一点也适用于更高级别的工具设计。

NSA guidance treats MCP security as a lifecycle

NSA 指南将 MCP 安全视为生命周期

issue that spans protocol design, runtime scheduling, integration with external services, and long-term monitoring (National Security Agency, 2026). A proposal for agent-first tool design argues that agent-facing APIs should offer search, resolve, preview, execute, verify, and recover phases, not just human-oriented CRUD endpoints (Pan, 2026). Read this way, MCP servers, plugins, skills, SDKs, and agent-to-agent protocols form a tool supply chain: they expand what an agent can do, but they also need identity, allowlists, versioning, audit logs, and revocation.

问题涵盖协议设计、运行时调度、与外部服务的集成以及长期监控（国家安全局，2026）。代理优先工具设计提案认为，面向代理的 API 应该提供搜索、解析、预览、执行、验证和恢复阶段，而不仅仅是面向人类的 CRUD 端点（Pan，2026）。这样理解，MCP 服务器、插件、技能、SDK 和代理到代理协议形成了一个工具供应链：它们扩展了代理的功能，但它们也需要身份、允许列表、版本控制、审核日志和撤销。

Software architecture.

软件架构。

Layered architecture patterns (Garlan et al., 1993) inform our five-layer decomposition.

分层架构模式（Garlan 等人，1993）告诉我们五层分解。

Role-based access control models (Sandhu et al., 2002) provide theory for the permission mode system.

基于角色的访问控制模型（Sandhu et al., 2002）为权限模式系统提供了理论。

Browser sandboxing (Reis and Gribble, 2009) is a similar per-process isolation approach. Multi-agent system theory (Wooldridge, 2009) helps explain subagent delegation.

浏览器沙箱（Reis 和 Gribble，2009）是一种类似的每进程隔离方法。多代理系统理论（Wooldridge，2009）有助于解释subagent委托。

Positioning.

定位。

Prior work on coding agents has focused on benchmarks (how well agents solve tasks), frameworks (how to compose agents), and products (what users can do). This paper contributes a source-grounded design- space analysis of a production coding agent, using source-level analysis and architectural comparison to surface design choices and trade-offs. It draws on the software architecture case study tradition (Garlan et al., 1993) but applies it to an LLM-based agent by systematically identifying design questions, mapping alternatives, and contrasting Claude Code’s choices with those of OpenClaw and Hermes Agent, two independent AI agent systems operating from different deployment contexts.

先前有关coding agent的工作主要集中在基准（代理解决任务的能力）、框架（如何组成代理）和产品（用户可以做什么）。本文利用源代码级分析和架构比较来对生产coding agent进行基于源代码的设计空间分析，以进行表面设计选择和权衡。它借鉴了软件架构案例研究传统（Garlan 等人，1993），但通过系统地识别设计问题、映射替代方案，并将 Claude Code 的选择与 OpenClaw 和 Hermes Agent（两个在不同部署环境中运行的独立 AI agent系统）的选择进行对比，将其应用于基于 LLM 的代理。

12

12

Discussion

讨论

The analysis in the preceding sections documented how Claude Code answers recurring design questions about loop architecture, safety posture, extensibility, context management, delegation, and persistence. Each answer reflects a position in a design space with real alternatives and measurable trade-offs. This section examines what those answers reveal when read together: the design philosophy they reflect (Section 12.1), the value tensions they create (Section 12.2), the architectural trade-offs they entail (Section 12.3), the empirical predictions they generate (Section 12.4), and the cross-cutting commitments that recur across subsystems (Section 12.7). The five-value framework from Section 2.1 organizes the section.

前面几节中的分析记录了 Claude Code 如何回答有关循环架构、安全态势、可扩展性、上下文管理、委派和持久性的反复出现的设计问题。每个答案都反映了设计空间中的立场，具有真正的替代方案和可衡量的权衡。本节将探讨这些答案在一起阅读时揭示的内容：它们反映的设计理念（第 12.1 节）、它们创造的价值张力（第 12.2 节）、它们带来的架构权衡（第 12.3 节）、它们生成的经验预测（第 12.4 节）以及跨子系统重复出现的跨领域承诺（第 12.7 节）。 2.1 节中的五值框架组织了本节。

32

32

12.1

12.1

Design Philosophy

设计理念

The values and design principles introduced in Section 2 predict an architecture that invests in operational infrastructure rather than decision scaffolding. The implementation confirms this: the architecture documented in Sections 3 to 9 is overwhelmingly deterministic infrastructure (permission gates, tool routing, context management, recovery logic), with the LLM invoked as a stateless completion endpoint. An estimated 1.6% of the codebase constitutes decision logic, the remaining 98.4% is the operational harness. This ratio is not accidental.

第 2 节中介绍的价值观和设计原则预测了一种投资于运营基础设施而不是决策脚手架的架构。实现证实了这一点：第 3 节到第 9 节中记录的架构是压倒性的确定性基础设施（权限门、工具路由、上下文管理、恢复逻辑），并且 LLM 作为无状态完成端点调用。估计代码库的 1.6% 构成决策逻辑，其余 98.4% 是操作harness。这个比例并非偶然。

The design principles documented in Section 2.2 underpin this approach: the harness creates conditions under which the model can decide well, rather than constraining its choices.

第 2.2 节中记录的设计原则支撑了这种方法：harness创造了模型可以做出良好决策的条件，而不是限制其选择。

This design runs counter to the dominant pattern in agent engineering, where frameworks such as LangGraph route model outputs through explicit graph nodes with typed edges, and systems like Devin pair multi-step planners with heavy operational infrastructure. Claude Code instead gives the model maximum decision latitude within a rich operational harness. The engineering complexity exists not to constrain the model’s decisions but to enable them. This layered architecture, where the model reasons and the harness enforces, raises the question of whether agentic coding tools are converging toward operating-system-like abstractions in which the core loop serves as the kernel and everything else constitutes the OS.

这种设计与代理工程中的主导模式背道而驰，其中 LangGraph 等框架通过具有类型化边缘的显式图形节点路由模型输出，而像 Devin 这样的系统将多步骤规划器与繁重的操作基础设施配对。相反，Claude Code 在丰富的操作harness内为模型提供了最大的决策自由度。工程复杂性的存在不是为了限制模型的决策，而是为了实现它们。这种由模型推理和harness强制执行的分层架构提出了这样一个问题：代理编码工具是否正在向类似操作系统的抽象收敛，其中核心循环充当内核，而其他一切都构成操作系统。

The design gains additional significance as frontier models converge in practical capability for coding tasks: the quality of the surrounding operational harness becomes the principal differentiator, validating an architecture that invests in infrastructure over decision scaffolding. For agent builders, the implication is that investing in deterministic infrastructure such as context management, safety layering, and recovery mechanisms may yield greater reliability gains than adding planning scaffolding around increasingly capable models. Recent native-runtime benchmarking gives this otherwise reasoned claim direct empirical support: holding the model fixed and changing only the surrounding harness shifts a single model’s score on long-horizon tasks by up to 18 points, with Claude Code and OpenClaw among the harnesses tested (Ding et al., 2026).

随着前沿模型在编码任务的实际能力上的融合，该设计获得了额外的意义：周围操作harness的质量成为主要的区别因素，验证了投资于基础设施而不是决策脚手架的架构。对于代理构建者来说，这意味着投资于上下文管理、安全分层和恢复机制等确定性基础设施可能会比围绕功能日益强大的模型添加规划脚手架带来更大的可靠性收益。最近的本机运行时基准测试为这一合理的主张提供了直接的实证支持：保持模型固定并仅更改周围的harness可以使单个模型在长期任务上的得分最多改变 18 分，测试的harness包括 Claude Code 和 OpenClaw（Ding 等人，2026）。

(Tier C) As model capability grows stronger, is the harness still needed for a model like Fable 5 that can read a codebase and fix it on its own? The harness serves the five values of Section 2.1, and only one of them, Capability Amplification, is mainly about how capable the model is. Within it, the part a stronger model affects is the decision scaffolding, like the planning and control logic that some frameworks wrap around the model to guide and constrain it toward a solution. Where the model is already strong, such as in code and mathematics, it reaches a successful solution on its own, so that guidance and constraint are less necessary.

（C层）随着模型能力越来越强，像《神鬼寓言5》这样可以读取代码库并自行修复的模型是否还需要harness？harness服务于2.1节的五个价值，其中只有一个，能力放大，主要是关于模型的能力如何。在其中，更强大的模型影响的部分是决策脚手架，例如一些框架围绕模型来指导和约束模型以实现解决方案的规划和控制逻辑。在模型已经很强大的地方，例如在代码和数学方面，它可以自行找到成功的解决方案，因此不需要指导和约束。

The other four values are not mainly about how capable the model is, so the harness that serves them stays.

其他四个值主要不是关于模型的能力如何，因此为它们服务的harness保留了下来。

Reliable Execution is the partial exception: a stronger model reads a request more accurately and fixes its own mistakes a bit better. But the context window is still finite, sessions still resume, and the model still tends to rebuild the same thing a different way each run, so context management and recovery are still needed.

可靠执行是部分例外：更强大的模型可以更准确地读取请求，并更好地修复自己的错误。但上下文窗口仍然是有限的，会话仍然恢复，并且模型仍然倾向于在每次运行时以不同的方式重建相同的事物，因此仍然需要上下文管理和恢复。

Safety, Security, and Privacy do not depend on capability at all. Deny-first rules, sandboxing, the auto-mode classifier, and audit logs matter as much as before, and a stronger model is riskier, not safer: Fable 5 itself is held back by safety classifiers in higher-risk areas (Anthropic, 2026d). Human Decision Authority is about the person’s right to decide, not the model’s capability, so approval, interruption, and transcripts keep their role.

安全、保密和隐私根本不依赖于能力。拒绝优先规则、沙箱、自动模式分类器和审计日志和以前一样重要，更强的模型风险更大，而不是更安全：《神鬼寓言 5》本身在高风险领域受到安全分类器的阻碍（Anthropic，2026d）。人类决策权是关于人的决定权，而不是模型的能力，因此批准、中断和记录保留其作用。

Contextual Adaptability is similar: a strong model is still a general model that has not seen your code, so CLAUDE.md, skills, and MCP still fit it to your project. Token economics, which bounds all five, does not relax either, since a better model does not make tokens free.

上下文适应性类似：强模型仍然是没有见过你的代码的通用模型，因此 CLAUDE.md、技能和 MCP 仍然适合你的项目。限制所有五个的代币经济学也没有放松，因为更好的模型并不会让代币变得免费。

Research bears this out even for the strongest models: the choice of harness alone moves Fable 5’s functional and security pass rates by ten points or more (Compagna, 2026). The harness does not become useless as model capability grows; what changes is only the decision scaffolding, and only where the model is already strong, while everything else it does stays as important as before.

即使对于最强大的模型，研究也证明了这一点：仅harness的选择就可以将《神鬼寓言 5》的功能和安全通过率提高十个百分点或更多（Compagna，2026）。harness不会随着模型能力的增长而变得毫无用处；改变的只是决策脚手架，而且只有模型已经很强大的地方，而它所做的其他一切仍然像以前一样重要。

Taken together, the preceding sections show that production coding agents face recurring design choices:

综上所述，前面的部分表明生产coding agent面临重复的设计选择：

where reasoning lives relative to the harness, how the iteration loop is structured, what safety posture to adopt by default, how the extension surface is partitioned, how context is assembled and compressed, how subagents are delegated and orchestrated, and how sessions persist across boundaries. Claude Code’s answers to these questions form a coherent design point that privileges model autonomy within a rich operational harness.

推理相对于harness的位置、迭代循环的结构、默认采用的安全态势、扩展表面如何分区、上下文如何组装和压缩、subagent如何委派和编排以及会话如何跨边界持续存在。 Claude Code 对这些问题的回答形成了一个连贯的设计点，在丰富的操作harness中赋予模型自治特权。
