12.2

12.2

Value Tensions

价值紧张

The five values identified in Section 2.1 generate tensions where pursuing one value constrains another (Table 6). These tensions are not design failures; they are structural consequences of pursuing multiple values simultaneously. We report the tensions with the strongest supporting evidence, not the full combinatorial set.

第 2.1 节中确定的五个价值观会产生紧张关系，其中追求一种价值观会限制另一种价值观（表 6）。这些紧张局势并非设计失败，而是设计失败。它们是同时追求多种价值观的结构性后果。我们用最有力的支持证据来报告紧张局势，而不是完整的组合集。

A separate question is whether short-term gains weaken long-term developer capability (Section 2.4). A randomized controlled trial of 16 experienced developers across 246 tasks (Becker et al., 2025) found that AI tools made developers 19% slower, despite a perceived 20% improvement. A causal analysis of Cursor adoption across 807 repositories (He et al., 2025) found that code complexity increased by 40.7%. An EEG study of 54 participants (Kosmyna et al., 2025) found that LLM users showed weakened neural connectivity that persisted after AI was removed. Researchers have proposed protocols for measuring cognitive offloading in AI-assisted programming, motivated by concerns that students using AI produce applications without understanding the underlying logic (Aiersilan, 2026). These findings, combined with a 25% decline in entry-level tech hiring between 2023 and 2024 (Rak, 2025), suggest that the tension between capability amplification and long-term sustainability extends beyond individual productivity to the broader developer pipeline. This evidence motivates the long-term capability question but does not target Claude Code’s architecture specifically. It is most relevant to agent systems that rely on bounded context, tool-use loops, and local generation decisions.

另一个问题是短期收益是否会削弱开发人员的长期能力（第 2.4 节）。对 16 名经验丰富的开发人员进行的一项针对 246 项任务的随机对照试验（Becker 等人，2025 年）发现，人工智能工具使开发人员的速度慢了 19%，尽管人们认为 AI 工具的速度提高了 20%。对 807 个存储库采用 Cursor 的因果分析（He et al., 2025）发现代码复杂性增加了 40.7%。一项针对 54 名参与者的脑电图研究（Kosmyna 等人，2025）发现，法学硕士用户的神经连接能力减弱，在人工智能被移除后，这种现象仍然存在。研究人员提出了测量人工智能辅助编程中认知卸载的协议，其动机是担心使用人工智能的学生在不了解底层逻辑的情况下生成应用程序（Aiersilan，2026）。这些发现，加上 2023 年至 2024 年间入门级技术招聘下降 25%（Rak，2025），表明能力放大和长期可持续性之间的紧张关系超出了个人生产力，扩展到了更广泛的开发人员渠道。该证据引发了长期能力问题，但并未专门针对 Claude Code 的架构。它与依赖有界上下文、工具使用循环和本地生成决策的代理系统最相关。

12.3

12.3

Architectural Trade-offs

架构权衡

The tensions in Table 6 manifest as concrete architectural trade-offs in four areas. The long-term sustainability concerns documented above surface in the empirical predictions of Section 12.4.

表 6 中的紧张局势表现为四个领域的具体架构权衡。第 12.4 节的实证预测中记录了长期可持续性问题。

Safety vs. autonomy.

安全与自主。

The permission modes (five always present, plus auto when the classifier feature flag is

权限模式（五种始终存在，当分类器功能标志为自动时

active, and the internal bubble mode) create a gradient from plan (user approves all plans) through default, acceptEdits, auto (ML classifier), to bypassPermissions (skips most prompts but safety-critical checks remain). The progression represents a monotonically decreasing safety gradient with increasing autonomy.

活动和内部气泡模式）创建从计划（用户批准所有计划）到默认、acceptEdits、自动（ML 分类器）到绕过权限（跳过大多数提示，但仍然保留安全关键检查）的渐变。该进展代表随着自主性的增加，安全梯度单调递减。

Not restoring session-scoped permissions from the transcript reflects a deliberate choice to err toward safety:

不从记录中恢复会话范围的权限反映了故意选择安全错误：

a new process starts without the old session grants.

新进程在没有旧会话授权的情况下启动。

34

34

The safety-autonomy gradient is shaped not only by architectural design but by user behavior. Anthropic’s auto-mode analysis (Hughes, 2026) found that users approve approximately 93% of permission prompts, indicating that approval fatigue renders interactive confirmation behaviorally unreliable. Longitudinal usage data (McCain et al., 2026) shows that auto-approve rates increase from approximately 20% at fewer than 50 sessions to over 40% by 750 sessions, with substantial increases in session duration. These patterns suggest that the gradient is navigated not by deliberate mode selection but by gradual habituation. Sandboxing reduced the frequency of permission prompts by an estimated 84% (Dworken and Weller-Davies, 2025), reframing the problem as a human-factors concern: the architectural response to unreliable human approval is to reduce the number of decisions humans must make.

安全-自主梯度不仅由架构设计决定，还由用户行为决定。 Anthropic 的自动模式分析（Hughes，2026）发现用户批准了大约 93% 的权限提示，这表明批准疲劳导致交互式确认行为不可靠。纵向使用数据（McCain 等人，2026 年）显示，自动批准率从少于 50 个会话时的约 20% 增加到 750 个会话时的 40% 以上，并且会话持续时间大幅增加。这些模式表明，梯度不是通过刻意的模式选择来引导的，而是通过逐渐习惯来引导的。沙盒将权限提示的频率减少了约 84%（Dworken 和 Weller-Davies，2025），将问题重新定义为人为因素问题：对不可靠的人类批准的架构响应是减少人类必须做出的决策数量。

More fundamentally, the defense-in-depth architecture described in Section 5 rests on an independence assumption: if one safety layer fails, others catch the violation. But Claude Code’s safety layers share common performance and economic constraints. The auto-mode classifier is a separate LLM call with direct token cost. The bashSecurity.ts module performs sequential AST-based checks with parsing latency. The deny-first rule evaluation operates on command structure. When performance pressure pushes toward reducing these costs, layers can degrade simultaneously. Security researchers (Adversa.ai, 2026) have documented that commands with more than 50 subcommands fall back to a single generic approval prompt instead of running per-subcommand deny-rule checks, because per-subcommand parsing caused UI freezes, demonstrating that defense-in-depth fails when the independence assumption is violated.

更根本的是，第 5 节中描述的深度防御架构基于一个独立假设：如果一个安全层发生故障，其他安全层就会发现违规行为。但 Claude Code 的安全层具有共同的性能和经济限制。自动模式分类器是一个单独的 LLM 调用，具有直接token成本。 bashSecurity.ts 模块执行基于 AST 的顺序检查，并具有解析延迟。拒绝优先规则评估在命令结构上运行。当性能压力推动降低这些成本时，各层可能会同时降级。安全研究人员（Adversa.ai，2026）记录了具有超过 50 个子命令的命令会回退到单个通用批准提示，而不是运行每个子命令拒绝规则检查，因为每个子命令解析导致 UI 冻结，这表明当违反独立性假设时，深度防御会失败。

This tension is structural. Any LLM-based agent system that uses the model itself for safety evaluation faces it. The relevant evaluation criterion is not whether any individual layer can be bypassed, but how many independent layers must fail simultaneously and whether they share failure modes.

这种紧张是结构性的。任何使用模型本身进行安全评估的基于LLM的代理系统都面临着它。相关的评估标准不是是否可以绕过任何单个层，而是有多少个独立层必须同时发生故障以及它们是否共享故障模式。

Permission model under adversarial conditions.

对抗条件下的权限模型。

Independent security research provides empirical validation

独立安全研究提供实证验证

of the permission architecture, specifically by revealing a temporal ordering property not captured in Figure 4.

权限架构的详细信息，特别是通过揭示图 4 中未捕获的时间排序属性。

Two independently verified vulnerabilities share a root cause in pre-trust initialization ordering: code executing during project initialization (MCP server connections and application of settings environment variables) ran, in earlier releases, before the user accepted the interactive trust dialog5. That pre-trust execution window fell outside the deny-first evaluation pipeline (permissions.ts), creating a structurally privileged phase where the safety guarantees documented in Section 5 did not yet apply. In the analyzed snapshot, the standard interactive startup path requires trust acceptance before hook execution, project MCP server connections, and application of project-scoped settings environment variables beyond a safe allowlist (hooks.ts, main.tsx, managedEnv.ts).

两个独立验证的漏洞在预信任初始化顺序中具有共同的根本原因：在早期版本中，在用户接受交互式信任对话框之前，在项目初始化期间执行的代码（MCP 服务器连接和设置环境变量的应用程序）运行。该预信任执行窗口超出了拒绝优先评估管道 (permissions.ts)，从而创建了一个结构性特权阶段，其中第 5 节中记录的安全保证尚未适用。在分析的快照中，标准交互式启动路径需要在钩子执行、项目 MCP 服务器连接以及应用超出安全允许列表（hooks.ts、main.tsx、managedEnv.ts）的项目范围设置环境变量之前接受信任。

This pattern reveals that the permission pipeline depicts a spatial ordering of safety checks but does not capture the temporal dimension: specifically, when during session initialization each mechanism becomes active. The pre-patch initialization sequence (MCP server execution, then trust acceptance, then permission enforcement) created a window where the extensibility architecture (Section 6) operated before the safety architecture (Section 5) was fully engaged. This finding refines the extensibility-versus-simplicity tension by adding a security dimension: extensibility creates attack surface not only through combinatorial complexity but through initialization ordering.

这种模式揭示了权限管道描述了安全检查的空间顺序，但没有捕获时间维度：具体来说，当在会话初始化期间每个机制变得活跃时。预补丁初始化序列（MCP 服务器执行，然后信任接受，然后权限执行）创建了一个窗口，在安全架构（第 5 节）完全参与之前，可扩展性架构（第 6 节）在该窗口中运行。这一发现通过添加安全维度来细化可扩展性与简单性之间的紧张关系：可扩展性不仅通过组合复杂性而且通过初始化排序来创建攻击面。

Context efficiency vs. transparency.

上下文效率与透明度。

The five-layer compaction pipeline achieves effective context management, but compression is largely invisible to the user. When budget reduction replaces a long tool output with a reference, when context collapse substitutes messages with a summary (described in the source as “a read-time projection over the REPL’s full history”), or when snip trims older history, the user has no easy way to inspect what was lost. The cache-aware behavior of microcompact adds further opacity, as compression decisions are influenced by prompt caching in ways not visible to the user. External research on summary-based compaction documents two costs beyond opacity that the source-level view does not surface: the summarization step is a blocking inference stall, and it is non-deterministic, with the retained content fluctuating across runs on identical inputs (Cim et al., 2026).

五层压缩管道实现了有效的上下文管理，但压缩对于用户来说基本上是不可见的。当预算减少用参考替换长工具输出时，当上下文崩溃用摘要替换消息时（在源代码中描述为“对 REPL 完整历史记录的读取时间投影”），或者当剪裁修剪旧历史记录时，用户没有简单的方法来检查丢失的内容。 microcompact 的缓存感知行为进一步增加了不透明性，因为压缩决策会以用户不可见的方式受到即时缓存的影响。基于摘要的压缩的外部研究记录了源级视图未显示的除了不透明度之外的两个成本：摘要步骤是阻塞推理停顿，并且它是非确定性的，保留的内容在相同输入的运行中波动（Cim 等人，2026）。

5The two pre-trust ordering vulnerabilities are CVE-2025-59536 (CVSS 8.7) and CVE-2026-21852 (CVSS 5.3) (Donenfeld and Vanunu, 2026), discovered by Check Point Research. CVE-2025-54794 and CVE-2025-54795 (Beber, 2025) exploit path validation and command parsing flaws elsewhere in the permission pipeline, separately. All four were patched within weeks of disclosure.

5这两个预信任排序漏洞是由 Check Point Research 发现的 CVE-2025-59536 (CVSS 8.7) 和 CVE-2026-21852 (CVSS 5.3)（Donenfeld 和 Vanunu，2026）。 CVE-2025-54794 和 CVE-2025-54795（Beber，2025）分别利用权限管道中其他位置的路径验证和命令解析缺陷。所有四个补丁都在披露后几周内得到了修复。

35

35

Simplicity vs. extensibility.

简单性与可扩展性。

The four extension mechanisms enable rich customization but create combinatorial

四种扩展机制可实现丰富的定制，但会创建组合

interactions. A plugin contributes a PreToolUse hook that modifies tool inputs. The auto-mode classifier reads cached CLAUDE.md content. Path-scoped rules load lazily when new directories are read, potentially changing classifier behavior mid-conversation. The permission handler’s four branches interact with the hook pipeline at multiple points. These cross-cutting concerns create emergent behaviors difficult to predict from any single configuration file.

互动。插件提供了一个 PreToolUse 钩子来修改工具输入。自动模式分类器读取缓存的 CLAUDE.md 内容。当读取新目录时，路径范围的规则会延迟加载，这可能会在对话中改变分类器的行为。权限处理程序的四个分支在多个点与钩子管道交互。这些横切关注点会产生难以从任何单个配置文件中预测的紧急行为。

12.4

12.4

Empirical Predictions and Early Signals

经验预测和早期信号

The architectural properties documented in this paper generate testable predictions about code quality outcomes not derivable from the source code alone. The bounded context window (Section 7) prevents the agent from maintaining simultaneous awareness of the full codebase: the five-layer compaction pipeline preserves useful information but introduces lossy compression at each stage. This makes it architecturally predicted that agent-generated code will exhibit higher rates of pattern duplication and convention violation than code produced with full codebase visibility. Subagent isolation (Section 8), where each subagent operates in its own context window with an independently assembled tool pool, compounds the effect: parallel agents can independently re-implement solutions that already exist elsewhere. The design philosophy of Section 12.1 trusts the model to make good local decisions, but good local decisions can produce poor global outcomes when the model lacks global context.

本文中记录的架构属性生成有关代码质量结果的可测试预测，而这些预测不能仅从源代码中得出。有界上下文窗口（第 7 节）阻止代理保持对完整代码库的同时感知：五层压缩管道保留有用的信息，但在每个阶段引入有损压缩。这使得从架构上预测代理生成的代码将比使用完整代码库可见性生成的代码表现出更高的模式重复和约定违反率。subagent隔离（第 8 节），其中每个subagent在其自己的上下文窗口中使用独立组装的工具池运行，复合了效果：并行代理可以独立地重新实现其他地方已经存在的解决方案。第 12.1 节的设计理念相信模型能够做出良好的局部决策，但当模型缺乏全局背景时，良好的局部决策可能会产生较差的全局结果。

Published empirical work on architecturally similar tools provides data consistent with these predictions. A causal analysis of Cursor adoption across 807 repositories (He et al., 2025) found a statistically significant increase in code complexity, with an initial velocity spike that dissipated to baseline by month three. Rising complexity was associated with a proportional decrease in future development velocity, suggesting that short-term gains can be offset by later maintenance costs6. A large-scale audit of 304,000 AI-authored commits across 6,275 repositories (Liu et al., 2026) found measurable technical debt, with approximately one-quarter of AI-introduced issues persisting to the latest revision and security-related issues persisting at a substantially higher rate. While these studies target adjacent systems, the architectural parallels (bounded context, tool-use loops, local generation decisions) make them relevant comparison points for the design analyzed here. A controlled minimal-pair study run on Claude Code itself sharpens where these effects are measurable: code cleanliness did not change task pass rate, but cleaner inputs reduced token use by 7 to 8% and file revisitations by 34% (Trivedi and Schmitt, 2026), indicating that the operational footprint (cost and navigation), rather than raw success, is where the harness’s handling of context most visibly registers.

已发表的关于架构相似工具的实证研究提供了与这些预测一致的数据。对 807 个存储库采用 Cursor 的因果分析（He et al., 2025）发现，代码复杂性在统计上显着增加，初始速度峰值在第三个月时消散到基线。复杂性的上升与未来开发速度的成比例下降相关，这表明短期收益可以被后期的维护成本所抵消6。对 6,275 个存储库中 304,000 个人工智能编写的提交进行的大规模审计（Liu 等人，2026）发现了可衡量的技术债务，大约四分之一的人工智能引入的问题持续到最新版本，而与安全相关的问题持续存在的比例要高得多。虽然这些研究针对的是相邻系统，但架构上的相似之处（有界上下文、工具使用循环、本地生成决策）使它们成为此处分析的设计的相关比较点。在 Claude Code 上运行的受控最小对研究本身在这些影响可测量的地方变得更加尖锐：代码清洁度并没有改变任务通过率，但更清洁的输入将token使用量减少了 7% 到 8%，文件重新访问量减少了 34%（Trivedi 和 Schmitt，2026），这表明操作足迹（成本和导航），而不是原始成功，是harness对上下文的处理最明显的地方。

Claude Code’s context management pipeline is specifically designed to mitigate these effects: graduated compression preserves the most recent and most relevant context, cache-aware compaction avoids invalidating prompt caches during compression, read-time projection maintains full history for reconstruction while presenting a compressed view to the model, and subagent summary isolation prevents exploratory noise from accumulating in the parent context. Whether these mechanisms are sufficient to overcome the structural limitations of bounded context is a directly measurable empirical question that the source-level analysis in this paper cannot resolve.

Claude Code 的上下文管理管道专门设计用于减轻这些影响：分级压缩保留最新和最相关的上下文，缓存感知压缩避免在压缩期间使提示缓存无效，读取时投影在向模型呈现压缩视图的同时保留完整的重建历史记录，subagent摘要隔离可防止探索性噪声在父上下文中累积。这些机制是否足以克服有界上下文的结构限制，是一个可直接测量的实证问题，本文的源级分析无法解决。

12.5

12.5

Limitations

局限性

Beyond the methodological limitations described in the appendix, several analytical constraints apply. The memoized context assembly functions (getSystemContext() and getUserContext() both use lodash memoize at context.ts) mean that git status and CLAUDE.md content are cached rather than recomputed on every turn. Dynamic changes during a conversation may not be reflected immediately, though compaction can clear caches and lazy-loaded path-scoped rules provide a partial counter-mechanism.

除了附录中描述的方法限制之外，还存在一些分析限制。记忆上下文组装函数（getSystemContext() 和 getUserContext() 都在 context.ts 中使用 lodash memoize）意味着 git status 和 CLAUDE.md 内容会被缓存，而不是每次都重新计算。尽管压缩可以清除缓存并且延迟加载的路径范围规则提供了部分反机制，但会话期间的动态变化可能不会立即反映出来。

Feature flags create build-time variability. In a build where TRANSCRIPT_CLASSIFIER is false, the entire auto-mode classifier is eliminated. Feature-gated modules use dynamic require() rather than static import (e.g., query.ts for context collapse), because feature() only works in if/ternary conditions due to a bun:bundle tree-shaking constraint. Different build targets may produce functionally different applications.

功能标志会产生构建时的可变性。在 TRANSCRIPT_CLASSIFIER 为 false 的构建中，整个自动模式分类器将被消除。功能门控模块使用动态 require() 而不是静态导入（例如，用于上下文折叠的 query.ts），因为由于 Bun:bundle 树摇晃约束，feature() 只能在 if/三元条件下工作。不同的构建目标可能会产生功能不同的应用程序。

6Complexity +40.7% (p < 0.001), with a velocity spike of +281% in month one and baseline performance by month three.

6复杂性 +40.7% (p < 0.001)，第一个月速度峰值 +281%，第三个月达到基线性能。

36

36

12.6

12.6

Emerging Directions

新兴方向

Several aspects of the implementation relate to broader design questions. Longer context windows would reduce compaction pressure, potentially simplifying the graduated pipeline. Multi-modal tools (screenshots, diagrams, UI previews) would expand the tool surface and create new context challenges. Claude Code Desktop already moves in this direction: in its Browser pane, Claude checks UI changes by taking screenshots, inspecting page elements, and interacting with the running app (Anthropic, 2026c). Formal verification of permission properties (for example, proving that deny rules always take precedence, that sandboxed commands cannot escape isolation, or that resumed sessions cannot inherit stale permissions) would provide stronger safety guarantees.

实施的几个方面涉及更广泛的设计问题。较长的上下文窗口将减少压实压力，从而可能简化分级管道。多模式工具（屏幕截图、图表、UI 预览）将扩展工具表面并带来新的上下文挑战。 Claude Code Desktop 已经朝这个方向发展：在其浏览器窗格中，Claude 通过截取屏幕截图、检查页面元素以及与正在运行的应用程序交互来检查 UI 更改（Anthropic，2026c）。权限属性的正式验证（例如，证明拒绝规则始终优先，沙盒命令无法逃脱隔离，或者恢复的会话无法继承过时的权限）将提供更强的安全保证。

Architectural decoupling.

架构解耦。

The tightly coupled local architecture analyzed here is one point on a spectrum

这里分析的紧密耦合的局部架构只是整个谱系中的一个点

that is already evolving. Anthropic’s own Managed Agents work (Martin et al., 2026) describes virtualizing the components of an agent (session, harness, sandbox) so that “each became an interface that made few assumptions about the others, and each could fail or be replaced independently”, drawing an explicit analogy to how operating systems virtualized hardware into processes and files. The Harness Design essay (Rajasekaran, 2026) makes a similar point from a different angle, arguing that better models move the space of useful harness combinations rather than eliminating it. The architecture documented in this paper should therefore be read as a snapshot of a co-evolving system rather than a fixed optimum.

这已经在不断发展。 Anthropic 自己的托管代理工作（Martin 等人，2026）描述了对代理组件（会话、harness、沙箱）进行虚拟化，以便“每个组件都成为一个对其他组件进行很少假设的接口，并且每个组件都可以独立失败或被替换”，这与操作系统如何将硬件虚拟化为进程和文件进行了明确的类比。 《Harness Design》论文（Rajasekaran，2026）从不同的角度提出了类似的观点，认为更好的模型会移动有用的harness组合的空间，而不是消除它。因此，本文中记录的架构应被视为共同进化系统的快照，而不是固定的最佳值。

Cloud-agent reports make this concrete without relying on the operating-system analogy. Cursor separates the agent loop, machine state, and conversation state, and treats VM checkpointing, network policy, secret handling, and credential management as part of the agent product (Ma, 2026). LangChain’s Managed Deep Agents makes a similar move by placing durable threads, checkpointing, streaming, context, observability, and human-in-the-loop workflows in the managed runtime (LangChain, 2026c). Google’s Antigravity gives people artifacts such as plans, screenshots, and recordings to check an agent’s work (Google, 2025). DeepSeek Harness makes runtime components, including the agent loop and session log, replaceable through plugin configuration (DeepSeek-AI, 2026). The useful design question is therefore runtime ownership: which layer owns the loop, state, sandbox, policy, and recovery path?

云代理报告使这一点变得具体，而不依赖于操作系统的类比。 Cursor 将代理循环、机器状态和会话状态分开，并将虚拟机检查点、网络策略、秘密处理和凭证管理视为代理产品的一部分（Ma，2026）。 LangChain 的托管深度代理也采取了类似的举措，在托管运行时中放置持久线程、检查点、流、上下文、可观察性和人机交互工作流程（LangChain，2026c）。谷歌的反重力为人们提供了计划、屏幕截图和录音等工件来检查特工的工作（谷歌，2025）。 DeepSeek Harness 使运行时组件（包括代理循环和会话日志）可以通过插件配置进行替换（DeepSeek-AI，2026）。因此，有用的设计问题是运行时所有权：哪一层拥有循环、状态、沙箱、策略和恢复路径？

Adapting models and harnesses.

调整模型和harness。

Automated adaptation can change the harness, the model, or both. Yang

自动适应可以改变harness、模型或两者。杨

et al. (2026b) keep model weights fixed and use failure traces to revise instructions, tools, hooks, and context management. Yu et al. (2026) go one step further: an expert model corrects failed turns in a smaller model’s own traces, and these corrections become fine-tuning data for a model that is then evaluated with the evolved harness.

等人。 (2026b) 保持模型权重固定，并使用故障跟踪来修改指令、工具、挂钩和上下文管理。于等人。 （2026）更进一步：专家模型纠正较小模型自身轨迹中的失败转弯，这些纠正成为模型的微调数据，然后使用进化的harness进行评估。

Memory as a first-class subsystem.

内存作为一流的子系统。

The memory survey of Hu et al. (2025) argues that agent memory is

胡等人的记忆调查。 (2025) 认为主体记忆是

becoming a distinct cognitive substrate rather than a side effect of context window management, and identifies automated memory management, RL-driven memory, and trustworthy memory (privacy, explainability, and hallucination robustness) as open frontiers. Claude Code today exposes the factual tier (CLAUDE.md, auto memory) and the working tier (the conversation window). The experiential tier (accumulated, automatically curated playbooks of strategies learned from past sessions) is the natural next step, and the context-engineering literature (Zhang et al., 2025a) has started to provide mechanisms for that accumulation.

成为一种独特的认知基础，而不是上下文窗口管理的副作用，并将自动记忆管理、强化学习驱动的记忆和可信记忆（隐私、可解释性和幻觉鲁棒性）确定为开放前沿。 Claude Code 今天公开了事实层（CLAUDE.md，自动记忆）和工作层（对话窗口）。体验层（从过去的课程中学习到的策略的积累、自动策划的剧本）是自然的下一步，上下文工程文献（Zhang 等人，2025a）已经开始提供这种积累的机制。

Context Hub makes the boundary visible. LangSmith Context Hub treats AGENTS.md files, skills, policies, examples, and related bundles as versioned and reviewable artifacts, with tags for deployment across environ- ments (LangChain, 2026a). That is a different design point from Claude Code’s file hierarchy, but it names the same problem: context needs a lifecycle, not only a token budget. Skills raise a related maintenance problem, because an update to one skill can affect others. Yang et al. (2026a) represent dependencies, joint use, and conflicts among skills in a graph, revise related skills together, and check each combined change by replaying relevant cases.

Context Hub 使边界可见。 LangSmith Context Hub 将 AGENTS.md 文件、技能、策略、示例和相关捆绑包视为版本化和可审查的工件，并带有用于跨环境部署的标签（LangChain，2026a）。这是与 Claude Code 的文件层次结构不同的设计点，但它提出了相同的问题：上下文需要生命周期，而不仅仅是token预算。技能会引发相关的维护问题，因为一项技能的更新可能会影响其他技能。杨等人。 (2026a) 用图表表示技能之间的依赖关系、共同使用和冲突，一起修改相关技能，并通过重放相关案例来检查每个组合的变化。

Observability and silent failure.

可观察性和静默失败。

Industry surveys suggest that the dominant failure mode of deployed agents is

行业调查表明，已部署代理的主要故障模式是

not crashes but silent mistakes. Bessemer’s 2026 infrastructure report (Wade et al., 2026) estimates that “78% of AI failures are invisible”, while LangChain’s 1,340-respondent state-of-agent-engineering survey (LangChain, 2026d) identifies quality, not cost, as the top barrier to production use and finds a wide gap between 37 observability (nearly 89% adoption) and offline evaluation (52.4%). The architecture analyzed here gives operators visibility into tool calls, hooks, and session transcripts. Closing the evaluation gap likely requires additional scaffolding (generator-evaluator separation, sprint contracts, and post-hoc checks of the kind discussed in Rajasekaran (2026)) rather than model improvements alone.

不是崩溃，而是无声的错误。 Bessemer 的 2026 年基础设施报告（Wade 等，2026）估计“78% 的人工智能故障是看不见的”，而 LangChain 的 1,340 名受访者代理工程状况调查（LangChain，2026d）将质量而非成本视为生产使用的最大障碍，并发现 37 可观察性（近 89% 采用率）与离线评估之间存在巨大差距（52.4%）。这里分析的架构使操作员能够了解工具调用、挂钩和会话记录。缩小评估差距可能需要额外的脚手架（生成器-评估器分离、冲刺合同以及 Rajasekaran (2026) 中讨论的那种事后检查），而不仅仅是模型改进。

OpenAI’s agent-improvement loop makes this operational: traces and feedback become evals, and the resulting evidence becomes a Codex handoff for harness changes (Pasfield, 2026). In that loop, telemetry matters because it turns into a reusable test or a concrete change request.

OpenAI 的代理改进循环使这一操作变得可行：跟踪和反馈成为评估，所得证据成为harness更改的 Codex 交接（Pasfield，2026）。在这个循环中，遥测很重要，因为它变成了可重用的测试或具体的变更请求。

Governance.

治理。

Broader governance trends will constrain the design space as agents become more autonomous.

随着代理变得更加自主，更广泛的治理趋势将限制设计空间。

The International AI Safety Report (Bengio et al., 2026) warns that “AI agents pose heightened risks because they act autonomously, making it harder for humans to intervene before failures cause harm,” and the MIT AI Agent Index (Staufer et al., 2026) finds that only 13.3% of indexed agentic systems publish agent-specific safety cards. Emerging regulatory efforts, including the EU AI Act and evolving copyright debates around AI-generated code, may impose external constraints on logging, transparency, risk management, and human oversight that shape how coding agent architectures evolve.

国际人工智能安全报告（Bengio 等人，2026）警告说，“人工智能代理会带来更高的风险，因为它们自主行动，使人类更难在故障造成伤害之前进行干预”，麻省理工学院人工智能代理索引（Staufer 等人，2026）发现，只有 13.3% 的索引代理系统发布了代理特定的安全卡。新兴的监管努力，包括欧盟人工智能法案和围绕人工智能生成代码不断演变的版权争论，可能会对日志记录、透明度、风险管理和人类监督施加外部限制，从而影响coding agent架构的发展。

Proactive architectures.

主动架构。

The feature-gated KAIROS system illustrates how this architecture may evolve

功能门控 KAIROS 系统说明了该架构如何演变

beyond reactive tool use. KAIROS implements a persistent background agent with tick-based heartbeats:

超越反应性工具的使用。 KAIROS 实现了一个带有基于蜱的心跳的持久后台代理：

when no user messages are pending, the system injects periodic <tick> prompts, and the model decides whether to act or sleep. The design addresses a documented tension in one study of proactive AI assistants:

当没有待处理的用户消息时，系统会注入定期的 <tick> 提示，并且模型决定是执行还是休眠。该设计解决了一项针对主动人工智能助手的研究中记录的紧张局势：

task completion increased by 12 to 18%, while user preference dropped at high frequencies (Chen et al., 2025).

任务完成率增加了 12% 至 18%，而用户偏好却频繁下降（Chen 等人，2025）。

KAIROS tries to resolve this through terminal focus awareness (maximizing autonomous action when the user is away, increasing collaboration when present) and economic throttling via SleepTool (each wake-up costs an API call, and the prompt cache expires after five minutes of inactivity, making sleep/wake an explicit cost optimization). This binding of proactivity to both user presence and token economics is notable, though KAIROS cannot be confirmed as active in production builds.

KAIROS 尝试通过终端焦点感知（用户离开时最大化自主操作，在场时增加协作）和通过 SleepTool 进行经济节流（每次唤醒需要一次 API 调用，并且提示缓存在五分钟不活动后过期，使睡眠/唤醒成为显式成本优化）来解决此问题。尽管无法确认 KAIROS 在生产版本中是否活跃，但这种主动性与用户存在和代币经济的结合是值得注意的。

12.7

12.7

Recurring Design Choices

重复设计选择

Reading the six subsystem analyses together reveals three cross-cutting design commitments that recur across otherwise independent components.

一起阅读六个子系统分析揭示了三个跨领域的设计承诺，这些设计承诺在其他独立的组件中重复出现。

Graduated layering over monolithic mechanisms.

整体机制上的分级分层。

Safety, context management, and extensibility all use grad-

安全性、上下文管理和可扩展性都使用梯度

uated stacks of independent mechanisms rather than single integrated solutions. The permission architecture layers the seven independent stages enumerated in Section 3.5: tool pre-filtering, deny-first rule evaluation, permission-mode constraints, the auto-mode classifier, shell sandboxing, non-restoration of session permissions

独立机制的堆栈，而不是单一的集成解决方案。权限架构分层了 3.5 节中列举的七个独立阶段：工具预过滤、拒绝优先规则评估、权限模式约束、自动模式分类器、shell 沙箱、会话权限不恢复

from transcripts, and hook interception. Context management layers five compaction stages, lazy-loaded

CLAUDE.md files, deferred tool schemas, and summary-only subagent returns. Extensibility layers four mechanisms (MCP servers, plugins, skills, and hooks) at different context costs (Section 6). In each case, the design trades simplicity and debuggability for defense in depth, accepting that the interaction between layers can produce emergent behaviors difficult to predict from any single configuration. The OpenClaw and Hermes Agent contrasts in Section 10 show that graduated layering recurs across systems with very different deployment contexts, suggesting that the layering pattern reflects the shared design questions rather than Claude Code-specific implementation choices.

CLAUDE.md 文件、延迟工具模式和仅摘要subagent返回。可扩展性以不同的上下文成本分层了四种机制（MCP 服务器、插件、技能和挂钩）（第 6 节）。在每种情况下，设计都会牺牲简单性和可调试性来换取深度防御，并接受层之间的交互可能会产生难以从任何单一配置预测的紧急行为。第 10 节中的 OpenClaw 和 Hermes Agent 对比表明，分级分层在具有非常不同的部署上下文的系统中重复出现，这表明分层模式反映了共享的设计问题，而不是 Claude Code 特定的实现选择。

Append-only designs that favor auditability over query power.

仅附加设计更注重可审核性而不是查询能力。

Session transcripts are append-only JSONL

会话记录是仅附加的 JSONL

files with read-time chain patching. Session-scoped permissions are not stored in the transcript. Context compaction applies read-time projections over a full history rather than destructive edits. This commitment recurs because it preserves the ability to resume, fork, and audit sessions without modifying previously written state. The cost is that richer structured queries (“show me all tool calls that modified file X across sessions”) require post-hoc reconstruction rather than direct lookup.

具有读取时间链修补的文件。会话范围的权限不存储在记录中。上下文压缩对完整历史记录应用读取时间投影，而不是破坏性编辑。此承诺会重复出现，因为它保留了恢复、分叉和审核会话的能力，而无需修改先前写入的状态。代价是更丰富的结构化查询（“向我显示跨会话修改文件 X 的所有工具调用”）需要事后重建而不是直接查找。

Model judgment within a deterministic harness.

确定性harness内的模型判断。

Across all subsystems, the architecture trusts the model’s

在所有子系统中，架构都信任模型的

judgment within a rich deterministic harness rather than constraining its choices. The estimated 1.6% 38 decision-logic ratio captures this quantitatively: the harness creates conditions (tool routing, permission enforcement, context assembly, recovery logic) under which the model can decide well. Hierarchical permissions preserve safety invariants across agent boundaries, and assembleToolPool() merges built-in and MCP tools into a single unified interface, but the model retains full latitude over which tools to invoke and in what order.

在丰富的确定性harness内进行判断，而不是限制其选择。估计的 1.6% 38 决策逻辑比率定量地反映了这一点：harness创建了模型可以做出良好决策的条件（工具路由、权限执行、上下文组装、恢复逻辑）。分层权限保留了跨代理边界的安全不变性，assembleToolPool() 将内置工具和 MCP 工具合并到一个统一的接口中，但模型保留了调用哪些工具以及按什么顺序调用的完全自由度。

The trade-off is that good local decisions can produce poor global outcomes when bounded context prevents global awareness, as the empirical predictions of Section 12.4 document.

权衡是，当有限环境阻碍全局意识时，良好的局部决策可能会产生糟糕的全局结果，正如第 12.4 节文件的经验预测。

13

13

Future Directions

未来的方向

Section 12 read the architecture documented in Sections 3 to 9 as a coherent design point and surfaced the tensions, trade-offs, and near-horizon directions that design point implies. This section steps beyond the architecture itself to record six open questions that Section 12.6 partially names and that a growing external literature has sharpened enough to state concretely. These questions cover the paper’s five-value framework (Section 2.1) and the long-term developer-capability question introduced in Section 2.4. They concern governance constraints on the Authority hierarchy (Section 13.5), the observability-evaluation gap on the Safety side (Section 13.1), cross-session persistence on the Reliability side (Section 13.2), four extensions of the Capability frontier (Section 13.3), horizon scaling as a distinct axis of Reliable Execution (Section 13.4), and long-term developer capability reframed as a design question (Section 13.6). Consistent with Section 12.6’s framing, each question is posed in the form whether/how/which. Specific mechanism choices are named when the cited sources name them and left open otherwise.

第 12 节将第 3 节至第 9 节中记录的架构视为连贯的设计点，并揭示了设计点所暗示的紧张、权衡和近水平方向。本节超越了架构本身，记录了第 12.6 节部分提及的六个开放问题，并且不断增长的外部文献已经足够清晰地具体说明这些问题。这些问题涵盖了本文的五值框架（第 2.1 节）和第 2.4 节中介绍的长期开发人员能力问题。它们涉及权威层次结构的治理约束（第 13.5 节）、安全方面的可观察性评估差距（第 13.1 节）、可靠性方面的跨会话持久性（第 13.2 节）、能力边界的四个扩展（第 13.3 节）、作为可靠执行的独特轴的水平扩展（第 13.4 节）以及重新构建为设计问题的长期开发人员能力（第 13.6 节）。与第 12.6 节的框架一致，每个问题都以“是否/如何/哪个”的形式提出。具体的机制选择在引用的来源命名时命名，否则保持开放。

13.1

13.1

Silent Failure and the Observability-Evaluation Gap

无声的失败和可观察性评估差距

The observability-evaluation gap reported in Section 12.6 could reflect a missing tooling layer, a missing evaluation interface inside the harness, or a model-capability ceiling. The sources there do not adjudicate.

第 12.6 节中报告的可观察性-评估差距可能反映了缺少的工具层、harness内缺少的评估接口或模型能力上限。那里的消息来源不做裁决。

How the silent-mistake failure mode noted in that paragraph should be surfaced is therefore an architectural question for the harness rather than a capability question for the model. Recent empirical work characterises the gap at several resolutions. Cemri et al. (2025) catalogue fourteen failure modes spanning system-design issues, inter-agent misalignment, and task verification. Pathak et al. (2025) build a benchmark of agent trajectories specifically for anomaly detection in traces. Yao et al. (2024) expose consistency gaps via the passk metric, the probability that all k independent trials succeed. Kapoor et al. (2024) argue that current agent benchmarks lack holdouts and cost controls, limiting what observability can actually diagnose.

因此，该段落中提到的无声错误故障模式应如何显现是harness的架构问题，而不是模型的能力问题。最近的实证工作描述了几个决议中的差距。塞姆里等人。 (2025) 列出了十四种故障模式，涵盖系统设计问题、代理间错位和任务验证。帕塔克等人。 (2025) 专门针对痕迹中的异常检测建立代理轨迹基准。姚等人。 (2024) 通过 passk 指标暴露一致性差距，即所有 k 个独立试验成功的概率。卡普尔等人。 (2024) 认为当前的代理基准缺乏坚持和成本控制，限制了可观察性实际诊断的内容。

Harnesses that update themselves also need fair comparisons. Wang et al. (2026b) compare harness evolution with search over task solutions under matched feedback and inference budgets, and Le et al. (2026) test trained models with a harness held out of training.

自我更新的安全带也需要公平的比较。王等人。 (2026b) 将harness的演变与匹配反馈和推理预算下的任务解决方案搜索进行比较，Le 等人。 (2026) 使用训练外的harness来测试经过训练的模型。

Against the permission pipeline and tool-orchestration layers analysed in Sections 4 and 5, two architectural questions remain open. First, whether the scaffolding the paper cites from Rajasekaran (2026) (generator- evaluator separation, sprint contracts, post-hoc checks, building on Madaan et al. (2023)’s self-refine pattern) belongs inside the harness (e.g., as additional hook events alongside the 27 documented in Section 6) or outside it as a separate evaluation layer is not settled by the cited sources. Second, whether the existing hook pipeline of Section 6 can host such scaffolding within its current context-cost envelope is a further open question. The observation that closing this gap “likely requires additional scaffolding . . . rather than model improvements alone” (Section 12.6) locates the open work at the harness layer.

相对于第 4 节和第 5 节中分析的权限管道和工具编排层，有两个架构问题仍然悬而未决。首先，论文引用的 Rajasekaran (2026) 脚手架（生成器-评估器分离、冲刺合同、事后检查、基于 Madaan 等人 (2023) 的自我完善模式）是否属于harness内部（例如，作为第 6 节中记录的 27 个事件旁边的附加钩子事件），还是作为单独的评估层在harness外部，尚未由引用的来源解决。其次，第 6 节的现有钩子管道是否可以在其当前的上下文成本范围内托管此类脚手架是一个进一步悬而未决的问题。缩小这一差距“可能需要额外的脚手架……而不仅仅是模型改进”（第 12.6 节）的观察结果将开放工作定位在harness层。

13.2

13.2

Persistence: Memory and Longitudinal Colleague Relationships

持久性：记忆和纵向同事关系

Whether agent state and the human-agent working relationship should persist across sessions, and in what form, is treated by the paper at two distinct layers today. Section 7 documents the four-level CLAUDE.md hierarchy and auto memory. Section 9 documents mostly-append-only JSONL transcripts, with explicit cleanup rewrites; session-scoped permissions are not stored in them. What belongs between these two layers is an open design question: durable state that is neither a static instruction nor a single session’s transcript. Hu et al. (2025) and Zhang et al. (2025a), already cited in Section 12.6, motivate an accumulating layer. Packer et al. (2023) reframes the LLM as an operating system with paged memory. Chhikara et al.

今天的论文在两个不同的层面上讨论了代理状态和人类与代理工作关系是否应该在会话中持续存在以及以什么形式存在。第 7 节记录了四级 CLAUDE.md 层次结构和自动内存。第 9 节记录了大部分仅附加的 JSONL 转录本，并进行了显式清理重写；会话范围的权限不存储在其中。这两层之间属于一个开放的设计问题：持久状态既不是静态指令，也不是单个会话的记录。胡等人。 （2025）和张等人。 (2025a)，已在第 12.6 节中引用，激励累积层。帕克等人。 (2023) 将 LLM 重新构建为具有分页内存的操作系统。奇卡拉等人。

39

39

(2025) builds a production-oriented memory store that survives restarts, while Xu et al. (2025) proposes a research agentic-memory design. Wang et al. (2024c) captures reusable procedural traces. Shinn et al. (2023) accumulates self-reflection traces via verbal reinforcement across attempts. Surveys by Zhang et al. (2025b) and Huang et al. (2026) map candidate mechanisms.

(2025) 构建了一个面向生产的内存存储，可以在重新启动后继续存在，而 Xu 等人。 (2025) 提出了一种研究代理记忆设计。王等人。 (2024c) 捕获可重用的程序痕迹。辛恩等人。 （2023）通过尝试中的言语强化来积累自我反思的痕迹。张等人的调查。 （2025b）和黄等人。 （2026）映射候选机制。

MemGym makes the memory question more concrete. It evaluates dynamic memory formation across tool-use dialogue, deep research, coding, and computer use, rather than only retention of personal facts in chat (Xu et al., 2026). This moves the question from “where should notes be stored?” to “which remembered state helps the agent keep working correctly?” Persistent memory also needs a way to recover from harmful entries. MemSecBench tests whether stored information affects later actions and whether repair keeps specified valid memories intact (Chen et al., 2026).

MemGym 让记忆问题变得更加具体。它评估工具使用对话、深入研究、编码和计算机使用中的动态记忆形成，而不仅仅是聊天中个人事实的保留（Xu 等人，2026）。这将问题从“笔记应该存储在哪里？”转移了。 “哪个记住的状态可以帮助代理继续正常工作？”持久内存还需要一种从有害条目中恢复的方法。 MemSecBench 测试存储的信息是否影响后续操作以及修复是否保持指定的有效内存完好无损（Chen 等人，2026）。

The same persistence question recurs on the human side. Section 12.6 already cites longitudinal autonomy evidence from Huang et al. (2025) and McCain et al. (2026). Dell’Acqua et al. (2025)’s field experiment with 776 Procter & Gamble professionals, together with longitudinal and organisational studies of Copilot rollouts (Stray et al., 2025) and AI-teamwork trajectories (Xiao et al., 2025), report shifts in human-AI work dynamics as collaboration accumulates. Wang et al. (2023) illustrates an embodied agent that accumulates a skill library across tasks. Mollick (2024) frames the human-AI working relationship as co-intelligence.

同样的持久性问题也出现在人类身上。第 12.6 节已经引用了 Huang 等人的纵向自治证据。 （2025）和麦凯恩等人。 （2026）。戴尔阿夸等人。 (2025) 对 776 名宝洁专业人员进行的现场实验，以及对 Copilot 部署的纵向和组织研究 (Stray 等人，2025) 以及人工智能团队合作轨迹 (Xiao 等人，2025)，报告了随着协作的积累，人类与人工智能工作动态的变化。王等人。 (2023) 展示了一个跨任务积累技能库的具体代理。 Mollick（2024）将人类与人工智能的工作关系定义为共同智能。

Whether a single substrate can carry both a user’s personal instruction hierarchy and a shared organisational context while preserving the file-based transparency of CLAUDE.md that Section 7 documents is an open architectural question. How session-scoped permissions interact with such a substrate, without reintroducing the resume-restoration concern that Section 9 closes as a deliberate safety choice, is a further open question.

单个基质是否可以承载用户的个人指令层次结构和共享的组织上下文，同时保留第 7 节文档中的 CLAUDE.md 基于文件的透明度，这是一个开放的架构问题。会话范围的权限如何与这样的底层交互，而不重新引入第 9 节作为有意的安全选择而关闭的恢复恢复问题，是一个进一步开放的问题。

Claude Code’s Projects beta is one product step in this direction: separate cloud sessions add to and draw

Claude Code 的 Projects beta 是朝这个方向迈出的一个产品步骤：单独的云会话添加和绘制

from a shared project memory that carries decisions across sessions (Anthropic, 2026l). The OpenClaw

memory subsystem (dreaming, daily notes, hybrid retrieval) and Hermes Agent’s WAL-mode SQLite session store with full-text search, both documented in Sections 10 and 10.2, provide two concrete reference points for how a persistent substrate can sit between a static instruction hierarchy and a single-session transcript.

内存子系统（做梦、日常笔记、混合检索）和 Hermes Agent 的带有全文搜索的 WAL 模式 SQLite 会话存储（均记录在第 10 节和 10.2 节中）为如何将持久性底层置于静态指令层次结构和单会话转录本之间提供了两个具体的参考点。

13.3

13.3

Harness Boundary Evolution: Where, When, What, and with Whom the Agent Acts

利用边界进化：代理在何处、何时、什么以及与谁一起行动

Section 12.6 cites Rajasekaran (2026)’s argument that better models move the space of useful harness combinations rather than eliminating it. Whether that movement will be most pronounced in where the harness runs, when it acts, what it acts on, or with whom it coordinates is not resolved by the source-level analysis in Sections 3 to 9. Each of the four has an active research literature that the paper touches only in passing.

第 12.6 节引用了 Rajasekaran（2026）的论点，即更好的模型会移动有用的harness组合的空间，而不是消除它。第 3 节到第 9 节中的源代码分析并不能解决这种运动是否在harness运行的位置、作用时间、作用对象或与谁协调方面最为明显。这四个部分中的每一个都有活跃的研究文献，本文只是顺便提及。

Where.

在哪里。

Martin et al. (2026)’s Managed Agents design virtualizes session, harness, and sandbox into inde- pendently replaceable interfaces. This extends the virtual-memory analogy that Packer et al. (2023) applies to context-window management and that Karpathy (2023) popularizes more broadly. Khattab et al. (2023) treats the harness itself as a compile target.

马丁等人。 (2026) 的托管代理设计将会话、harness和沙箱虚拟化为独立的可替换接口。这扩展了 Packer 等人的虚拟内存类比。 (2023) 适用于上下文窗口管理，而 Karpathy (2023) 则更广泛地普及。哈塔布等人。 (2023) 将harness本身视为编译目标。

When.

什么时候。

Section 12.6 already introduces KAIROS as a feature-gated illustration, motivated by the +12% to +18% task-pass gain that Chen et al. (2025) report and the sharp preference penalty (47% vs. 80% to 90%) restricted to the high-frequency Persistent Suggest variant. Claude Code routines use a simpler trigger:

第 12.6 节已经介绍了 KAIROS 作为特征门控说明，其动机是 Chen 等人获得了 +12% 到 +18% 的任务传递增益。 (2025) 报告和尖锐偏好惩罚（47% vs. 80% 到 90%）仅限于高频持久建议变体。 Claude Code 例程使用更简单的触发器：

they start a new cloud session when a configured schedule, API call, or GitHub event fires (Anthropic, 2026m), whereas KAIROS wakes a background agent and lets it decide at each tick whether to act. Liu et al.

当配置的计划、API 调用或 GitHub 事件触发时，它们会启动一个新的云会话（Anthropic，2026m），而 KAIROS 会唤醒后台代理并让它在每次更新时决定是否采取行动。刘等人。

(2025), Pu et al. (2025), and Lee et al. (2025) extend the proactivity design space across programming and ambient-interface settings. Pasternak et al. (2025) and Sun et al. (2025) introduce benchmarks and training regimes aimed at sharpening it, and Deng et al. (2025) surveys the broader literature.

（2025），Pu 等人。 （2025）和李等人。 (2025) 将主动设计空间扩展到编程和环境界面设置。帕斯捷尔纳克等人。 （2025）和孙等人。 (2025) 引入了旨在强化它的基准和培训制度，Deng 等人。 （2025）调查了更广泛的文献。

What.

什么。

Vision-language-action work extends the harness beyond textual tool returns. Brohan et al. (2023) and Black et al. (2024) train VLA policies that execute physical actions, and Ahn et al. (2022) grounds plans in robot affordances. Industry systems such as Figure AI (2025) and Bjorck et al. (2025) push similar ideas into humanoid control. These systems face the reversibility-weighted risk principle (Table 1) at a cost asymmetry that the principle names but does not quantify for non-textual actions.

视觉-语言-行动工作将harness扩展到文本工具返回之外。布罗汉等人。 （2023）和布莱克等人。 (2024) 训练执行物理动作的 VLA 策略，Ahn 等人。 （2022）制定了机器人可供性的计划。 Figure AI (2025) 和 Bjorck 等行业系统。 （2025）将类似的想法推向人形控制。这些系统面临着可逆性加权风险原则（表 1），其成本不对称性被该原则命名，但并未量化非文本操作。

40

40

With whom.

和谁。

Role-differentiated multi-agent systems (Hong et al. (2023), Li et al. (2023), Chen et al. (2023), Qian et al. (2024)) compose agents with distinct responsibilities. Multi-agent debate (Du et al., 2024; Liang et al., 2024) and graph-structured workflows (Zhuge et al., 2024) explore alternatives to the parent/subagent pattern of Section 8. Guo et al. (2024) surveys this space.

角色区分的多智能体系统（Hong et al. (2023)、Li et al. (2023)、Chen et al. (2023)、Qian et al. (2024)）由具有不同职责的智能体组成。多智能体辩论（Du et al., 2024；Liang et al., 2024）和图结构工作流程（Zhuge et al., 2024）探索了第 8 节的父/子智能体模式的替代方案。 （2024）调查了这个空间。

Whether a single harness architecture can span all four extensions, or whether the “harness combinations” Rajasekaran (2026) describes will fragment into specialised stacks, is an open design question. The when- extension directly continues the Capability-versus-Adaptability tension in Table 6. The with-whom-extension partially maps onto Capability-versus-Reliability but raises cross-agent consistency concerns that Table 6 does not itself cover. The where- and what-extensions raise further questions the paper’s current subsystem boundaries do not cover: which governance obligations attach when harness components become hosted services (Section 13.5), and how reversibility-weighted risk (Table 1) scales to physical rather than textual effects. How these extensions compose across axes, rather than within any one, is not something the paper’s single-subsystem analyses can resolve.

单个harness架构是否可以跨越所有四个扩展，或者 Rajasekaran（2026）描述的“harness组合”是否会分裂成专门的堆栈，是一个开放的设计问题。 When-扩展直接延续了表 6 中的能力与适应性张力。with-whom-扩展部分映射到能力与可靠性，但引起了表 6 本身未涵盖的跨代理一致性问题。何处扩展和什么扩展提出了本文当前子系统边界未涵盖的进一步问题：当harness组件成为托管服务时附加哪些治理义务（第 13.5 节），以及可逆性加权风险（表 1）如何扩展到物理效果而不是文本效果。这些扩展如何跨轴而不是在任何轴内组成，并不是本文的单子系统分析可以解决的问题。

13.4

13.4

Horizon Scaling: From Session to Scientific Program

地平线扩展：从会议到科学计划

Section 2.1 defines Reliable Execution as spanning “both single-turn correctness and long-horizon dependability.” How the architecture documented in Sections 3, 4 and 7 to 9 continues to support long-horizon dependability as autonomous work extends beyond a single session is an open question. Its primary units are the turn, the session, and the sub-agent. A growing literature targets this regime. Lu et al. (2024) present an end-to-end autonomous research pipeline producing draft manuscripts. Beel et al. (2025) provide an independent SIGIR Forum evaluation of that pipeline, characterising what “autonomous research” currently delivers and where it falls short. Gottweis et al. (2025) develop a multi-agent hypothesis-generation system that runs across days rather than turns, and Novikov et al. (2025) pursue algorithmic discovery over timescales that previously took human experts weeks. Kwa et al.’s METR study measures the task duration at which frontier agents succeed with fixed reliability, giving an empirical frame for this scaling question.

第 2.1 节将可靠执行定义为涵盖“单轮正确性和长期可靠性”。当自主工作扩展到单个会话之外时，第 3、4 和 7 至 9 节中记录的架构如何继续支持长期可靠性是一个悬而未决的问题。它的主要单位是回合、会话和subagent。越来越多的文献针对这一政权。卢等人。 （2024）提出了一个端到端的自主研究流程，生成手稿草稿。贝尔等人。 (2025) 提供了对该管道的独立 SIGIR 论坛评估，描述了“自主研究”目前提供的内容以及不足之处。戈特维斯等人。 (2025) 开发了一种多代理假设生成系统，该系统可以跨天运行，而不是轮流运行，Novikov 等人。 （2025）在过去需要人类专家几周时间的时间尺度上追求算法发现。 Kwa 等人的 METR 研究测量了前沿智能体以固定可靠性成功的任务持续时间，为这个规模问题提供了一个经验框架。

The Quantitative Goal Persistence benchmark names a narrower failure mode: an agent can make reasonable local tool calls and still stop before a verifier confirms enough valid work units (Cai et al., 2026). For long-horizon coding agents, progress needs an external notion of done, not only a fluent final message.

定量目标持久性基准命名了一种更窄的故障模式：代理可以进行合理的本地工具调用，并且仍然在验证者确认足够的有效工作单元之前停止（Cai 等人，2026）。对于长期coding agent来说，进展需要一个外部的完成概念，而不仅仅是流畅的最终消息。

SWE Refactor Bench applies the same idea to repository migration: it checks migration requirements separately

SWE Refactor Bench 将相同的想法应用于存储库迁移：它单独检查迁移需求

from fixed behavior tests, so an incomplete migration and a migration that breaks required behavior appear

as different failures (Hong et al., 2026).

作为不同的失败（Hong et al., 2026）。

Against the paper’s analysis, long-horizon deployment tests whether the context-management pipeline of Section 7, the last-assistant-text return policy of Section 8, and the append-only persistence of Section 9 remain sufficient when sessions compose into multi-session programs. Section 12.4 already frames this as “a directly measurable empirical question” that source-level analysis cannot resolve. Horizon scaling restates that question at the scale of weeks: whether the harness layer alone closes the gap, whether a cross-session memory substrate (Section 13.2) is required, or whether horizon-scale work demands coordination primitives beyond session, sub-agent, and memory, is not something the paper’s session-scoped analyses can settle.

根据本文的分析，长期部署测试了当会话组成多会话程序时，第 7 节的上下文管理管道、第 8 节的最后辅助文本返回策略以及第 9 节的仅附加持久性是否仍然足够。第 12.4 节已经将此描述为“一个可直接测量的经验问题”，源级分析无法解决。水平扩展在几周的尺度上重申了这个问题：harness层是否能够单独缩小差距，是否需要跨会话内存基底（第 13.2 节），或者水平规模的工作是否需要会话、subagent和内存之外的协调原语，这些都不是本文的会话范围分析可以解决的问题。

(Tier A) A development subsequent to the v2.1.88 snapshot analyzed here makes this question concrete.

（A 层）此处分析的 v2.1.88 快照之后的开发使这个问题变得具体。

Claude Code v2.1.154 introduced dynamic workflows, in which the model writes a JavaScript orchestration script that a background runtime executes, fanning out to many subagents (bounded at up to a thousand per run) while intermediate results live in script variables outside the model’s context window rather than passing through it (Anthropic, 2026n). Released alongside Claude Opus 4.8 (Anthropic, 2026k), this is precisely a coordination primitive beyond the session, sub-agent, and memory units analyzed here: it relocates the orchestration logic itself out of the conversation, extending the context as scarce resource and isolated subagent boundaries principles (Table 1) from individual subagents to the orchestration layer. Whether orchestration-as-code becomes the dominant long-horizon primitive, and how its token cost and reduced human mid-run oversight trade against its reliability gains, is an open question the session-scoped analysis here cannot settle.

Claude Code v2.1.154 引入了动态工作流程，其中模型编写一个由后台运行时执行的 JavaScript 编排脚本，分散到许多subagent（每次运行最多一千个），而中间结果位于模型上下文窗口外部的脚本变量中，而不是通过它传递（Anthropic，2026n）。与 Claude Opus 4.8（Anthropic，2026k）一起发布，这正是超出此处分析的会话、subagent和内存单元的协调原语：它将编排逻辑本身从对话中重新定位，将上下文作为稀缺资源和隔离的subagent边界原则（表 1）从单个subagent扩展到编排层。编排即代码是否成为占主导地位的长期原语，以及其代币成本和减少的人工中期监督交易如何与其可靠性收益相抵触，是一个会话范围分析无法解决的悬而未决的问题。

41

41

13.5

13.5

Governance and Oversight at Scale

大规模治理和监督

Emerging AI regulation adds an external constraint on the architectures that implement the Authority hierarchy of Anthropic, operators, and users documented in Section 2.1. Which logging, transparency, and human-oversight affordances coding-agent architectures should expose under that external constraint remains an open design question. The European Commission’s GPAI Code of Practice (European Commission, 2025a) and implementation guidelines (European Commission, 2025b) illustrate how general-purpose AI governance is moving toward more explicit expectations for documentation, risk management, transparency, and oversight. The MIT AI Agent Index (Staufer et al., 2026) and the International AI Safety Report (Bengio et al., 2026), already cited in Section 12.6, motivate the disclosure and oversight side of this constraint. The Bartz v. Anthropic ruling (bar, 2025) adds an input-side constraint on training-data sourcing, distinct from the output-side copyright questions about AI-generated code that emerging cases address separately. An OECD report on AI governance frameworks (OECD, 2025) and an early analysis of compliance obligations for agent providers by Nannini et al. (2026) sketch what regulator-facing interfaces might look like without prescribing specifics.

新兴的人工智能监管为实现第 2.1 节中记录的人类、运营商和用户的权威层次结构的架构添加了外部约束。在外部约束下，coding agent架构应该公开哪些日志记录、透明度和人工监督功能仍然是一个悬而未决的设计问题。欧盟委员会的 GPAI 实践准则（欧盟委员会，2025a）和实施指南（欧盟委员会，2025b）说明了通用人工智能治理如何朝着对文档、风险管理、透明度和监督更明确的期望迈进。麻省理工学院人工智能代理指数（Staufer 等人，2026 年）和国际人工智能安全报告（Bengio 等人，2026 年）（已在第 12.6 节中引用）推动了这一限制的披露和监督。 Bartz v. Anthropic 裁决（bar，2025）增加了对训练数据来源的输入侧限制，这与新兴案件单独解决的有关人工智能生成代码的输出侧版权问题不同。经合组织关于人工智能治理框架的报告（经合组织，2025）以及 Nannini 等人对代理提供商合规义务的早期分析。 (2026) 勾勒出面向监管机构的接口可能是什么样子，但没有规定具体细节。

(Tier A) Regulation can also act on a model’s availability, not only on what an architecture must disclose.

（A 层）监管还可以对模型的可用性起作用，而不仅仅是架构必须披露的内容。

In June 2026 a US export-control order barred foreign nationals from Anthropic’s Fable 5 and Mythos 5 models, and within days of launch Anthropic disabled both models for all users worldwide (Anthropic, 2026e).

2026 年 6 月，美国出口管制令禁止外国人使用 Anthropic 的《神鬼寓言 5》和《神话 5》模型，并且在发布后几天内，Anthropic 就对全球所有用户禁用了这两种模型（Anthropic，2026e）。

Anthropic reported that the controls were lifted on June 30; Fable 5 returned for users globally on July 1, while Mythos 5 access was restored only for a set of US organizations (Anthropic, 2026f). Government policy of this kind can directly determine when, and to whom, such a model is available.

Anthropic 报道称，控制已于 6 月 30 日解除； 《神鬼寓言 5》于 7 月 1 日重新向全球用户开放，而《神话 5》仅针对一组美国组织恢复了访问权限（Anthropic，2026f）。此类政府政策可以直接决定何时以及向谁提供这种模式。

Agentic safety benchmarks also move the safety object from text to environment state. Boiling the Frog evaluates whether multi-turn workspace edits become unsafe under incremental attacks (Bisconti et al., 2026).

代理安全基准还将安全对象从文本状态转移到环境状态。煮青蛙评估多轮工作区编辑在增量攻击下是否变得不安全（Bisconti 等人，2026）。

This matters for coding agents because the risky object may be the modified repository, not the final answer text.

这对于coding agent很重要，因为有风险的对象可能是修改后的存储库，而不是最终的答案文本。

Read against the permission pipeline analyzed in Section 5, two properties of the current architecture are open under this constraint. First, the deny-first evaluation the paper documents is internally auditable through session transcripts (Section 9), and Claude Code can export tool permission decision events through OpenTelemetry (Anthropic, 2026j). How these records can support external audits under emerging frameworks such as the GPAI Code of Practice (European Commission, 2025a) remains an open question. Second, whether the values-over-rules principle, which the paper pairs with deterministic guardrails, admits the kind of explicit rule articulation that compliance review may call for is a further open question. Both properties lie within the harness rather than the model, which is where future architectures may need to expose new interfaces.

对照第 5 节中分析的权限管道，当前架构的两个属性在此约束下是开放的。首先，论文文档中的拒绝优先评估可通过会话记录进行内部审核（第 9 节），并且 Claude Code 可以通过 OpenTelemetry 导出工具权限决策事件（Anthropic，2026j）。这些记录如何支持 GPAI 实践守则（欧盟委员会，2025a）等新兴框架下的外部审计仍然是一个悬而未决的问题。其次，本文与确定性护栏相结合的价值高于规则的原则是否承认合规审查可能需要的那种明确的规则表述，这是一个进一步悬而未决的问题。这两个属性都位于harness内，而不是模型内，这是未来架构可能需要公开新接口的地方。

13.6

13.6

Long-term Developer Capability

长期开发能力

Section 2.4 introduces long-term developer capability as a cross-cutting question rather than a co-equal design value. Sections 12.2 and 12.4 extend the question with external evidence, including perceived-versus- measured productivity, comprehension loss, complexity accrual, technical-debt persistence, neural-connectivity persistence, and early-career hiring decline. Practitioners say the same: reflecting on his own move to agent-driven coding, Karpathy (2026) warns that “you can outsource your thinking, but you can’t outsource your understanding.” Section 14 then pivots: “Future systems could treat that sustainability gap as a first-class design problem, not a downstream evaluation metric.” Whether that pivot is possible, and what architectural mechanisms a first-class treatment would require, is the last of the open questions this section records.

2.4 节介绍了长期开发人员能力作为一个横切问题，而不是一个同等的设计价值。第 12.2 和 12.4 节用外部证据扩展了这个问题，包括感知与测量的生产力、理解损失、复杂性应计、技术债务持续性、神经连接持续性和早期职业招聘下降。从业者也这么说：在反思自己转向代理驱动编码时，Karpathy（2026）警告说，“你可以外包你的思维，但你不能外包你的理解。”第 14 节接着​​提出：“未来的系统可以将可持续性差距视为一流的设计问题，而不是下游评估指标。”这个支点是否可能，以及一流的处理需要什么架构机制，是本节记录的最后一个开放问题。

This also links to process supervision. Human-LLM collaborative planning work argues that users need process-level control, not only outcome-level review, and organizes steering by mode, scope, and edit level (He et al., 2026). For coding agents, the analogous question is what a user can inspect and change while the work is still unfolding.

这也与过程监督有关。人类-法学硕士协作规划工作认为，用户需要过程级控制，而不仅仅是结果级审查，并按模式、范围和编辑级别组织指导（He et al., 2026）。对于coding agent来说，类似的问题是用户在工作仍在进行时可以检查和更改什么。

Two sub-questions separate the measurement gap from the design gap. First, whether the empirical claims that motivate the question are measurable at session granularity. The existing citations operate at session to multi-month scales (Becker et al. (2025)’s 16-developer RCT, Shen and Tamkin (2026)’s comprehension-test comparison, Kosmyna et al. (2025)’s EEG study, He et al. (2025)’s 807-repository causal analysis, Liu et al.

两个子问题将测量差距与设计差距分开。首先，引发该问题的经验主张是否可以在会话粒度上进行衡量。现有的引文在会话至数月的范围内进行（Becker 等人（2025）的 16 个开发者随机对照试验、Shen 和 Tamkin（2026）的理解测试比较、Kosmyna 等人（2025）的脑电图研究、He 等人（2025）的 807 存储库因果分析、Liu 等人（2025）的 807 存储库因果分析。

(2026)’s 304,000-commit audit, Rak (2025)’s hiring series), but the harness documented in Sections 3, 4 42 and 7 exposes no per-session signal for comprehension or convention drift. Related work on programmer interaction modes (Barke et al., 2023) and AI-induced code-security regressions (Perry et al., 2023) sketches session-granularity measurement, and Aiersilan (2026) proposes a protocol for session-level cognitive-offloading probes. Second, whether architecture can respond to such measurements once they exist (an analogue of the generator-evaluator separation (Rajasekaran, 2026) applied to the human loop, comprehension-preserving surfaces, or mechanisms not yet named) is the design-gap question Section 14 poses. The paper takes no position on which mechanism class is appropriate. It also does not settle whether the harness documented here is the right locus for that action, rather than the IDE, the organisation, or the human development loop.

(2026) 的 304,000 次提交审计、Rak (2025) 的招聘系列)，但第 3、4、42 和 7 节中记录的harness没有暴露每个会话的理解或约定漂移信号。关于程序员交互模式（Barke et al., 2023）和人工智能引发的代码安全回归（Perry et al., 2023）的相关工作概述了会话粒度测量，而 Aiersilan（2026）提出了一种用于会话级认知卸载探测的协议。其次，一旦这些测量存在，架构是否能够对其做出响应（类似于生成器-评估器分离（Rajasekaran，2026）应用于人类循环、理解保留表面或尚未命名的机制）是第 14 节提出的设计差距问题。本文对于哪种机制类别合适没有采取任何立场。它还不能解决此处记录的harness（而不是 IDE、组织或人类开发循环）是否是该操作的正确位置。

The related work surveyed in Section 11 and the sustainability pivot of Section 14 mark where this paper leaves the question.

第 11 节中调查的相关工作和第 14 节中的可持续发展支点标志着本文留下的问题。

14

14

Conclusion

结论

This paper shows that production coding agents can be understood as answers to a recurring set of design questions: where reasoning sits relative to the harness, how execution, safety, extensibility, context, delegation, and persistence are organized, and which trade-offs those choices encode. Claude Code occupies a clear design point within that space. It gives the model broad local autonomy while surrounding it with a dense deterministic harness for permissioning, tool routing, context compaction, extensibility, and session recovery.

本文表明，生产coding agent可以被理解为一组重复出现的设计问题的答案：推理相对于harness的位置，如何组织执行、安全性、可扩展性、上下文、委托和持久性，以及这些选择编码的权衡。Claude·科德（Claude Code）在该空间中占据了清晰的设计点。它为模型提供了广泛的本地自治权，同时围绕它提供了用于许可、工具路由、上下文压缩、可扩展性和会话恢复的密集确定性harness。

Read through the five values and thirteen design principles identified in Section 2, these choices are coherent rather than ad hoc: the system consistently prioritizes human decision authority, safety, reliable execution, capability amplification, and contextual adaptability.

仔细阅读第 2 节中确定的五个价值观和十三个设计原则，这些选择是连贯的而不是临时的：系统始终优先考虑人类决策权、安全性、可靠的执行、能力放大和上下文适应性。

The OpenClaw and Hermes Agent comparisons show that the same design questions recur in different agent systems but produce different answers. Where Claude Code invests in per-action safety classification and graduated context compression within a CLI harness, OpenClaw invests in perimeter-level access control and structured long-term memory within a multi-channel gateway, and Hermes Agent renders per-action approvals across many surfaces, from one process, with pluggable memory and model backends. The three systems compose through ACP at multiple positions: OpenClaw can host Claude Code as an external harness via ACP, and Hermes Agent sits on both sides of the host/guest split. For agent builders, the most consequential open question is therefore not how to add more autonomy, but how to preserve human capability over time.

OpenClaw 和 Hermes Agent 的比较表明，相同的设计问题在不同的代理系统中重复出现，但产生不同的答案。 Claude Code 投资于 CLI harness内的每操作安全分类和分级上下文压缩，OpenClaw 投资于多通道网关内的外围级别访问控制和结构化长期内存，而 Hermes Agent 通过可插拔内存和模型后端从一个进程跨多个表面呈现每操作批准。这三个系统通过 ACP 在多个位置组成：OpenClaw 可以通过 ACP 托管 Claude Code 作为外部harness，而 Hermes Agent 则位于主机/客户分割的两侧。因此，对于代理构建者来说，最重要的开放问题不是如何增加更多自主权，而是如何随着时间的推移保持人类能力。

Taken together, the long-term capability question introduced in Section 2.4, the analysis in Section 12, and the open questions surveyed in Section 13 show that the architecture provides limited mechanisms that explicitly preserve long-term human understanding, codebase coherence, or the developer pipeline. Future systems could treat that sustainability gap as a first-class design problem, not a downstream evaluation metric.

总而言之，第 2.4 节中介绍的长期能力问题、第 12 节中的分析以及第 13 节中调查的开放式问题表明，该架构提供了有限的机制，可以明确保留长期的人类理解、代码库一致性或开发人员管道。未来的系统可以将可持续性差距视为一流的设计问题，而不是下游评估指标。

学习者视角

关键收获

  1. Agent 工程 = harness 工程。 模型提供推理，harness 提供权限、上下文、工具、记忆和恢复。真正可复用的能力在后者。
  2. 安全设计要从“审批”转向“边界”。 用户会习惯性批准，所以沙箱、分类器、deny-first 规则比无限弹窗更可靠。
  3. 上下文管理是系统能力，不是 prompt 技巧。 压缩、裁剪、折叠、摘要要组合使用。
  4. Subagent 的第一价值是隔离。 不是“多线程”，而是“把噪声挡在主上下文之外”。
  5. 长期能力是设计问题。 如果课程只教你让 agent 更快，不教你如何保持理解，那只是短期提效。

放回课程里看

这篇论文是 Week 1 的“总架构图”。读完它，你再看后面的 Context Engineering、Skills、MCP、Code Review，都会更容易定位：每个主题对应 harness 的哪一个子系统。

一个反直觉点

论文发现 Claude Code 的核心 loop 非常简单，大部分代码都在“围绕 loop 的系统”里。这解释了一个常见误区：** demo 只需要 loop，产品需要 harness。**

拓展

思考题

  1. 如果让你给一个“数据分析 agent”设计权限系统，你会沿用 Claude Code 的 7 种模式，还是重新设计？为什么？
  2. 五层压缩管线里，哪一层最可能造成“静默错误”？你会如何观测？
  3. Subagent 的“只返回最终消息”策略，什么时候会成为瓶颈？
  4. 论文提出的“长期开发者能力”问题，在你自己的学习或工作中有什么具体表现？

练习

画一张你自己项目的 agent harness 图，标注：

  • 核心 loop；
  • 工具；
  • 权限/安全边界；
  • 上下文来源；
  • 压缩策略；
  • 持久化；
  • 人类监督点。

然后圈出你最薄弱的一层，写一句可执行的改进。
